# Homilies I–III (Omelie I-III)

**Author**: Sévère Ibn-al-Moqaffa`  
**Series**: PO Tome 56  
**Columns**: pp. 164–338  

[p. 164] {#po.56.p164}

### PROLOGUE
መቅድም ።
Invocation
'በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አም ላክ ። ንዌጥን' ፡ ብረድኤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፤ ንጽሕፍ ፣ ዐሠርቱ ፡ ወክልኤቱ" ፡ ድርሳናተ" ፡ ጣእንተ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወልዕልናሃ ፡ ወዕበያ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ባዕዳን ፡ ሃይማኖታት ።" ዘደ ረሰ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ' ፡ ሳዊሮስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆስ ፡ ዘገረ ፡ እስሙና ይን ፤ እም ፡ ብሔረ ፣ ግብጽ ። በረከቱ" ፣ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ'' ፡ ለዓለም ፡
1 In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one (only) God. We begin, with the help of God, to him be glory! We write the twelve homilies on the glory of the right Christian faith and on its excellence and greatness compared to other faiths, which the holy father Abba Sawiros, bishop of the city of Ashmunain from the country of Egypt, composed. May his blessing be with us for the ages of ages, amen!

### List of contents of the Kabra Haymanot
ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ''ድርሳን ፡ ቀዳማይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌሁ'፤ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወተዋሕዶቶሙ ። ድርሳን ፡ ዳግማይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ከሚተ'' ፡ ትስብ እቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወስቅለቱ ። ድርሳን ፡ ሣልስ" ፡ ትርጓሜ ፣ ቃላት ፡ እምነ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያጤይቅ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ርትዕት ። ድርሳን ፡ ራብዕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትር ጓሜ ፡ ፍሥሕ ፡ ወበግፅ ፡ ፣ወከዊኖ ፡ ለኅብስት!' ፡ ወወይን ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፣ ለእግዚእነ” ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ድርሳን ፡ ኃምስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸብእ ፡ ዘይጸብእ ዎሙ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ይመውእዎሙ ። ድርሳን ፣ ሳድስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትርጓሜ፤' ፡ ክብሩ ፡ ለዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ። ድርሳን ፡ ሳብዕ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ከሚተ ፡ ምክንያት ፡ ጸመ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ረቡዕ ፡ ወዐርብ ። ድርሳን ፡ ሳምን ፡ በእንተ" ፡ ጾም ፡ ምንት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ይደሉ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ። ድርሳን ፡ ታስፅ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ከሚተ ፡ መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ "ሞት ፡ ዘሞያዖ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዲበ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወያበጥል” ፡ ለዘይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር”! ፡ ሥሩፅ ፡ ዘያመው ቶሙ” ፣ ለሰብእ ። ድርሳን ፣ ዓሥር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፳ንዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርት ዕት ፡ ያፅቆባዊት ፡ ወክብራ ፡ ወይከሥት ፡፣ ኩሎ ፡ የሃይማኖተ ፡ ውሉ ጣተ” ። ድርሳን ፣ ዐሠርቱ ፡ ወአሐዱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወማርያም ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ፣ወትርጓሜ ፡ መዝሙር ፡ ፻ቋወ፳ ፡ ወመዝ ሙር ፡ ፻ወ፻ ፡ ወራእይ ፡ ራብዕ ፡ ዘዳንኤል” ፡ ወስብሐተ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡
2 First homily: on the explanation of the three hypostases of God and their union. Second homily: on the manifestation of the incarnation of the Son of God and on his crucifixion. Third homily: interpretation of terms from the Torah and from Joshua son of Nun to demonstrate the glory of the right Christian faith. Fourth homily: on the interpretation of the Passover and the Lamb, and on the bread and wine that become the flesh and blood of our Lord Christ. Fifth homily: on the battle that the demons fight against the faithful and how they defeat them. Sixth homily: on the interpretation of the glory of Sunday. Seventh homily: on the manifestation of the reason for the two-day fast, of Wednesday and Friday. Eighth homily: on what fasting is and how it must be. Ninth homily: on the manifestation of who is the death that God defeated on the cross and makes vain the one who said that it is an angel appointed by God who causes people to die. Tenth homily: on the firmness of the right Jacobite faith and on its glory and exposes all the altered faiths. Eleventh homily: on the interpretation of the Praise of Moses and of Miriam his sister and on the interpretation of Psalm 135, of Psalm 150, the Fourth Vision of


[p. 166] {#po.56.p166}
ደቂቅ ። ድርሳን ፡ ፲ ወ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኑዛዜ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወትግፅሥቶሙ ፡ የላዕለ ፡ ሐዘን' ፡ ወምንዳቤ ፡ በሰላመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሜን ።
Daniel and the Praise of the Three Youths. Twelfth homily: on the consolation of Christians and on their patience in pain and affliction for the peace of God, amen.

### HOMILY
ድርሳን ፡፣ ቀዳማይ ።
| Programmatic dialogue between the master and his disciple
ድርሳን ፡ ቀዳማይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡፣ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፣ ወተዋሕዶቶሙ ። ኦእኅው ፡ ፍቁር' ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክር ስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ያብርህ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልብከ ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትለቡ ፣ ምሥጢረ ፡፣ መለኮት" ። ትቤለኒ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መልእክትከ ፡ ዘፈኖከ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ በግፅዝ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ የትሥልስተ ፡ አካላቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወተዋሕዶቱ' ፡ ወትቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኢተአምር ፡ እፎ" ፡ ይትከ ሀል ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢይከውኑ ፡ ሠለስቱ"' ፡ አሐደ ። ወዘሰአልከኒ'' ፡ የከመ ፣ እክሥት" ፡ ለከ ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ዝክተ ። ወአጤይቅ"' ፡ ትስብእቶ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግ
3 First homily: on the explanation of the three hypostases of God and their union. O beloved brother, may the Lord Jesus Christ, son of the living God (Mt 16:16), illuminate the eyes of your heart with the light of the Holy Spirit Paraclete so that you may understand the mystery of the divinity. You tell me in your letter that you sent me that you are in great perplexity concerning the triplicity of the Persons of God and concerning his unity and you said that you do not know how it is possible that the Three are One. What you asked me to illustrate for you is a certainty and I will demonstrate the incarnation of the Son of God and his crucifixion and what the reason for this is. Instruction to a dual audience
ዚአብሔር ፡ ወስቅለቶ ፡ ወምንት ፡ ምክንያተ ፣ዝክቱ"' ። ወናሁ ፡ ተሰጠውኩከ ፡ ዘሰአልከኒ'!' ፡ ወከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ በውስተ" ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ "”ለስቱ ፡ አካላቲሁ ፡ ወተዋህዶቶ'' ፡ በክ ልኤ ፡ ፍካርያት” ። አሐዱ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ለኅዳጣነ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ወአእምሮ” ፡ ወዳ ግም ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ለለባውያን ፣ ወመምህራን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈወስ ፡ ዘአንበቦ ፡ አው ፡ ዘአንበቡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እምክልኤሆሙ ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ” ፡ ሕዳጣነ ፡ ልቡኖ ፡፣ ወአእምሮ ፡ ወለባውያን ፡ ወመምህራን ።
4 I have therefore answered you regarding what you asked me and in this book I have illustrated for you with frankness, the Three Persons and their Unity in two explanations. One explanation is for those of scarce understanding and knowledge. The second explanation is for the intelligent and for the masters so that he who reads or he to whom they read may be healed from both (sides), that is, those of scarce understanding and knowledge and the intelligent and the masters.

### Cause of the alienation of the Copts from the faith in the Son of God
፣ወእዌጥን ፡ እከሥት ፡ ለከ"! ፡ ለሕዳጣነ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ቀዳሚ” ፡ ወአቤ ፣ እስመ ፡ ምክንያት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኅቡእ ፡ ዝውእቱ?" ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ እምነ ፡ ክርስ ቲያን ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተደምሮቶሙ”" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ እስመ ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ እምኔሆሙ” ፣ ልሳኖሙ ፡ ዘቅብጢ ፡ ዘእምኔሁ” ፡ የአምሩ ፣ ጽድቀ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ኢይሰምዑ” ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ማእከሌ
5 And I begin to explain first to those of scarce intelligence and I say that the reason why this mystery is hidden from Christians in these days is because of their mixing with the Muslims, since their language, Coptic, through which they knew the truthfulness of their faith, was lost to them and they did not hear (anymore) in their midst, except little, the mention of the Triune, nor the
ሆጮ፤ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ ወኢወልደ! ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተዘክር" ፡ ማእከሌ ሆሙ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አላ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ዘይሰምዑ ፡ እምሃ ፡ ተንባላት ፣ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ገጽ" ። ወዘተርፈሰ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘይ ብሉ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ ለመዱ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ክርስቲያን' ፡ ወልህቁ" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ "እስከ ፡ ኮኑ" ፡ የዐጽቦሙ ፡ ዝክረ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወኢያአ ምሩ ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ወፍካሬሁ' ።
mention in their midst of the Son of God, except like this. But what they heard from the Muslims was more, that God is one face, and beyond this the Muslims said other things still to which the Christians became accustomed and grew in it to such a point that the memory of the Son of God became difficult for them before them and they did not know (anymore) its explanation nor its meaning.

### Formation for the poorly instructed


[p. 168] {#po.56.p168}
ወብዙኃን ፡ እምክርስቲያን'! ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሰምዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወወልደ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ይቴልዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጥንቱ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ኮነ ፡ በከመ! ፡ ይቴጌልዩ ፡ ተንባላት ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንቤ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወኢያአምሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር' ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እም ቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡፣ ዓለም ። ወእምቅድመ" ፣ አዳም'' ፡ ዘእማርያም" ፡ እምዘርኡ ፡ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ፤' ፡ ትውልዳት ። እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ኢሀሎ ፡ ግሙራ ፡፣ ዘእንበሌሁ ፡ ወእስመ" ፣ ውእቱ" ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወቃሉ ፣ ዘህልው ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወትረ ፣ ወኢይትፈለጥ” ፣ እምኔሁ ። ወአንሰ"! ፡ እከ
6 Many of the Christians when they hear that He is the Son of God and the Son of the Virgin Mary think that his origin was from the Virgin Mary as the Muslims think regarding us who maintain this. They do not know that He exists eternally with God from whom He was born before the world was created and before Adam, of whom Mary is a descendant, and before all generations. Because God never existed without Him, because He is his Son and his Word existing with him always and does not separate from him. Of this, to those of scarce understanding, I will express the explanation in a few words.

### Recourse to metaphor for a discourse on the ineffable God
ሥት ፡፣ ለእሉ ፡ ኅዳጣነ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ትርጓሜ” ፡ ዝንቱ” ፡ በነገር ፡ ንስቲት ። ወእወጥን” ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወእቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ብሔር ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ምስለ” ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፣ ወኢያስተማስልዎ ፡ በምንትኒ ፡ አላ ፡ አልባቢነሰ ፡ ድኩማት ፡ ወያጽህቀነ”" ፡ ለምሳሌ ፡ ወመክፈልት"' ፡ ናስ ተማስሎ ፡ ቦሙ ፣፡ ከመ ፡ ናእምሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ወእቱ ፡ ዝክተ ፡ ለር እሱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አስተማሰለ ፡ ርእሶ” ፡ ለነ ፡ በአምሳላት” ፣ ብዙኃት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠ ይቀነ"' ፡ አእምሮቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አልባቢነ ፡ ድኩማት ።'?! እስመ” ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ይቤ ፡ በወንጌል" ፡ የቅዱስ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፣። ፣ብርሃነ ፡ ዓለም'' ። ወይቤ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ዳዊት ፣ በመዝሙር ፡ በብርሃንከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ንርአይ ፡ ብርሃነ" ። ወአስተርአዮ ፣፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ጳጦስ ፡ በአምሳለ” ፡ እሳት ። የወይቤ ፡ በፀአት ፡ እምኦሪት"' ፡ ከመ"! ፡ እሳት ፡ ኮነት"' ፡ ትነድድ ፡ በው ስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ሀሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይነብር ፡ ቦቱ ።
7 I begin before everything and I say that God has no likeness whatsoever and they do not represent him with anything, but our minds are weak and (this) pushes us to (resort to) likeness and with it we represent him in part, so that we may know Him, as He himself did this with himself when he represented himself for our benefit, through many likenesses to ensure his knowledge to our weak minds. Because he said in the holy gospel: “I am the light of the world” (Jn 8:12; 9:5) and David in the Psalm says to Him: “In your light, Lord, we see the light” (Ps 36:10). He revealed himself to Moses in the tree of the bush in the image of fire (Ex 3:2-4). He said in the Exodus of the Torah that the fire burned on the mountain in the place where God dwelt.

### Limit of the representation of God in metaphor
ዘንተ' ፡ አምሳላት ፡ አስተማሰለ' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያጠይቀነ ፣ አእምሮቶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አልባቢነ ፣ ድኩማት ። አላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይትሌዓል ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወአምሳል ፡ ወምሳሌ ፡ ወአእ ምሮ ። ወለእመ ፡ ጠየቅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰመየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ወአ ስተርአየ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ደለወነ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ኖስተማስሎ ፡ በብርሃን ፣ ከመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ አእምሮቱ ፡ውስተ ፡ አልባቢነ ፡ ድኩማት ፡ ወንክሥት" ፡ ሠለስተ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ወተዋሕዶቶሙ ፡ እም ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አምሳል ።
8 These likenesses with which God represented himself are to ensure his knowledge to our weak minds. As for Him, He transcends every work, likeness, analogy, and knowledge. And if we clarify that He called himself “light” and revealed himself through the likeness of light, (we too) must represent him with light so that the knowledge of Him may reach our weak minds and we illustrate his triple essence and their unity through these likenesses.

### Analogies to illustrate the mystery of God and of Christ


[p. 170] {#po.56.p170}
ወንቤ ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ስፉሕ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ዘመል ፅልት ፡ ወመትሕተ ፡ ዘመትሕት ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ወሰነ' ፡ ወኢመጠን ፡ ወኢያገ ምሮ ፣ መካን ፣ ወኢይትኃጣእ ፡፣ መካን ፡ ወክርስቶስኒ ፡ ወልዱ' ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘከማሁ ፡፣ ተወልደ ፡፣ እምኔሁ ፡ ይመልእ ፡፣ ኩሎ ፡፣ መካነ ፡ ወኢያገምሮ ፡ መካን ፡ ወኢይትኃጣእ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ መካን ፡ ስፉሕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ አቡሁ ። እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እምብርሃን" ፡ ልደት ፡ ዘሀለወት ፡ ትትሌዓል ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አልባበ ፡ ፍጡራን ፡ ወንሕነሰ ፡ አስተማሰልናሃ ፡ በብር ሃን ፡ እምብርሃን ። እስመ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ እምብርሃን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣
9 And we say that God is an extended light, (more) exalted than the exalted, and (more) low than the low. He has no boundary, nor measure; no place contains him and no place lacks him. Also Christ his Son is light like Him, was born from Him; he fills every place and no place contains him and no place lacks him, he is extended with God his Father. Because (every) son is like his father from whom he was born. As light is born from light, the birth that rises above the minds of creatures we have compared it to light from light. Because light is born from light without (carnal) conjunction, nor effort, nor pregnancy, nor diminution.

### Nicene Creed with comments
ተራክቦ ፡ ወኢድካም ፡ ወኢፅንስ ፡ ወኢሕጸት ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻፲ ወቿ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ጉባኡ ፡ ነአ ምን ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ እግዚእ'' ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እም ፡ አብ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ። ብር ሃን ፡ ዘእምብርሃን ፣ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን'' ፡ ዘእምአምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ። አጠ የቁነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እምአቡሁ ፡ እም ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ይትፈ ጠር'' ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እም ፡ በከመ'፤' ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እምብ ርሃን ። ወውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ በከመ ፣ አቡሁ ፡ አምሳክ ፡ ዘበአ ማን ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ እምባሕር ይሁ ፣ ወመለኮቱ ። ፣ወከማሁ ፡፣ ያፍ" ፡ ይወልድ ፡ ዖፈ ፡ ወኢይወልድ ፡ አርዌ ። ፣ወአርዌኒ ፡ ይወልድ ፡ አርዌ ።፡" ወኢይወልድ ፡ ዖፈ ። ወሰ ብእ" ፣ ይወልድ ፡፣ ሰብአ!' ፡ ወኢይወልድ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ወኢዖፈ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወልድ ፡ አምሳለ” ፡ አቡሁ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ እምባሕርይሁ ፡ ወጠ ባይዓቱ” ።
10 Thus said the 318 in the synod of the faith: “We believe in one Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the unique God, who was born from the Father before the creation of the world. Light from light, true God from true God”. They demonstrated to us that He was born from his Father before the world was created: without a mother, as light is born from light. He is true God as his Father is true God, because the Son is in the likeness of his Father in everything, from his nature and from his divinity. In the same way, the bird generates a bird and does not generate a beast, and also the beast generates a beast and does not generate a bird. And man generates a man and does not generate a beast, nor a bird, because every son is an image of his father (Gen 5:3), in everything he is from his substance and from his nature.

### Divinity of the Son
ወከማሁ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ።፤ ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻፲ ወቿ፳ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕሩይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ወኢተገብረ" ። ዝብሂል ፣ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢፈጠሮ ፡ አላ ፡ ወለዶ ፣ እምባሕርይሁ ፡ ወመለ ኮቱ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ሶበሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተገብረ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ዕሩየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ እስመ ፣ መኑሂ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ወኢይትከሀሎ ፡ ይግ በር ፡ ከመ ዚአሁ ፡ አላ ፣ ይትከሀሎ ፡ ይለድ ፡ በከመ ፡፣ ዚአሁ ። በከመ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ ዘይወልድ ፡ ዖፈ ፡ ወሰብእ ፡ ዘይወልድ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዚአሁ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጠየቅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እምነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢተገብረ ፡ አእመርነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አምላክ ፡፣ ዘበአማን ፡ ዘዕሩይ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አብ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ከማሁ ፡
11 In the same way is the Son of God in truth, God in truth as his Father. And thus said the 318, that He is equal to the Father in his divinity: he was generated and was not made. This means that God did not create him but generated him from his essence and from his divinity. If He had been created he would not be equal to the Father in his divinity, because no one can, nor is it possible for him to create (someone) like him, but it is possible for him to generate (someone) like him, like the bird that generates a bird, and a man who generates a man like him. In the same way if we ascertain that Christ was born from God and was not created, we have understood that He, like his Father is God in truth, that he is equal to Him in his divinity because also the Son is like his Father. And if the Father is God in truth, in the same way also the Son, (he) too is God in truth. The Son is creator as the Father. The Father and the Son are co-equal


[p. 172] {#po.56.p172}
ወልድኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ፣ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡፣ አብ ፡ ብርነነ ፡ ወወልድኒ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ብርሃን' ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አብ ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ወልድኒ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻፲ ወ፳ቿ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ አጠየቁነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ። ናሁ ፡ ተከ ሥተ ፡፣ ለነ ፡ በዘተናገርነ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ህላዌ" ፡ ዘሀለዉ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ" ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ካልእ ፡ አብ ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወወል ድኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አብ ። ወንፈቅድ ፡ ንክሥት ፡ እፎ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ተሰነ አው ፡ በኃይል ፡ ወገቢር ፡ ወምክር ፡ በከመ ፡ ከሠትነ ፣ ተሰናእዎቶሙ ፡ "በመለኮት ፡ ወጠባይዓት'' ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ኢንኩን ፡ ናምልክ'!! ፡ ክልኤ ፡ አማል ክተ ፡ ውሉጣነ ፡ ወንክሐድ"' ፡ ክሕደተ ፡ ገሃደ ። እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢንከሥት ፡ ከመ ፡ እሎንቱ" ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ በተ ሰናእዎቶሙ ፡ በመለኮቶሙ ፡ ወባሕርይሆሙ ፡ ተሰናአው ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በምክ ሮሙ ፣ ወኃይሎሙ ፣ ወግብሮሙ”"፤ ፣ ንሕነ ፡፣ ንክሕድ ፡ ዘበአማን ።
12 And if the Father is light also the Son is likewise light, and if the Father is creator, so also the Son is creator as his Father. Thus said the 318: “Because through him every thing existed”. They ascertained to us that He is creator as his Father. It was therefore revealed to us what we said (about) the two hypostases that exist, the Father and the Only-begotten Son, without (any) other father and without (any) other son and also the son without father. And we wish to expose how they agreed in power, in action and in counsel, as we have exposed their agreement in the divinity and in the substances, so that we would not worship two altered divinities, (and) would not commit a manifest apostasy. Because if we did not declare that these two, the Father and also the Son were in agreement in their divinity and in their substance, also in their counsel, in their power and in their action, we truly apostatize.

### Parenesis

We have therefore illustrated to you that the Father and the Son were in agreement in the divinity and in the essence and understand what we tell you so that their agreement in power, in will and in action may be ascertained among you. ### Articulation of the father-son analogy
ወውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወአቡሁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዘመደ ፣ ፍጥረት' ፡ ይሰናአው ፡ ፣በጠባይዓት ፡ ወባሕርይ፤ ፡ ወኢይሰናአው ፡ በፈቃድ ፣ ወኃይል" ፡፣ ወግብር ። አላ ፡ ይትፈለጡ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ወምክንያተ ፡ ፍልጠ ቶሙ ፣ በዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ መንፈስ' ፡ ወለ ካልኡ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ካልእ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ፍልጠቶሙ ፡ ለመናፍስት ፡ ተፈ ልጡ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በፈቃዶሙ ፡ ወሕይወቶሙ ፡ ወኃይሎሙ ፡ ወግብሮሙ ። ወሶበሰ ፣ ይከውን ፡ ለክልኤቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ የሐይዉ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ክል ኤሆሙ ፣ እምኮነ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወፈቃዶሙ ፡ አሐዱ'' ፣ ወኃይ ሎሙ ፡ አሐዱ፤! ፡ ወግብሮሙ ፡ አሐዱ" ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ለክልኤቱ ፡ ለባሕቲ ቶሙ ፡ አላ ፡ ለአእላፈ ፣ አእላፋት ፡ ወትእልፊተ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ለኩሎመሙ-'" ፡ ይከውን ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ ለኩ
14 Therefore, as every son and his father, of all kinds of creature, are in agreement in natural elements and in nature but do not agree in will, in power and in action but separate in this. The reason for their separation in this, is that each of them possesses, one a spirit, and another a spirit. And regarding the separation of the spirits, they separated by their will, their life, their power, and their action. And if for the two there were only one spirit for which both live, the life of both would be one; one their will; one their power and one their action. Not only in the case of these two, but for thousands of thousands, and for myriads of thousands, if there were a unique spirit for all, the life of all of them would be one life and one power and one action.

### Singularity of the relationship between the Father and the Son
ሎሙ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ሕይወተ" ፣ ወአሐደ" ፣ ኃይለ"! ፡ ወአሐደ'' ፣ ግብረ” ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ኢይትከሀል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ አላ ፡ አብ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ወወልዱ"' ፣ ፈጣሪ ፡ በከመ ፣ አቡሁ ፡ ይትከሃሎሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈሶሙ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወወልድ ። ወአኮ ፡ ባለለአ ሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ” ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ካልእ ፡ በከመ ፡ ክልኤቱ" ፡ ዘፍ ጡራን ፡ አላ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡፣ ለአብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ መንፈሱ ፡ ለወልድ ። ወጠየ ቅነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ወመድኃኒነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለአር ዳኢሁ ፣ ሖሩ” ፣ ወመሀሩ ፣ ኩሎ” ፣ አሕዛብ” ፡ ወአጥምቅዎሙ” ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወኢይቤ” ፡ ክልኤቱ" ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አላ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
15 But it is not possible that this happens within the created, but the Father creator and the Son his, creator as his Father, can this because their spirit is a unique spirit for the Father and for the Son. There is not another spirit for each of them like two creatures, but the spirit of the Father is the spirit of the Son. We have ascertained it from the word of our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ to his disciples: “Go and teach all nations and baptize them in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit” (Mt 28:19) and he did not say “two Holy Spirit” but “Holy Spirit”.

### The Father and the Son have a unique Holy Spirit. Procession of the Holy Spirit from the Father to the Son
አጠየቅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፣ኢይኩኑ ፡ ፍሉጣነ” ፣ በከመ ፡ ፍልጠተ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ዘፍጡ ራን ። አላ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ አሐደ ፡ መንፈሰ” ፡፣ ወውእቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ" ፡ ለወልድ ። ወውእቱ" ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መን ፈሶሙ ፡፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ ዘሠረጸ ፡ እምአብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወልድ ። ወሠረጸሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዝብሂል ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምአብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወልድ ፡፣ ጽኑዕ ፡ በአብ ፡ ወበወልድ” ። ኢይትፈለጥ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ወኢይ ትፈለጥ ፣ እምወልድ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወለወልድ ፡
16 We have ascertained that the Father and the Son do not have two spirits so that they are not separated in the likeness of the creaturely separation between a father and (his) son, but they have one (only) Spirit, that is the Holy Spirit which is the Spirit of the Father and the Spirit of the Son and He is the life of the Father and He is the life of the Son because He is their Spirit and He is their life that proceeded from the Father to the Son (Jn 15:26). “Proceeded” means that it is He who


[p. 176] {#po.56.p176}
ዘይሄሉ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ጽኑዕ! ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ። ወኢይትፈለጥ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወግብሮሙ፡! ፡ አሐዱ ። ናሁ ፡ ተከሥተ ፣ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ" ፡ አሐዱ ፡ በሕይወት ፡ ወኃይል ፡ ወፈቃድ ፡ ወገቢር ፡ በ'ከመ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ከመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ በመለኮት ፡ ወባሕርይ ። ወለእመ ፡ ጥኑ ፣ ኅዳጣነ' ፡ ልቡና ፡ ወአእምሮ ፣ ኢይሌብው ፡ ትርጓሜ ፣ ቃልነ ፡ ከመ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ" ፣ ውስተ ፡ አብ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ወልድ ። አንሰ ፣ እሜስል" ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ለዝክቱ'' ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይለብው፤' ፡ እፎ ፡ ውእቱ ።
went out from the Father to the Son, firm in the Father and in the Son. He does not separate from the Father and does not separate from the Son. Because He is the Spirit of the Father and of the Son that is inside Them, firm inside Them. And he does not separate from Them because He is one, and Their action is one. It was therefore illustrated that They are one in life, in power, in will and in action as it was illustrated that They are one in divinity and in substance. And if those of scarce mind and knowledge do not understand the interpretation of our word that the Holy Spirit is inside the Father and inside the Son, I will make a likeness to this so that they understand how it is.
ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢይትሜሰል ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ወኢ ምንትኒ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ንመስል ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ በከመ ፡ መሰለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለርእሱ ። እስመ ፡ ሰማዕነ ፡ እግ ዚእነ'' ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ‹በአጽባዕተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ አወፅኦሙ"' ፣ ለአጋ ንንት ። ወይቤ ፡ በኦሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ጸሐፈ ፡ ዐሠርተ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ በአጻ ብዒሁ ፡ አእመርነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መሰለ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በአጽባዕት ፡ ወእምኔሁ ፣ ነአምር ፣ ጥያቄ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ። ዝብሂል ፡ ከመ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ" ፣ ውስተ ፡ አብ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ እምአብ" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አጽባዕት ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ክፍል ። ፣ወለለአ ሐዱ ፡ ክፍል'' ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ መንኮብ ፡ ኅቡራን ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወንቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ክፍል ፡፣ ቀዳማይ ፡ ዘጉንድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘአ ልቦቱ ፡ አምሳለ' ፡ ወኢምሳሌ ። አላ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘያጽሕቀነ ፡፣ አስተማሰል

### The three segments of the finger as an analogy of the relationship between the Three Persons
17 If to God it were impossible to apply any likeness and there is no (idea) on how (God) is, we however apply likenesses to Him, as (also) He applied likenesses to himself. We have in fact heard our Lord who said: “I cast out demons with the finger of God” (Lk 11:20). And in the Torah he said that He wrote the ten words with his finger (Ex 31:18); we have learned that He depicted himself with the finger and from Him we have the certainty of this word. This means that the Holy Spirit is in the Father and in the Son; he goes out from the Father towards the Son. The unique finger has in fact three parts and for every part of it there is a joint, (by which the three parts) are united among themselves. We said that the first part of the stump is a likeness of the Father who has neither likeness, nor resemblance, but it is because we are concerned about it that we have likened him due to the weakness of our minds in the capacity to know.
ናሁ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ድካመ ፡ አልባቢነ ፡ ለአእምሮ ፡ ክሂሎቱ ። እስመ ፡ ክፍል ፡ ቀዳማይ ፡ ከመ” ፡ አምሳሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ጉጐንደ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ። "ወክፍለ ፡ ካልአ” ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ጽፍር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምአብ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ "አምሳለ ፡ ወኢምሳሌ”! ። ወክፍል ፡ ዘማእከለ ፡ ክልኤ” ፡ ክፍል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ አምሳለ?" ፡ ወኢም ሳሌ ፡፣ ወበከመ ፡ ክፍል ፡ ዘማእከል” ፣ ኅቡር” ፡ ምስለ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ክፍል ፣ ጽኑፅ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ እምክፍለ ፡ ጉንድ” ፡ ኀበ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኅቡር"' ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወይወፅእ” ፣ እምአብ ፡ ኀበ” ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወኢይትፈለጥ ፡ እም ኔሆሙ” ። ወበከመ ፣ ክፍል ፡ ዘማእከል ፡ ዘአጽባዕት ፡ ኢይትፈለጥ ፣ እም
18 The first part is like a likeness of him, because He is the stump of every action, and the second part which has the nail, is the likeness of the Son who was born from the Father even if he has neither likeness, nor resemblance. The part in the middle between the two parts is the likeness of the Holy Spirit even if he has neither likeness, nor resemblance. As the central part is united to the two parts, is firm in the middle of them, goes out from the part of the stump to the second, so the Holy Spirit is united to the Father and to the Son. He is firm in Them and goes out from the Father towards the Son, and does not separate from Them. As the central part of the finger does not
ክልኤቱ ፡ ክፍል ፡ ወኢይትከሠት፤ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፣ ወኢፅርዐት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢይትፈለጥ ፡ እምአብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወኢይትከሠት" ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወይወፅእ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ ወኢፅ ርዐት ። ወበከመ ፣ ክፍል ፡ ዘማእከል ፡ ወዘድኅር ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ጽፍር ፡ ይወ ርዱ ፡ ወየዓርጉ ፡ ወኢይትፈለጡ ፡ እምክፍለ ፡ ጉንድ ወክፍለ ፡ ጉንድሰ ፣ ኢየዓርግ ፡ ወኢይወርድ ። ከማሁ ፡ ንቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እምሰ
separate from the two parts and is not distinguished from them, like one of them, without (causing) separation and interruption, so the Holy Spirit does not separate from the Father and from the Son. And he is not distinguished from Them, like one of Them; he goes out from Them without separation nor interruption. As the central part, and the posterior one that has the nail, descend and ascend and do not separate from the part of the stump, while the stump does not ascend and does not descend, so we say that the Son descended from heaven and became incarnate.

19 In the same way, the Holy Spirit descended together with the Son, as John saw Him descending upon the Son in the river Jordan (Mt 3:16), and He did not separate from the Father. Thus, it was not said of the Father that He descended, nor that He ascended. The back of the fingernail, which we said is a likeness of the Son, (this) has only the fingernail. Likewise, the Son alone became incarnate, while neither the Father nor the Holy Spirit became incarnate; and just as the finger performs every action with the back part that has the fingernail, so God the Father performed all His works through His Son: the heaven and the earth, the water and the fire, the wind and the angels, the birds and the fish, the animals and the beasts, the reptiles and humans, everything that is not seen, were created through the Son. As it says in the holy Gospel: “For every work existed through Him, but without Him nothing existed, nothing of that which has existed” (Jn 1:3). Panegyric on the metaphor of the finger
ወዘእንበሌሁሰ ፡ ፣አልቦ ፡ ዘኮነ'' ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ኮነ ። ነጽር'" ፣ ኦእኅው ፡ ለዝንቱ” ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ዐቢይ"”' ፡ ዘረሰዮ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር?! ፡ ልዑል ፡ ፈጣሪ ፣ ዓቢይ ፣ ጠቢብ” ፡ በውስተ ፡ አጽባዕ ፡ ከሠተ ፣ እምኔሁ ፣ ፍካሬ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህሳዌሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣፡ ወተፈልጦቶሙ ፡፣፡ በኅቡር” ፤ ወኅብረቶሙ” ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ። ወተሠገወ ፡፣ እምኔሆሙ ፣ አሐዱ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ክልኤቱ” ፡፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ "ይወርዱ ፡ ወየዓርጉ ። ወአ ሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ” ፣ ኢይወርድ” ፡ ወኢየዓርግ ፡ ዘውእቱ” ፡ ጉንድ ፡ ዕቀብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምሳሌ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወክቡር ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ አማዕቀቦ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ በጥበቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አጽባዒሁ” ፣ ለሰብእ ። ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንግርዎሙ ፡ ለኅዳጣነ ፡፣ ልቡና ፡ ወአእምሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጠ የቅ"! ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አልባቢሆሙ ፣ ወይለብው ፣ ምሥጢረ ፡ ዘኅ ቡእ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ።
20 Observe, O brother, this great similitude that God the Most High, the great and wise Creator, has placed upon the finger; through it, He illustrated the interpretation of the three essences of God, their distinction in unity, and their unity without distinction. Among Them, only one became incarnate; two of Them descend and ascend, while one of Them does not descend and does not ascend; He is the root. Guard this similitude, for it is an exalted and venerable similitude. The Creator in His wisdom entrusted the abundance of the mystery to the human finger. Tell all this to those who are lacking in mind and knowledge, so that the explanation may be affirmed in their minds and they may understand the mystery that is hidden from them.

### Instruction to the learned from Scripture
ወለመምህራንሰ፤ ፡ ወለለባውያን ፡ ንቤሎሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወንከሥት ፣ ሎሙ ፣ ሠለስተ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እምነ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወመጽሐፍ ፡፣፡ ኅቡረ ። ወንዌጥን ፣ ወንቤ ፡ እምነ ፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ልብ!፣ እስመ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ክቡር ፣ ወየዓቢ ፡ እምልብ ፡ ዘፍጡር ። ፣እስመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ኦሪት" ፣ ከመ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፍጥረተ' ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፣ በወልዱ ፡ ወበመንፈሱ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምሳክ ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወዘ ሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ለይኩን ፡ ብርሃን ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ወገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ብርሃነ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወርእዮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለብርሃን ፡ ከመ ፡
21 Thus we speak to the teachers and the intelligent, and we set forth to them the threefold essence of God, both from the mind and from the Book. We begin and pronounce from the Book even before the mind, because the Book is the word of God, the Creator, which is more important and greater than the mind of the creature. For it is written in the book of the Torah that when God created all creatures, He created them by means of His Son and His Spirit, because He is God like His Father and is together with Him. In the Book of Creation, He in fact said, “God said: ‘Let there be light’” (Gen 1:3). And again He says, “God made the light,” and again He says: “And God saw that the light was good” (Gen 1:4).

### Hermeneutics of Gen 1
ውእቱ ፡ ሠናይ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወመንፈሰ ፣ አም ላክ'' ፡ ቫሀሎ ፡ ምስለ'!! ፡ አብ ፡ አምላክ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ጥሙ ፡ ፈጠረ"፣ አብ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፍጥረተ ። እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ለይኩን ፡ ብርሃን ። ዘንተ'' ፡ ይቤ" ፡ "በቃሉ ፡ ወበመንፈሱ" ፣ የአብ ፡ ይቤ"! ፡ ለይኩን'' ፡ ብርሃን ። ወዘሰ ፡ ዘይቤ!" ፡ ወገብረ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ብርሃን” ፣ ዝብሂል ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ወበመንፈሱ?' ፡ የአምላክ ፡ ወወልዱ ።፤ ፈጠሮ ፡ ለብርሃን” ። ወከማሁ ፡ በፍጥረተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ፣ወበምድር ፡ ወበቀ፦ ላት ፡ ወፀሐይ?”' ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብት ፡ ወዓሣት ፡ ወአዕዋፍ ፡ ወእንስሳ ፣ ወአራዊት ፡ ወተሐዋስያን ። ወሶበ ፣ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እም ኔሆሙ ። ይቤ ፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ "ወርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ ከመ ፣ ሠናይ” ። "ወእምዝ ፣ ይቤ ፡፣ ወገብረ” ፣ እግዚአብሔር?' ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወከመዝ” ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሠናይ ። ከሠተ ፡፣ በውስተ” ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነ” ፣ አሐዱ ፡፣ ይኤዝዝ ፡ በፍጥረቶሙ ፡ ወካልኡ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ዘአዘዘ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ነጽር ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ለዘገብረ ፡ ወየአድሞ'! ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ”' ፡ ሠናይ ። ወአጠየቀ ፣ መለኮቶ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወመለኮቶ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወመለኮቶ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ”፣0፣ ከመ” ፣ ተሰምዩ ፡ አሐዱ” ፣ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ እስመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ፣ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን” ። እስመ?' ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዐረቢ ፡
22 This explanation, that the Son of God and the Spirit of God are with God the Father, is great, because God the Father created every creature by means of Them. The Book declares: “God said — ‘Let there be light’”. He declares this by means of His Word and His Spirit. The Father said: “Let there be light”. And this which He said: “And God made the light,” this means that by means of His Word and His Spirit, God and His Son created the light. Likewise in the creation of the heaven, the earth, and the plants, the sun, the moon and the stars, the fish, the birds, the animals, the beasts and the reptiles, when He created each of them, the Book says: “And God saw that it was good”. Thereafter He said and God made such and such. And it says again: “And God saw that it was good”. (God) revealed in each creature that [ One commanded so that they might be created and the Other executed what was commanded to Him. Therefore observe what He commanded and it pleased Him because it is good (Gen 1:4 etc.). He confirmed the divinity of the Father and the divinity of the Son and the divinity of the Holy Spirit: each of Them was named God because the Son is truly God, and the Holy Spirit


[p. 182] {#po.56.p182}
አምላክ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወወልድኒ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወአቡሁኒ ፡ አምላክ ። ወመ ንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስኒ ፣ አምላክ' ።
is truly God. For the meaning of (the word) Lord in the Arabic language is God. The Son is also God, and His Father is also God, and the Holy Spirit is also God.

### Trinitarian hermeneutics of Gen 1:26
በከመ ፣ ከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ በትርጓሜ ። ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈጠሮ ፡ ለአዳም ። ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምሳክ ፡ ንግበር ፣፡ ሰብአ ፡ በከመ ፡ አርያነ ፡ ወአምሳሊነ' ። አልቦ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ዘዝየዓቢ' ፡ እም ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር" ፡ እስመ ፡ ክሥት ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ከመ ፡ አብ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ዘሀለው ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ንግበር ፡ ሰብአ ፡ በከመ ፡ አርአያነ ፡ ወአምሳሊነ' ። አጠየ ቀነ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ፣ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ ሀለዉ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጠሮ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ በከመ ፣ አርአያሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ዝብሂል ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ፈጠሮ ፣ ለሰብእ ፡ የከመ ፡ አርአያሁ፤! ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘእም ኔሁ'' ፣ ያስተርኢ ፡ በሥጋ ። እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለወ
23 As I have set forth to you in the interpretation, the Book of the Torah says: “when He created Adam, the Lord God said, ‘Let us make man in our image and our likeness’” (Gen 1:26). There is no explanation that is greater than this word, because it is manifest that God the Father said to the Son and to the Holy Spirit who were with Him: “Let us make man in our image and our likeness” (Gen 1:26). The Book has confirmed to us that the Son and the Holy Spirit are with the Father. As for what it says, that God made man in the image of God, this means that God created man in the image of His Son, God, from whom He is manifested in the flesh. In fact, man in his flesh is the image of the Son who became incarnate, as it says here, and he in his spirit is the image of the Triune Saint.

### Anthropocentric hermeneutics of Gen 1:26
ልድ ፣ ዘተሠገዎ'' ፡፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ" ፡፣ አርአ ያሁ ፣ ለሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንግበር ፡ ሰብአ ፡ በከመ ፡ አምሳሊነ ፡ ወአርአያነ'' ፡ ወገጽነ'' ። አጠየቀነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ሥሉስ'' ፡ እስመ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ ዘሥሉስ” ፣ ወሠለስቱሰ ፡ ለመንፈሱ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ለሥጋሁ ። እስመ ፣ መንፈሱ ፡ ለሰብእ ፣ ሠለስቱ” ። ልብ ፡ ወነ ቢብ ፡ ወሕይወት ። ወልብሰ ፡ ይወልድ ፡ ነቢበ ። ለልብ ፡ ወለነቢብ ፡ ሕይ ወቶሙ ፡ መንፈስ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተሰምየት ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ወነባ ቢት” ፣ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወነቢብ ። ዝንቱ"! ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ሥሉስ” ፣ ዘበአማን ፡ ወአርአያሁ ፡ ዘሠዐለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ሥሉስ ፣ ቅዱስ” ፤ በውስተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ጣይሬኢያ ፡ በአዕይንተ” ፡ ልቡ ፡ ያእምር” ፡ እምኔሃ ፣ ሠለስተ ፡፣ ህሳዌሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተዋ ሕዶቶሙ” ። እስመ ፡ ልብ ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ አብ ፡ ወላዔ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወቃልኒ ፣
24 As God said: “Let us make man in our likeness and our image and our face”. He assured us that man is the image of the Triune because he has the three essences of the Triune, but the three are of his spirit and not of his flesh, because the spirit of man is threefold: the mind, the speech, and the life. The mind generates the speech; the spirit is the life of the mind and of the speech, therefore it has been called a rational and speaking spirit because it is the life of the mind and of the speech. This is truly the likeness of the Triune and the image that the Triune and Holy God designed in man, so that he who sees with the eyes of his mind may recognize from it the three hypostases of God and their unity. For the mind is the Father who generates the Word, and the Word, the Son, is He who is generated by the mind, always without interruption or separation

mind he prepares many words so that he may speak without moving his mouth or his tongue; thus when he is silent he writes many words in his mind. And after he has spoken, that word is fulfilled from his mind and does not separate from it but is always born in his mind from it, without interruption or separation. When he desires to manifest it, he incarnates it with his tongue and it is manifested to the listener.

26 Thus the Son who exists eternally, that is, the Word of God the Father, is born of God the Father always, without interruption or separation. His divine birth is eternally together with Him, firm within Him. When He willed to reveal Himself to carnal humans, He became incarnate and was manifested in the flesh, just as the word is revealed when it is incarnated in the tongue. After His incarnation, He did not separate from His Father and was not distinguished, nor did His birth end, but He is always born of Him at every time, just as the word does not separate from the mind of the creature after its incarnation in the tongue, and does not separate from it and does not break. The mind and the word are the likeness of the Father and the Son and they have one Spirit: He is their life. This means that the spirit of man which is in his mind and in his word is like the likeness of the Holy Spirit who is united to the Father and the Son, and He is their life. This is the image of the Triune God that He designed in Adam and it became a witness that it is His image and His likeness so that creatures might contemplate Him and through it recognize Him as He is of threefold hypostasis, the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit.

### Against the apostates and reiteration of the hermeneutics of Gen 1:26

27 Whoever has denied that God is three hypostases, the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, the image of God disavows him, and in it (the denial) he reveals his apostasy, that is, that this person worships a foreign god and does not worship the God of truth, (he) who is the image of God and His likeness. In fact, He, glory be to Him, for His mercy toward man (desires) that he not worship a foreign god, except His image designed within them, so that they might know Him from it, since this image of His is within them. But they, because of the weakness of their minds, did not recognize Him from it, that He is three Hypostases, until the only Son clothed Himself with it, and incarnating in

it, spoke through it, and confirmed to them that it is the image and likeness of His Father and of the Holy Spirit, who is the rational and speaking spirit of man, in which the Word of God, the only Son, was incarnated when He became incarnate and revealed that it is the image of the Triune. When He said to His disciples: “Go and teach all nations and baptize them in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit” (Mt 28:19), He confirms the Word of the Most High God who said: “Let us make man in our image and our likeness” (Gen 1:26). The spirit of man is rational and speaking, in the likeness of the Triune who wore flesh and became human in it. As for man, as (the citation) said, both in his exterior and in his interior he is the image of God.

### Consequences of the acceptance or rejection of the hermeneutics of Gen 1:26
ስጡ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡፣ ይቤ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፣ ይበቁዕ ፡ ለጥያቄ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እምነ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወመጽሐፍ ፡ ለዘአብርሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወአኮ ፡ ለዘአዖረ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ በሕጸተ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኅለየ ፡ አዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይኩን ፣ አምሳከ ፡ በስሕተተ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ወበልዐ ፡ እምፅፅ ፡ ወተዐርቀ ፡ እምክብሩ!' ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ ኀፍረቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጽ ሐፈ ፣ እግዚአብሔር'' ፣ ተሳለቀ!" ፣ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይቤ ፡ናሁ ፡ ኮነ፣፡ አዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ክሥት ፡ ከመ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ወለመንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሳለቅ ፡ ላፅለ ፡ አዳም ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ኃሠሠ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ አምላከ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕራቆ!' ፡ ኅፉረ ። ወዘንተ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ በመዝሙር ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ'' ፡ ይስሕ ቆሙ ፣ ወእግዚአሔር ፡ ይሳለቅ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወተናገርኩ"' ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ኢይትቃረነነ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ንቤ"! ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣
28 This word is useful for the confirmation of the Christian faith based on reason and the Book to him whose eyes of his heart God has illuminated. It is not (useful) to him whose eyes He has blinded due to the deficiency of his faith. In fact, when Adam thought to become God by the seduction of the serpent (Wis 2:24) and ate from the tree, he was stripped of his honor and his shame was unveiled; the Book says that God mocked him and said: “Behold, Adam has become like one of us” (Gen 3:22). This word is clear; God pronounced this word to His Son and to His Holy Spirit, mocking Adam, because he sought to become God like one of us, (instead) he became naked and reduced to shame. This is as David said in the Psalm: “He who sits in heaven laughs at them and God mocks them” (Ps 2:4). I have said this word so that no one may contradict us when we say that God mocked Adam.

### Trinitarian reading of Gen 11:6-7 in polemic with the Arabs

29 When after the flood all men were of one language, they gathered to build a tower, the book of God reports that God said: “Behold, all are one lip and one language and they have begun to do this. Let us not let them finish their work. Come, let us descend, let us divide their languages” (Gen 11:6-7). He manifested in truth that it is to His Son and to His Holy Spirit who are with Him that He addressed (saying): “Come, let us descend, let us divide their languages,” as He had said to them at the beginning: “Come, let us make man according to our image and our likeness.” But They

are Creators together with Him and makers of all creatures, so that none of the Arabic-speaking people may contradict us.

### Elucubration on Arabic grammar
ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቃሉሰ ፡ ንዑ ፡ ያኤምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፡ እምክልአ ቱቱ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ይደሉ! ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበል ፡ ለክል ኤቱ ፡ ነዓ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ወልሳነ ፡ ዕብ ራይስጥሰ" ፡ አልቦ ፡ ከመዝ ። አላ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ለክ ልኤቱ፡ ፡ ንፁ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ሠለስቱ' ፡ ለሠለስቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሉ ፣ ለአሐዱ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበሉ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ነዐ ፡ አላ ፡ ንዑ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ወያመከንዩ" ፡ በእበድ ፡ ይፈ ቅዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሐስዉ ፡ ሠለስቱ" ፣ ህላዌሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዚአሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ፈጠርነ ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ገበርነ ። አእምሩ'' ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ቃል ፡ ይጤይቅ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። አመክነዩ'' ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በትቢዕት ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢይደልዎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ልሳናት ፡ ይሬስይ
30 And He said: “because the word ‘come’ (venite) shows that He is more than two,” they retorted: “It is necessary that to two He say: ‘Come’ (vieni)”. It is a matter of an Arabic linguistic custom, while in the Hebrew language there is no such thing. Rather, when they say two (they use) “come” (venite), likewise, when they refer to three, to the three (they say: “come”), while when they speak to one (they say): “come” (vieni). It is not necessary that they say to many: “Come” (vieni) but “come” (venite) as the Arabic-speaking people do. And they adduced pretexts in their folly, and wanted to deny the threefold essence of God, because they had heard their book saying: “God said: ‘We have created, we have made’.” Know that this word validates the Christian religion. They explained it with pride but this is not lawful for them because all languages prove them false in that, and especially the Hebrew language.

31 When God became angry against Sodom and Gomorrah, the book of God says: “God rained from heaven, from near God, fire and brimstone upon Sodom and Gomorrah” (Gen 19:23). He manifested in this the divinity of the Father and the Son as He had manifested the divinity of them in the previous word (Gen 1:26; 11:7); in the same way He manifests the divinity of the Triune Saint. And this is in the book of Exodus, from the Torah, when He said to Moses: “I am the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob” (Ex 3:6), and He did not say “the God of Abraham and Isaac and Jacob” but repeated the divine name three times, saying: “I am God, God, God” to confirm that His three Hypostases are one equal divinity.

32 And David in the second Psalm confirms that Christ, in the flesh, was born of his lineage. He is the Son of God when he says: “God said to me: ‘You are my son and today I have generated you’” (Ps 2:7). And he confirms the divinity of the Father and the Son, when in Psalm 109 the Lord says to my Lord: “Sit at my right hand until I subject your enemies under the footstool of your feet” (Ps 109:1). And in this Psalm he reports again what the Father says to the Son: “I have generated you before the morning star” (Ps 109:3). As for what he called “morning star,” it is the devil, because the prophet Isaiah called him “morning star” (Is 14:12) and he is the first living being that God created. The Father said to the Son: “I have generated you before every living creature,” therefore His existence together with His Father has been manifested to us.

33 And in Psalm 44 he confirms the divinity of the Father and the Son as well as the humanity of the Son, who is the Messiah of God. Thus he says to the Son: “Your throne, O Lord, is for eternity; the scepter of justice is the scepter of your kingdom; you have loved justice and hated injustice, therefore the Lord your God has anointed you with the oil of gladness above those like you” (Ps 44:7-8). In that prophecy, David manifested the whole mystery of God because he clarified that He is the Son of God when he says: “Your throne, O Lord”; in this he named Him God because He is God, Son of God. He revealed His existence when he says: “for eternity,” and he declared His kingdom when he says: “the scepter of justice, and the scepter of your kingdom.” He revealed that He is man and the Messiah of God His Father when he says: “The Lord your God has anointed you with the oil of gladness.” With this he has therefore confirmed to those who possess intellect all the mysteries of the Christian faith.

### Trinitarian reading of Isaiah 6:3 in polemic with the Jews

### 34 The prophet Isaiah also became a witness when he confirms the Triune

Everything that we have told you from the Torah and everything that is in the Prophets they read and do not deny even a word of it; they read them as we (also) read them, but their minds are closed and they do not see it and do not understand it because God has closed them for their fatness (Is 6:9-10; Mt 13:13-14). As Isaiah the prophet was a witness against them, with that prophecy which is a prophecy of sanctification, that sanctification is a testimony against them, much more than all the prophets.

35 When in fact they gather in their synagogue on the day of the Jewish Sabbath, the administrator stands before them and pronounces in the Hebrew language this explanation of his and they do not deny it. He says this: “We sanctify you, we exalt you, for you we triple three sanctifications, as it is written in the prophet.” And they answer him one at a time, and all the Jews shout saying: “Holy! Holy! Holy! Lord of Hosts, the whole earth is full of the holiness of your glory!”. What is greater than this declaration? They in fact, if their minds were not blind, profess the Triune. Through this prophecy, the prophet has in fact revealed that God is three Hypostases, one only God, and the whole earth is filled with His glory in the manifestation of His Trinity. The new worship with the coming of Christ

in His name, to sanctify Him, to glorify Him, and to sacrifice to Him offerings and incense in the whole earth. The prophecy of Isaiah concerning the manifestation of the Triune Saint was fulfilled and the earth was filled with His glory. Parenesis to accept the faith in Christ and to remain firm in it 37 And concerning the twenty-four prophets we could have said much and at length, but from what we have said, a single word will be advantageous to him who believes in God and trusts in His word and does not trouble himself (to seek) one different from this word of the book of God in the Torah and in the Prophets. We do not trouble ourselves to repeat the words of the holy Gospels, of the holy books of the Apostles of Christ, because all of them were witnesses of this and confirmed it. In fact, the holy Gospels revealed what the Torah and the Prophets said in an occult way; they explained it and made it manifest.

38 For the Gospel became a witness that the Son was manifested and was recognized and they saw Him in His flesh, and the Holy Spirit descended upon Him in the likeness of the appearance of a dove, and the Father who from the heavens said: “This is my Son whom I love in whom I am well pleased” (Mt 3:17). And in the same way on Mount Tabor the Father became a witness with His word for a second time, saying: “This is my Son whom I love and in Him my soul is well pleased! Listen to Him” (Mt 17:5). And again, our Lord Jesus Christ said: “O my Father, glorify your Son!” (Jn 17:1). The Father called Him from heaven saying: “Behold, I have glorified you and I will glorify you again” (Jn 12:28). The Son also, in many places, confirms the three Hypostases with what is due to Him (in terms) of glory and honor, together with the Father and the Holy Spirit, because They are equal in divinity and in essence.

### Summary

I have manifested to you, O beloved, what you will say to the intelligent and to the teachers from the book of God. I have therefore manifested to you again what you will say to them from the mind. And if in that great section I had mentioned to you concerning the image

40 As for man, he is His image and His likeness and he is living, undoubtedly rational, and He Himself became a witness of Himself in this, in all the books. And when He became living and rational but without flesh, and it is not known how He is, He has no limit, He is not circumscribable, He does not change, and He does not move from one work to another. And it is not a transitory rationality like human rationality that passes and changes, but His rationality is like His essence, because He does not reason in this hour and is silent in the other hour, and does not move from work to work, but He is always rational and His rationality is always existing, as His intrinsic essence is eternal without mutation. If it is so, it is not possible for anyone to deny this. Behold, we have clarified to you that His rationality exists together with Him; what is from always was born from Him, in the same way as the essence that has existed (from always). But His existence is not like the essence that generates Him, because if we say that He is Son and that He has completed His birth, we separate Him from Him.

### Reiterated speculation on the relationship between the Three Persons
ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልደ” ፡ ወፈጸመ ፡ ልደቶ ፡ አፍለጥናሁ” ፡ እምኔሁ ። አላ ፡ ንቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወላዲሁ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ኢይሴስል ፣ ወኢየ ኀልፍ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወይትወለድ”! ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ ወኢው ላጤ ፡ እስመ ፣ መንፈሶሙ" ፡ አሐዱ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘዕ ሩይ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ንቤ” ፣ በእንተ ፡፣ ልብ ፡ ወቃል ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ዘፈጠሮሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአርአያሁ” ፡ ወአ ምሳሊሁ ፡ ወርእስሰ ፡ ወላዲ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ። ወነቢብ ፡ ዘተወልደ” ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቃል ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘኢይሴ ስል ፡ ወወትረ ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ እም ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አምላክ ፡ ጸራቅሊጦስ ፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ኅብረ ቶሙ” ፣ ወተዋሕዶቶሙ ፡ በፈቃዶሙ” ፣ ወኃይል ፡ ወገቢር ፡ አብኒ ፡ አም ላክ ፡ ወወልድኒ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ አምላክ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምሳከ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ አምላከ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ አም ላከ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወአኮ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ አማልክት ፡ እስመ ፡ መን ፈሶሙ ፣፡ ወባሕርየ ፡ መለኮቶሙ ፡ አሐዱ ። ወንሕነሰ ፡ "ንሬኢ ፡ ብእሴ” ፡
41 But we maintain that He is His Father always, He never departs and never passes; He is born of Him without separation or mutation because their Spirit is one (only), this is the Holy Spirit who is equal to Them and He is Their life as we have said regarding the mind, the word, and the spirit that God created in His image and likeness. But the head is the generating one, God the Father. The Reason that was born from Him is the God Word who does not depart and is always born of God the Father and (of) the Holy Spirit God Paraclete, and He is Their life and through Him is their communion and unity in their will, power, and action. The Father is God, and the Son is also God, and the Holy Spirit is God. As He said to Moses: “I am the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob” (Ex 3:6), but They are not three divinities because Their spirit and the essence of their divinity are one. As for us, if


[p. 198] {#po.56.p198}
ወብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሰነአዉ ፡ በባሕርይ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰ መዩ ፡ ብዙኃን፤' ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ ስም" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተዋሕዶተ" ፡ ባሕርይሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢይሰነአዉ‹ ፡ ጣእንበለ ፡ በባሕርይ ፣፡ ወኢይሰነአዉ!፤ ፡ በሀልዎት ፡ ወሕይወት ፡
we see that one man and another agree only in nature, it is lawful that many of them be called by a single name by virtue of the unity of their nature, because they do not agree except in nature but do not agree in existing and in life.

### Gold as an analogy of the Trinity and Unity of God
በከመ ፣ ነአምር ፡ ከመ ፣ ባሕርይ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አሐዱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነሣእነ ፡ ሠለስተ ፡፣ ዲናራተ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ንሰሚ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፣ ዲናር ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡፣ ወኢንብል ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ወርቃት ፡ አላ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ባሕርይሆሙ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይትፈለጡ ፡ ህላዌሆሙ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወይትፈለጡ ፣ በትር ፈት ፡ ወሙስና ። እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ታውዒ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ዲናረ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ኢይውዕዩ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ካልአን ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢይ ኀብሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስሰ ፡ አኮ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ባሕ ርይ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ "አላ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ወለእመ' ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መን ፈስ" ፡ ሕይወቱ" ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወፈቃዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወኃይሉ ፡ ወግብሩ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ይትበሀል'' ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአ ማን ። ወአኮ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠለስተ ፡ አማልክት ፡ በከመ ፡፣ ኮነ ፣ ዝክቱ ፣ በምግባራት ፡ ዘተሰነአዉ ፡ በባሕርይ ፡ ወኢተሰናአዉ ፡ በካልኡ ፣፡ ወውእ ቱኬ ፣ ከመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ጠየቅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ወነባቢ ። ወከማሁ ፣፡ ሶበ ፡ ዘከርነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ነአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ወነ ባቢ'' ። ንሕነ ፡ ንብል ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወነአምር ፡ ጥያቄሁ ፡ ባጣአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ወኢንትአመን ፡ ከመ! ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔ ሆሙ ፡ የቃል ፡ ወመንፈስ! ፡ ይነብብ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወይሐዩ ፡ በባሕቲቱ" ፡ ባሕቱ'' ፣ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ለእመ ፣ ኮኑ ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ከመ"! ፡ ኢንስም
42 We know in fact that the nature of gold is all equal. If we take three denarii, we call each of them “gold denarius,” and because of the three we do not say that they are “three golds,” but one only gold because their nature is one only. Their essence is divided among them and they separate in remaining and in corruption, because if you burn one of the three denarii with fire, the other two do not burn because they are not united among themselves, but the Triune Saint is never an only essence but one only spirit, and if it is one only spirit, His life is one only, one only is His will, one only His power, and His action is one only, and it is necessary that each of Them be called “God” in truth. And not that They are three gods, like that which happened in creatures that are cohesive in nature but are not cohesive in anything else; this is therefore as we have clarified that the Father is living and rational. It is like when we remembered the Son, we know that He is living and rational. We pronounce this word and we know the truthfulness of each of Them and we do not believe that each of Them has a word and a spirit through which He speaks and lives alone. In fact, if They were like this we would not call them three Hypostases but nine. It is not lawful for us to say this but we confirm and believe that they are three stable, rational, and living Hypostases among Them and they do not need anything else.

### Analogy of the trunk with the Trinity
ዮሙ ፣ ሠለስተ ፡፣ ህላዌ ፡ አላ ፡ ተስዓተ ። ወኢይደሉ ፡ ንበል ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አላ ፣ ንሕነ ፡ ንጤይቅ ፡ ወነአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ ቀዋምያን ፡ ወነባቢያን ፡፣ ወሕያዋን ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ኢይጽህቁ'' ፡ ለካልእ ። እስመ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ለአብ ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእስ” ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ጉጐንድ ፡ ዘኢይጽህቆ'"' ፡ ለካልእ ፡ ጉጐንድ ፡ ወህላዌሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወህላዌሁ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወአብሰ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በርእሱ” ፣ ወነባቢ ፡ በወልዱ ፡ ወሕያው ፡ በመን ፈሱ፤' ፡ "ወመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ” ፣ ይቀውም ፡ በአብ ፣ ነባቢ” ፣ በወልድ” ፣
43 For the essence of the Father is that He is the head and He is the trunk that does not need another trunk. And the essence of the Son is that He is the word, and the essence of the Holy Spirit is that He is the life. But the Father subsists in Himself and speaks through His Son, living in His Spirit, and the Holy Spirit


[p. 200] {#po.56.p200}
ወሕያው፤ ፡ በርእሱ!" ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወለለአሐዱ ፣ አሐዱ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ በርእሱ ፡ ወክልኤቱ ፡ ካልአን ፡ ሱቱፋን ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ባቲ ። እስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ጉንድ ፡፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በርእሱ ። ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወወልድኒ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ቃል ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ነባቢ ፡ በርእሱ ። ወአብ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነባቢ ያን ፡ ቦቱ ። ወመንፈስ ፡፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ሕያው ፡ በርእሱ ። ወአብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ሕያዋን ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጠየቀ" ፡ ቦቱ" ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ትብል ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ወነባቢ ።
subsists in the Father, speaking through the Son, living in Himself since He is the essence of life. Each of Them has His own life and the other two participate in Him, through it. In fact, the Father is the trunk and is subsistent in Himself. The Son and the Holy Spirit also subsist in Him. The Son is the word and is rational in Himself, and the Father and the Holy Spirit speak through Him. The Holy Spirit is living in Himself and the Father and the Son are living in Him. And if through Him, you examine each of Them, you will say that He is living and speaking.

### Emphasis on the unity of the Three Persons and on the divinity of each of the Three Persons
ወሶበ ፡ ዘከርከ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ኢትበል ፡ ከመ ፡ ቦሙ ፣ ሠለስቱ ፡ ሕይ ወት ፡ ወሠለስቱ ፡ ቃላት ፡ አላ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወአሐዱ ፡ ነቢብ' ። ወከማሁ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ትጤይቆ ፡ ትብል ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወእግዚእ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዘከርከ ፡ ሠለስተ ፡ ኢት በል ፡ ከመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ አማልክት ፡ ወሠለስቱ" ፡ አጋእዝት ፡ አላ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወአሐዱ ፡ እግዚእ ። በከመ ፡ ንቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ወነቢብ ። እስመ ፣ አብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወወልድኒ ፡ አም ላክ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልድ" ፡ በከመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወመ ለኮቱ ፡ ለወልድ" ፡ እምአብ ፡ ወወልድኒ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በአብ ፡ እስመ'! ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወመለኮቱ ፡ በአቡሁ ። መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፡ አምላክ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ አምላክ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ርእስ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ወመለኮቱ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አም ላክ ፡ በአብ ፡ ወአብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻፲ወ፳ቿ ፣ ነአምን ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ አምላክ'' ፡ አብ ፡ አኃዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ወመለ ኮቱ'' ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፣ እሙንቱ"' ፡ ወልዱ ፡
44 And when you remember the Three you will not say that They have three lives and three words, but rather one only life and one only speech. In the same way, if you examine Them one by one, you will say that He is God and Lord, and if you remember Three, do not say that They are three divinities and three lords but rather one only God and one only Lord. Just as we have said regarding life and speech. For the Father is God and the Son is also God, in that He is His Son and the Son is like His Father. The divinity of the Son is from the Father, and the Son is God in the Father, because if He is not the Son of God He is not God, and His divinity is in His Father. The Holy Spirit is also God, because He is the Spirit of God the Father, in that every spirit is like the head, that is, the spirit and the divinity of the Holy Spirit is from the Father. And the Holy Spirit is God in the Father, and the Father is one only God as the 318 said: “We believe in one only God, the Father who holds everything,” and the divinity of the Son and of the Holy Spirit are in Him, because They are His Son and His Spirit. The Son is also one only Lord, since it is necessary that the Lord reign. One in fact is the Lord over his riches, as the owner of the riches has been defined as “lord,” and the master of the house as “lord.”

### On the lordship of the Son
ወመንፈሱ ፡ ወወልድኒ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ይደ ልዎ ፡ ይንግሥ" ፣ እስመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ባዕለ!" ፡ ንዋይ ፡ ወእግዚእ ፡ ባዕለ!' ፡ ቤት ። ወብእሲሰ ፡ እግዚእ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ኩሎሙ" ፣ አግብርተ ፣ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ በአድኅኖተ ፡ አዳም ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወተሣየጦሙ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ” ፡ በደሙ ፣ ወሕማማቲሁ ፡ በከመ ፣ አነ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፣ እከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ በትርጓሜ ፣ ምክንያተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወስቅለቱ ። ወወልድሰ ፡ በአማን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእ ፡፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘተሣ የጦሙ ፣ በደሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አሐደ ፡፣ እግዚእ ፣
45 Man is in fact lord over all riches and all the sons of Adam became slaves of the devil (who) reigned over them for the salvation of Adam their father to him, and the Only-Begotten Son purchased them from him with His blood and His sufferings, as I am about to manifest to you in the explanation of the reason for the Incarnation of the Son of God and of His crucifixion. The Son is truly the Lord of all men because it is He who purchased them with His blood (Acts 20:28) and they became His servants and He is


[p. 202] {#po.56.p202}
ወአቡሁ ፣ እግዚእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ በአ ማን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ወአብ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ እግዚእ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ! ፡ ዘተሣየጠ" ። ወአብ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ነአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብር ፡ ዘለወልደ' ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ገብር ፣ ለአቡሁ ። ፣እስመ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወነዋዩ ፡፣ ለአቡሁ" ።
The only Lord and His Father is Lord because He is the Father of the Lord. The Son is truly Lord; the Father is Lord in Him, and the Son is Lord because it is He who has purchased. And the Father is the Lord in Him. We know in fact that he who is a slave to the son of a man is also a slave to his father, because the son is the property of his father.

### On the Holy Spirit
ወአብ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ በወልድ ፡ በከመ ፡ ወልድ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በአብ ። ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እግዚእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለወልድ ፣ እግዚእ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚእ" ፡ በወልድ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ አሐዱ ፡ እግዚእ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻፲ ወቿ ፣ ነአምን ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ እግ ዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ወመለኮተ ፣ አብ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወመን ፈሱ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕይ ወት ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለመለኮታዊት ፡ ወእግዚአዊት ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወለወልድ ፡ ወአብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አም ላክ ፡ ወእግዚእ ፣ ቦሙ ። ወበከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀዋሚ' ፡ ወነባቢ ፡ ቦሙ ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ ወማሕየዊያን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ኩሉ" ፡ ግብር ፡ ወማሕየዊሆሙ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሠረጸ" ፡ እምአብ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ፣ወውእቱ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወለወልድ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ርእሶሙ!" ፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ወሀቤ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘሕያው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈ ቅድ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ማኅየዊ ፡ ዋሕድ ።!'! በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ፻፻፲ወቿ ፡ ወ፻ወ8፡'፤ ፡ ወነአምን ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚእ ፣ ማኅየዊ ፡ ዘሠረጸ ፡ እምአብ ፣ ወአብ'' ፡ ወወልድኒ ፡ ወሀብያነ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙ ንቱ ፡ ቀዊሞቱ ፡ ወነቢብ”' ፡ ወሎቱ ፡፣ ሰጊድ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወስብሐት ።'፤
46 The Father is Lord in the Son as the Son is God in the Father. And the Holy Spirit is Lord because He is the Spirit of the Son who is Lord, and the Holy Spirit is Lord with the Son, and the Son is the only Lord. As the 318 said: “We believe in one Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the only God.” And the divinity of the Father and of the Holy Spirit is in Him because They are His Father and His Spirit, and the Holy Spirit is the only life-giver, because life belongs to Him, just as divine and lordly (life) belongs to the Father and the Son. And the Father and the Son are alive in Him, just as He is also God and Lord in Them. As He is stable and speaking in Them, so too are They living and life-giving in Him, and He is the life of every work and vivifies them insofar as it is He who proceeds from the Father to the Son, and He is the life of the Father and the Son, their head, and He is the giver of life to every living being according to His will, and He is the only life-giver. As the 318 and the 150 said: “And we believe in the Holy Spirit, the Lord, the Life-giver who proceeded from the Father,” and the Father and the Son are givers of life through Him, because They are His subsistence and His utterance; to Him be adoration together with Them and glory. As the 150 said: “Him we adore and glorify together with the Father and the Son.” He is the one who speaks through the prophets.

47 He dwells in all the faithful who are in all the earth; He dwells in all in Christian baptism, in the Eucharist, and in the priesthood in all the churches because He is one Spirit who descends upon every Christian baptism, Eucharist, and priesthood. And all the churches and all the faithful become one single church, the assembly of the apostles. It is indeed the church of the holy apostles (Eph 2:20) who have gathered from all peoples (Col 3:11) and have sanctified it with the one Holy Spirit and with the one baptism for the forgiveness of sins and have promised to

48 As the 150 said after their profession in the Holy Spirit, they said: “One holy church, assembly of the apostles, and we profess one baptism for the forgiveness of sins and we hope for the resurrection of the dead and the life eternal that is to come, amen.” They added this to their faith in the Holy Spirit because the Holy Spirit is one Holy Spirit. And He dwells upon all the faithful and sanctifies them all, and makes them all one. Men and women, slaves and free, the circumcised and the uncircumcised (Gal 3:28), and He makes them all holy with one sanctification, and makes them all brothers and children of the one heavenly God. 49 And one is the mother, which is the baptism that they have received through the one Holy Spirit. They are all one body of Christ, but they are separated among themselves in their body, according to the separation of the bodily members among them (1 Cor 12). The hand is separated from the foot and the eye is separated from the ear, but all are gathered into the one body. Thus the faithful are separated among themselves and are gathered into the one Spirit of Christ, holy and one, who dwells upon all, and the body of Christ which is united in all, and all are one. But if the members of the one body of Christ are separated among themselves, each of them is a member of the other, and for all there is one covenant that they will rise from the dead as Christ has risen. The Holy Spirit as the author of the resurrection from death

ለኩሎሙ ፡ ትውልድ' ፡ ዘይመጽእ ። ወየሐይው ፡ በሕይወተ ፡ አብ! ፣ ወወልድ ፣ ዘኢይመውት ፡ ወይነግሥ ፣ በመንግሥቶሙ ፡ ዘኢየኀልቅ ፡ ወይ ሄልው!' ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ፣የውስተ ፣ መንግሥት" ፡ ዘኢየኃልፍ" ። ወይትፌ ሥሑ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፣ በትፍስሕቶሙ ፡ ዘኢያስተርኢ ፡ ለዐይን ፡፣ የወኢሰ ምዐ ፡ ቦቱ' ፡ እዝን ፡ ወዘኢተኀለየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘአስተዳ ለወ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለፍቁራኒሁ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ እለ ፡ ፍሉ ጣን ፡፣ በልሳናቲሆሙ ፣ ወዘመዶሙ ፡ ወነገዶሙ ፣ ሥጋዊያን ፡ እስመ ፡ ጥም ቀት፤ ፡ አግዓዘቶሙ ፣ እምኩሉ ፡ ምግባራት ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ወረሰየቶሙ ፡ ለኩ ሎሙ ፡፣ አሐደ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፣
of the Son, He will be the life of all generations to come. And they will live in the life of the Father and the Son that does not die (1 Jn 2:17) and they will reign in their kingdom that does not end. They will be with Them and will rejoice with Them in Their joy (1 Pt 1:8) which does not appear to the eye, which the ear has not heard, which was not conceived in the heart of men. (They will enjoy) what God has prepared for His beloved (Is 52:15; 64:3; 1 Cor 2:9), among all the Christians who are separated as to their languages, races, and carnal tribes. In fact, baptism has freed them from all the works of the flesh and has made them all one in Christ (Rom 6:1-11).

### Many members called to collaborate in one body
ኢይፈድፍድ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ላዕለ" ፡ ካልኡ ፡ ወኢየሐጽጽ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መለያልያት ፡ ፣አሐዱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወኩ ሎሙ ፣ መለያልያት" ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ካህን ፡ ወሕዝባዊ ፡ ይጽሕቁ ፡ በበ ይናቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሕቁ ፡ መለያልያት ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይግበሩ ፡ ሣህለ ፡ ወሠናየ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፣ ይገብሩ ፡ መለያልያት ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዐይን ፡ እንተ ፣ ተወቅባ ፡ ለእግር ፡ ሶበ ፣ ትሬኢ ፡ በፍኖት'! ፡ ዘየሐስማ ፡ ኢትኀድጋ ፡ ታንሶሱ ፡፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ትገብር ፡ እድ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ "በዘ ፡ ኢይክል" ፡ ይብላዕ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ኢይወዲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፍ ፡ ዘየሐስም ፡ መሪረ ፡ አው ፡ መጺጸ ፡ አው ፡ ግዙፈ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ" ፣ መለያልያት"፤ ፣ ይገብሩ ፣ ሣህለ ፡ ወሠናየ ፡ ወተዓቅቦ ፡ በበይ
51 None of them will prevail over his neighbor, nor will he be less, because all the members are one flesh of Christ, and all the members are among themselves. The priest and the layman need one another, just as the members have needed one another. All are bound to exercise mercy and kindness among themselves just as the members do among themselves. Like the eye that protects the foot, when it sees on the road what harms it, it does not let it move over it. The same does the hand toward the person regarding what he cannot eat. Thus the tongue does not put into the mouth what harms, the bitter or the vinegar or what is coarse. In fact, all the members exercise mercy, kindness, and mutual protection.

### The fate of the refractory members
ናቲሆሙ ። ወመሃይምናንሰ'"' ፡ ዘኢይከውን'' ፡ ከምዝ ፡ ምስለ” ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መሃይ ምናን ፡ ወዘኢይገብር”' ፡ ሣህሉ” ፡ ወኢያሜኒ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ኃይሌ" ፣ በከመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፣!ኃይሉ ፡ ወክሂሎቱ”" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አጥፍአ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ወሥ ጋሁ” ፣ ለክርስቶስ ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በቀሩዔት ፣ ወየኃድጎ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይገድፎ ፡፣ ወኢይሠሀሎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይማስን ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ እመልያልያተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወኢይክል ፡ ይግበር” ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘያጽሕቆ ። እመሂ ፡ ዐይን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተዐውር ፡ እምርእይ” ፡ ዘያጽ ሕቅ ፡ ላቲ ። አው ፡ እድ ፡ ሶበ ፡ የብሰት ፡ ወኢትገሥሥ ፡ ወኢትጸውር ፣፡ ወኢታነብር ፡ ዘያጽሕቅ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ትግበሮ ። አው ፡ እግር ፡ ዘኢታንሶሱ ፡ አው ፡ ልሳን ፡ ዘኢይትናገር ፡ ወባዕዳን ፡ እምነ ፡ መልያልያት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይማ ስኑ ፡ ወኢይክሉ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘያጽሕቆ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወውእ
52 And the faithful who are not so with all the faithful, who do not exercise mercy and do not do good to them with all their strength and according to the capacity of their power and ability, these [have destroyed the soul and the flesh of Christ, since these will be without any utility and Christ will leave them and abandon them and will not have mercy on them; just as a man when he destroys one of the members of his body cannot do the work he needs. If the eye blinds itself from seeing what it needs; or if the hand withers and does not touch, does not carry, and does not put down what needs to be done; or,

ቱሰ ፡ ይገድፎ ፡ ወኢይሣሀሎ ፡ ወኢየሐዝን ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፣ እመሂ ፡ ድኅነ ፡ አው ፣ ማሰነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢያተርፍ፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዮዔተ ፡ ጥቱ ፡ ከማሁ፤ ፡ ይከውን ፡፣ መሃይምን ፣ ዘኢያኒ ፣ ወኢይሣሀል ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፡ መሃይምናን" ፡ በከመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ክሂሎቱ ። ወኢያሌብዎሙ'" ፣ ዘይበቀ“ፆሙ ፡፣ እምግባረ" ፡ ምድር ፡ ወሰማይ ፡ ይገድፎ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኢ ይሣሃሎ ፡፣ ለውእቱ ። ወሶበ ፣ ይገብር ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ እኩየ ፡ ለካልእ ፡፣ መሃይምን ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡፣ መሃይምናን ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መለያልያቲሁ ፣ ውስተ ፡፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡፣ ወክርስቶስኒ ፣ ይመትሮ' ፣ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ወእ ምጸጋሁ ፡ ወእምሕይወት ፡ ወእመንግሥት ፡ ዘተካየዶሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ።
if the foot does not move or if the tongue does not speak, or if other members are broken and do not perform the action that this man needs, he abandons them. And he has neither pity nor pain toward it, (and is indifferent) whether it heals or perishes, because it does not procure him any advantage. Thus will be the faithful who does not do good and does not have pity toward all his faithful brothers, according to the measure of his capacity, and does not make them understand what is useful to them among the actions of the earth and of heaven. Christ abandons him and will not have pity on him. If the faithful does evil to another faithful among all the faithful who are members of the body of Christ, Christ will also cut him from his body, from his grace, from the life, from the kingdom that He has promised to the faithful.
በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ መለያልያት ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይከ ውን ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ ወይፈርህ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መለያልይ ፡ የሐስም ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፣ ወይጤይቅ'' ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ኢተ ርፈ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ይመትሮ ፡ ወኢይምሕኮ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እመ ለያልያተ ፣ ጠባይዓቱ'! ። ከማሁ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ ዘየሐስም'' ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መለያልያተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለእመ ፡ የንስሐ ፡ ኢይትኔስሕ!' ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወኢይኩን" ፡ በቋዔ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ሕሱም ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ይመትሮ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወእምኪዳኑ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወክቡር ፡ ወይወድዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዲያ ብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ። እስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኢረሰዮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መለያልይ ፡ ጠባይዓዊ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረሰዮ ፣፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡፣ በምግባራቲሁ'' ፣ ሠኖናያን" ፣ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ የሐስሞሙ ፣ ለአብያጺሁ ፡ መለያልያት ፡ ወኢተርፈ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በቀሩዔት ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ያነብሮ'' ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወኢይምሕኮ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አላ ፡ ይመትሮ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኢይምሕክ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ለመቲረ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እመለ ፡
53 As when a disease happens inside one of the members of the human body and that man fears that that member will damage another (member) of the body and ensures that no cure remains for it, then he cuts it and has no compassion for it even if it is of the nature of the members. Likewise (will happen to) every faithful who damages one among all the faithful who are members of his body, if he does not do penance quickly and if he will not have repaired the damages. Then Christ will cut him from his holy body and from his holy and glorious covenant and will throw him into the eternal fire, together with the devil and his army (Mt 25:41), because Christ did not associate him with his body as if he were a natural member, but He assumed him into his body for his good actions. If then he damages the members who are his companions and no utility (at all) remains from him, Christ does not need to place him in his body; Christ will not have pity on him but will cut him, as a man will not have pity to cut one of his natural members if it is harmful to one of the members.

### Exhortation
ያልያቲሁ ፡ ጠባይዓዊት ፡ ቦበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሕሱሙ”" ፡ ለአሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ መለያልያት” ። ወፍቅርሰ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፣ መሃይምናን ፡፣ ወጻማ ፡ በእንተ ፡ በቀሩዔት ፡ በበይናቲነ ፡ በከመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ክሂሎትነ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ተኃድር” ፡ ውስቴ ትነ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወንከውን”" ፣ ጽኑዓነ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወነሐዩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ፣ወንነግሥ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰመዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዮኖናውያን ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ዘይኖናዝዝ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘየኃድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡
54 As for love, it is for all the faithful, and the labor is for the utility among us according to the measure of our capacity. Therefore, inside us dwells the Holy Spirit who is the Spirit of Christ, and we become firm in the body of Christ and we will live with Him and will reign with Him until eternity (Rom 8:11). In fact, the Holy Spirit is a comforter as God called Him in the holy Gospel, because in the language of the Yonanites the meaning of Paraclete is “he who


[p. 210] {#po.56.p210}
መሃይምናን፤' ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይናዝዞጮሙ፤ ፡ ወይኃይሎሙ'" ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምግባ ራት ፣ ሥጋውያን ፣ እለ ፡ ይትቃረኑ ፡፣ ለመንፈስ" ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመውእ ዎሙ ፣ ወይገብሩ ፣ ምግባራተ ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይወርሱ ፡ ባቲ ፣፡ መንግሥቶ ፣ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘይሄሉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ።
consoles,” and it is He who dwells inside all the faithful, and it is He who consoles them and makes them (stronger) than the carnal actions that oppose the Spirit (Rom 7:23), until they defeat them and perform spiritual actions through which they inherit the kingdom of Christ that will last forever (Mt 25:34).

### Conclusion of the present homily and anticipation of the following one
ናሁ ፡ ከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘሰአልከ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ጥያቄ ፣ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተዋሕዶቶሙ ። ወናሁ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ እከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወስቅለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅረብ ፡ ለዘያነብቦ ፡ ወይሌብዎ ። ወእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ያብርህ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልብከ ፡ በአእምሮቱ ። ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ አኩ ቴት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ወልዕልና ፡ ወዕበይ ፡ ወገሪር ፡ ወሰጊድ ፣ ወአድንኖ'" ፣ እምይእዜ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ተፈጸመ ፡ ድርሳን ፡ ቀዳ ማይ ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ለሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተዋሕዶቶሙ ፡ በሰላመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ አሜን" ።
55 I have therefore illustrated to you in this book what you asked about the three Hypostases of God and their unity. Now I am about to manifest to you the reason for the humanization of the Son of God and His crucifixion so that it may approach the one who reads it and he may understand it. And may God illuminate the eyes of your heart with His knowledge. To Him be glory, praise, honor, excellence and greatness, submission, worship, and salvation from now until the ages of ages, amen. The first homily is finished, the explanation of the triple hypostasis of God and of Their unity in the peace of God the Father, Amen.

### HOMILY The Proem
ድርሳን ፡፣ ዳግማይ ።


[p. 212] {#po.56.p212}

1 The second homily is on the manifestation of the humanization and crucifixion of the Son of God. O beloved brother, may God illuminate the eyes of your mind with the grace of the Holy Spirit the Paraclete, so that you may understand the mystery of His divinity about which you already asked me to write for you a book, in which I would explain to you about the three Hypostases of God and about their unity; the reason for the humanization of the Son of God and His crucifixion. I have answered what you asked and I wrote you a book in which I illustrated to you about the three Hypostases of God and about their unity. I declared to you that God has three hypostases, without separation, nor change, and I assured you from the word of God for eternity in His essence in itself. In fact, our Lord Jesus Christ in the holy Gospel says: “There is no one who knows the Son except the Father and there is no one who knows the Father except the Son and he to whom the Son has willed to reveal it” (Mt 11:27).
ዘየአምሮ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወለዘፈቀደ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ይከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ። ኖሁ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ አእምሮቶ ፡ ወአእምሮርተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወመንፈስ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወለአበው ፡ ንጹ ሐን ፡ ወለመምህራነ ፣ ዘቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን'' ፣ ቅድስት ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ ከሠቱ ፡ ዝክተ ፡፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በጥም ቀት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወአዕይንቲሆሙኒ" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ብሩሃት" ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይሌ ብው ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘኀደረ ፡ ውስቴትሙ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ የሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ" ፡ እስመ ፣ መኑሂ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበል ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃል ፣ ዘይወፅእ ፡ ኀበ'' ፣ ልሳን ፡ እምልበ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ሥጋዊት ፡ ነባቢት" ። ኢይሌብው ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡
2 Behold, the Son has willed and revealed to the prophets and to the holy apostles, to the pure fathers and to the teachers of the holy Church, that they should know Him, that they should know His Father and the Holy Spirit. And they have manifested that to all the faithful who have the Holy Spirit through holy baptism and their eyes are illuminated by it and they understand this through the Holy Spirit who has dwelt in them. As the apostle Paul says: “For there is no one who can say that Jesus is Lord without the Holy Spirit” (1 Cor 12:3). And He is like the word that goes out toward the tongue, from the mind of the carnal, rational spirit. They do not understand its meaning without the flesh in which there is a rational, speaking spirit like the spirit from which the word went out.
ነባቢት ፡ በከመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ቃል ። ወአብድንተ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ አራዊት ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦ” ፡ ውስቴ ቶሙ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ነባቢት ፣ ፣ኢይሌብው ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘይወ ፅእ ፡፣ እመንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ነባቢት” ፡ እስመ ፡ ቦሙ ፡፣ እዝነ" ፡ ለሰሚዕ ።
3 And the bodies of animals and of all the beasts that do not have inside them a rational and speaking soul do not understand the meaning of the word that goes out from the rational and speaking spirit because they have ears to hear.


[p. 214] {#po.56.p214}
ወከማሁ ፡ አብድንት ፡ እለ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ መኖፍስት ፡ ለባዊያን ። ወከ ማሁ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢተጠመቁ፤ ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢይሌብው ፡ ቃለ ፡ ለዘመንፈሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮኑ: ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወቦሙ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ወነባቢት ። አሳላ" ፣ ለእመ ፡ ኢኮነ' ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘው ስተ' ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጠመቁ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ኢይሌብው ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ኢይሌብው ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፣
It is the same for the bodies inside each of which there are rational spirits. It is the same for those who have not been baptized, inside whom there is not the Spirit of the holy God; they do not understand the word of the Spirit of God, the holy one. And even if there are people who have a rational and speaking spirit, but if there is not inside them the Spirit of God, the holy one, who is inside all the baptized, they will not have understood the word of God, just as animals do not understand the word of man because the Spirit of God is not inside them.
ውስቴቶሙ ፣፡ መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወከማሁ ፣ ይቤ ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፣ እስመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘነፍስ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ' ፡ ኢይትዌከፍ ፡ ዘመንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ እበድ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ አልቦቱ ፡ መን ፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ውስተ" ፡ ዘይነብብ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወበ ከመ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወእለ ፡ ተጠምቁሰ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወስቴቶሙ ፣ መንፈሱ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፣ ኢይሌብው ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ኃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ አእምሮቶሙ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወአልቦ" ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ፣ዕሩቃን ፡ እመንፈሰ'' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አላ ፡ እሙንቱሰ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ዘኢይሌቡ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንእሱ" ፡ ወአኮ ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ ፅሩቅ፤' ፡ እመንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ነባቢት ፡ አላ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ንእሱ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ሕፃን ፡ ወኢይሌቡ ፡ ዝክተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይልሕቅ ፡ በትም
4 And so the apostle Paul said regarding them: “For the man who has a soul (but) who does not have inside him the Holy Spirit does not receive what is of the Spirit of God because to him, it is foolishness” (1 Cor 2:14), because he does not have inside him the Spirit of God, in the same way as the one who reasons through It, and like the man who speaks to those who do not have the spirit of man. Those, however, who have been baptized, since in all of them there is the Spirit of God, if among them there are (those) who do not understand the meaning of the Word of God quickly because of their scarce knowledge of it, they are not (only) empty of the Spirit of God but are like a child who because of his youth does not understand the meaning of human speech; but he is not empty of the rational and speaking spirit, rather he does not understand that because of his youth because he is a child. But if he grows with instruction and knowledge, little (by little) he will understand.
ህርት ፡ ወአእምሮ" ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ይሌቡ ፣፡ ከማሁ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ መሃይምናን ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፣ መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እምጊዜ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ አላ ፡ ያጽሕቆሙ ፡ ለት ምህርተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልማድ" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኅዳጠ'' ፡ ኅዳጠ” ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ይሌብውዎ ፡ መንፈሰ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኀደረ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፣ ወይትዌከፍዎ ፣ ወይትአመንዎ ፡ ወየአምሩ ፡ ፍካሬሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይሌቡ”" ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይትመሀርሮ"' ፡ ወይለምድ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወይትዌከፎ ፣ ወይትአመኖ ፡ በመንፈሰ ፣ ሰብእ ፡፣ ዘው"ስቴቱ” ። ወለአበውሰ ፡ ወሐዋርያት” ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ምሥጢሮ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ዘኅቡእ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ከሠትዎ ፡ ለመሃይም
5 So that all the faithful have in them the Spirit of the holy God from the time of Christian baptism but they need the teaching of the Word of God, and the gradual exercise in it. Then they will understand the Spirit of God that has dwelt in them and will welcome Him, will trust in Him, and will know His explanation as a child understands the explanation of human speech when he learns it and exercises himself in it, and welcomes it, trusts in it through the human spirit that is in it. As for the fathers and the apostles and the prophets, the Son manifested to them the mystery of God that is hidden (Rom 16:25) in the Holy Spirit, and they revealed it to the faithful who have in them the Holy Spirit.
