in knowledge and to the learned among all the faithful who have the Spirit of God. And now I manifest to you in this book, with the help of the Holy Spirit, the reason for the humanization of the Son of God and His crucifixion, and I repeat the memory of His explanation twice. In the first place, I will refer in it His explanation (with) a small number of words, so that His explanation may reach quickly those scarce in understanding and knowledge so that the word may not be lengthened for them and they may not forget His explanation. In the second place, I will recall the number of His explanation with a distribution, with a long word so that he who has verified its explanation through few words may understand. And he said that in the beginning God made the heaven and the earth (Gen 1:1).

### Creation of the angels


[p. 216] {#po.56.p216}
እምቅድመ ፡፣ ይግበር ፡ ወኢመነሂ ፡ በዲበ" ፡ ምድር ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ለመላ እክት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡፣ መኖፍስት" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አብድንት ። ፈጠ ሮሙ ፣ ሠራዊተ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ወለለ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ጽርሕ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ የኃድሩ"' ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ "ወለለ ፡ ሥርዐት'፤! ፡ ሊቅ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ጽርሕ" ፡ እንተ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሥርዓታተ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሳጥናኤል ። ወመሳላእክትሰ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መና ፍስት ፣ የሐይው ፣ በስብሐቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበቅዳሴሁ"' ፡ በከመ ፡ የሐይዉ ፡ አብድንት ፡ በመብልፅዕ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡
7 Before He had created anyone on earth, He created the angels in heaven, spirits without body (Jub 2:2). He created them armies and armies, and for each army there was a pavilion which is the order in which they dwell, and for each order there was a leader (1 En 69:3). And the leader of the armies in the pavilion that is above all the orders was the angel whose name is Satan. And since the angels are spirits, they live through the glorification of God and His sanctification as bodies live through food (1 En 41:7; 69:24). When God created them, all glorified Him and sanctified Him with their natures.

### Fall of Satan and his accomplice angels

8 And when Satan saw the superiority of his rank, he became proud and thought that he was the likeness of God and did not glorify Him and did not sanctify Him. He made all the armies of his rank desist and forbade them the glorification, and God made them all fall to earth together with him, and they were called Saytanat. And God created our father Adam, spirit, as an angel in animal flesh to set him in the order from which Satan and his army fell. And again He created for him the paradise of happiness (Gen 2:15) to the east of the earth under the heaven and left him in it until He would create in His image, according to the number of the armies that fell together with the devil and so that He would make him ascend and set him in that rank that became empty of the glorification and sanctification.

### Creation of man. Envy of the devil

And when the devil saw them, he knew that they had been created because of his rank; he became jealous of them (two) and said to himself: “As for me, I became proud and I likened myself to God and He made me fall. And if these become proud and will liken themselves to Him like me, He will make them fall with His justice as He made me fall. 10 And immediately he plotted against them until they sinned like him, with their will, without (suffering) oppression, and he hid himself from them (inserting himself) into a serpent, and by the mouth of the serpent he said to them: “Why did God forbid you so that you do not eat from that tree?” (Gen 3:1). And Eve said to him: “So that we do not die!” (Gen 3:3). And the devil said to her: “You will not die if you eat from it, but God forbade you the eating from it because He knew that if you eat you will be gods according to His likeness” (Gen 3:5). Then they believed him and rendered God a liar and wanted to become gods and ate of it so that they would become according to the likeness of God, as the devil became proud and likened himself to God when he abandoned the glorification and the sanctification.

mated, had children from one another, became many like the animals. And at the moment of their fall, the devil feared that they would not repent before God and He would forgive them and remit to them (their fault). Because the devil became the cause of their error and in each of them, from his armies, he made an unclean spirit because they blinded them with carnal actions so that they would not look toward God and would not convert to Him. And they did so in all those who were born from the seed of Adam as he made upon Adam an unclean spirit from his armies, together with the man, forcing him to perform carnal, animal actions, from the day of his birth, until the day in which God decides his death. Final confrontation between the devil and humans. Redemption of humans with equity and justice 12 That unclean spirit appears to him with his frightening face and dries up his blood and his body dies. He grabs his spirit and makes it descend into Sheol under the earth. Thus all the children of Adam lived for 5,500 years because they have sinned like the devil and deserve condemnation for eternity like him and it is not possible that the condemnation be removed from them, and he leaves the devil the dominion. If with him (God) He had acted without justice, for this reason He would have caused justice to fail in their salvation. And the other aspect of His justice that made Him desist from saving them is because the devil did not take them from God with violence but with a pretext, therefore it is not possible that God take them from him with violence and do him a wrong.

14 This came to pass with the good pleasure of the Son and with his will (Jn 10:18), as I manifested to you in the previous book, because the will of the Father and of the Son is one, and one is their good pleasure and their work. The example of this is like the Son who said to his Father: “O my Father, your judgment upon Adam and his seed belongs to you, an eternal condemnation, because they have sinned like the devil and his hosts. And behold, I myself have consented to redeem them for the sake of those among them who have believed in me and have done my will, from the seed of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. Look, O my Father, all that belongs to your justice upon them—from beatings and condemnations, scourging and toil, death and Sheol—lay it upon me, and I will restore it to you for them in one day, because the condemnation of all of them unto eternity is not equal to my condemnation in their favor for one hour.”

በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ የአሐቲ ፡ ሰዓት' ። በከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምሳሌ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፈቃድ ፡ ወሥምረተ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወ ልድ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አድኅኖተ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ ፡ እስመ ፣ ፈቃዶጮ፡ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ወሥምረቶሙ ፡ አሐዱ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተሠገወ ፡ ወልድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ በከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እምአቡሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ "ልደተ ፣ ጸዳለ"! ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ፣እምገጹ ፡ ዘኅቡር'! ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ዘኢይሴ ስል ፡ በከመ ፡ ኅብረቱ ፡ ለጸዳል ፡ በገጸ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡።
15 According to this example was the will and the good pleasure of the Father and of the Son concerning the salvation of Adam and his seed. Indeed, one is their will and one is their consent; therefore the Son became incarnate, because He is the light, like God his Father. He was born from his Father like the birth of a sunbeam from its face, which is always united to it, which does not depart—like the union of the ray with the face of the sun—(nor is) light found without a body.

16 When (the Son) consented to take upon himself, in favor of Adam and his seed, the condemnation that was due to them in the flesh, then, just as the devil hid himself from them in the serpent and plotted against them to take possession of them without violence, so the Son of God hid his divinity from the devil inside human flesh and did everything that a man performs, so much so that the devil thought that He was truly only a man. And it never happened that

መለኮቶ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘይገብር' ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ድካመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅባእ ፡ መለኮቶ ፡ እምዲያብሎስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ይትረከብ ፡ በውስተ ፡ "ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዘይቤ፤ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወያጠይቅ ፡ ቦቱ፤' ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አም ላክ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ያፈጥን ፡ ወይትናገር ፡ ቃለ ፡ በድካም" ፡ ከመ ፣ ይኅባእ ፣ መለኮቶ' ፡ እምሰይጣን ። ወጊዜ ፡ ዘይገብር ፣ ግብረ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቅ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ መለኮቶ ፡ ወፍጡነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ግብረ ፡ በድካም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅባእ ፣ ዝክተ ፡ እምሰይጣን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቅ ፡ ሰይ።ጣን ፡ ከመ ፡ ውአ”ቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ድኩም ።
He performed (an act of) power to confirm his divinity to men: rather, he would then perform weakness to hide his divinity from the devil. And this is found in the Holy Gospel when he would say a word and through it confirm that He was God, he would immediately hasten to pronounce a word in weakness to hide his divinity from Satan. When then he performed an action to confirm his divinity with it, he would quickly make a gesture in weakness to hide that from Satan, so that Satan would be convinced that He was a weak man.

17 Satan became angry at the multitude of his miracles and said within himself: “If this one does not obey me and does not sin like all the sons of Adam so that he becomes a slave to me, and I kill him and make him descend into Sheol, because it belongs to me to reign over him for the fact that he is from the seed of Adam.” Satan thought, in fact, that Christ was born in the concupiscence of Adam, and this is because our Lord hid from him his incarnation from the Virgin Mary, without the seed of a man; he hid that from him when he caused his mother to be betrothed to Joseph. And when Satan saw her pregnant, it seemed to him that it was from Joseph. Angered by the many miracles of our Lord, but seeing him not obeying him and not committing sin, he said within his spirit: “This one is from the seed of Adam, and when I kill him I will make him descend into Sheol to Adam his father, and I will rest from him as I did with all the prophets and saints whom I killed before him.”

All this (He) endured by his own will, because of the condemnation that is due toward Adam and his seed for their transgression, like the devil, for eternity. God. And (Adam) became empty of his grace when he ate from the tree, and (Christ) was crucified on the tree, naked in place of Adam's nakedness.

### The devil's struggle against the Son, until the last blood
ወሶበ ፡ ነጸሮ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ስቁለ ፡ ዕራቆ፤' ፡ ኀለየ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ድኩም ። በጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ፣ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ በራእዩ ፡፣ መፍርህ" ፡ ወጸሊም ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ያፈርሆ ። እስከ ፣ ይየብስ ፣ ደሙ ፣ ወይመውት ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወያወርዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይገ ብሩ ፣ ሠራዊቱ ፡ በደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፣ ወመጠወ ፡ መንፈሰ' ፡ ትስብእቱ" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ይየብስ ፡ ደሙ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ባያውርድ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል" ፣ ወአኃዞ ፡ እግ ዚእ ፡፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ በኃይለ ፣ መለኮቱ ። ወአሰሮ ፡ ለእንተ'' ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትል ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ቅትለቱ" ፡ ወማ ህረኮ ፣ ለአዳም ፣ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ዘርኡ፤' ፡ እሞቅሐ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡፣ ግፍዕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍድየተ ፡ ቅትለቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡
19 When the devil saw him hanging naked, the devil thought that He was a weak man; he approached him and appeared to him with his terrifying and black appearance to frighten him, so that his blood would dry up and his body would die and he would make him descend into Sheol just as his hosts used to do with the sons of Adam. And Christ died immediately by his own will and offered the spirit of his incarnation without his blood drying up. And the devil wanted to make his soul descend into Sheol, and the Lord Christ seized him with the power of his divinity. He chained him who came to kill his body. Christ killed him in place of his killing and captured Adam and all his progeny from his captivity with justice without violence as a nemesis for his killing, just as the devil took them without violence. Completion of the number of the sons of Adam
ነሥኦሙ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ግፍዕ ። ወኢኮኑ" ፣ አዳም ፣ ወኩሉ" ፣ ዘርኡ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልቆሙ ፡ ለሠራዊት ፡ እለ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ የምስለ ፡ ዲያብሎስ" ። ወበእንተዝ ፣፡ ኢተክህለ'' ፡ ከመ ፣ ያንሥእ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ እምውታን ፡፣ ወያዕርጎሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መናፍስ ቲሆሙ" ፣ ኀበ ፣ ሥርዐት ፡ ልዕልት ፡ እም ፣ ቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ጉል ቁሙ ፡፣፡ ለእለ ፡ ወድቁ ፡፣ እምኔሃ ። ወወሰደ ፡ መናፍስቶሙ ፡፣ እስከ” ፡ ገነት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ኮኑ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወኀደጎሙ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይከውኑ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልቁ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ እለ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በሥጋሁ” ፣ እምውታን ፡ በሣልስት ፣ ፅለት ፣ እምስቅለቱ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ፅለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ አረቦን” ፡ ለትንሣኤ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡፣ ሶበ ፣ ይትፈጸም” ፡ ጉልቁሙ ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፣ እለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ፣እምነ ፡ አይሁድ” ፣ ወአጠየቆሙ ፡ በዝንቱ” ፡ ምሥ ጢር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ስቅለቱ ፡፣ ኀብኦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያእምሮ” ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ፡ ወአጠየቆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፣ውእቱ ፡ የኃሥሥ"' ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ጉልቄሙ ፣
20 Adam and all his progeny did not reach the number of the hosts that fell together with the devil. For this reason, it was not possible that he would raise their bodies from the dead and make them ascend with their spirits to the high degree before the number of those who fell from it was completed. He took their spirits to the paradise where Adam and Eve were at the beginning and left them in it until they had become according to the number of the hosts that fell together with the devil. And He rose in his flesh from the dead on the third day from his crucifixion; it is the first day, a pledge of the resurrection of their flesh when their number will be completed. He appeared to his disciples among the Jews who had believed in him and confirmed to them this mystery that he kept hidden before his crucifixion because the devil would not recognize it, and he confirmed to them that He aims at the completion of the number of the sons of Adam.
እምደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ። ወአዘዞሙ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወይስብኩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ በዝንቱ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡፣ ሰማያዊ" ፣ ዘአስተዳለዎ'፤ ፡ ሎሙ ፣፡ ወይዜንውዎሙ ፡ ቦቱ ።
And he ordered them to go to all the peoples and preach to them this heavenly kingdom that he prepared for them and announce it to them.

### Christian baptism
ወዘያፈቅር፤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወይጸልእ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ኃላፍያተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ያበውእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወይጸልዩ፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ጸሎተ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወይስአልዎ ፡ ለአብ ፡ በስመ" ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰድድ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፣መንፈሰ ፡ ርኩሰ' ፡ ዘረሰየ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፣ ሥጋዊ ። ወዐዲ ፡ ይጸልዩ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወይስአልዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈኑ ፡ ላዕሌሁ፤! ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘወረደ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ይወርድ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ማይ ፡ ያጥምቅዎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሠለስተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡፣ በስመ"! ፣ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወየኃድር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወይሰድድ"' ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ወያብልዕዎ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ አረቦነ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ዘየሐዩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ በመንግሥት" ፡
21 Whoever will love this and will hate the transitory works forever, let them bring him into the church and pray over him the prayer of Christian baptism, and ask the Father in the name of his Son Jesus Christ so that the impure spirit that the devil had placed inside him on the day of his bodily birth may be cast out from him. And again, let them pray over the water and ask God to send upon it the Holy Spirit who descended upon the only-begotten Son and upon the water of the Jordan. And when the Holy Spirit descends upon the water, let them baptize him in it three times in the name of the Holy Trinity, and the Holy Spirit will dwell in him, and the impure spirit will be cast out from him; and let them make him eat the body and blood of Christ, a pledge of goodness, through which he lives in the heavenly kingdom. For He lives in the life of Christ and will reign together with him forever (Jn 6:54-57).

### On the dangers for the baptized


[p. 228] {#po.56.p228}
ሰማያዊ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የሐዩ ፡ በሕይወቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይነግሥ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወያጠይቅዎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ያጠምቅዎ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ሠራዊተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ይቀ ንኡ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፣ በእንተ ፡ መንግሥቱ" ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ዘይፈቅድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይዕርግ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወያሠነዩ" ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ሥጋዊያተ'' ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትገበር ፣ ሎሙ" ፣ እምጊዜ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ወያወድቅዎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ወድቁ ። እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ" ፡፣ ሶበ ፡ ኀደጉ ፡፣ ስብ ሐታተ ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ እምሰማይ ። ወሶበ ፡ የሀክይዎ ፡ ለመሃይምን ፡ ወያጌብርዎ ፡ በምግባራተ” ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ኀላፊ"! ፡ እምጊዜ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወይበጽሖ ፡ ሞት ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፣ወድቀ ፡ እመንግሥት ፡ ዘለዓለም” ፣ ወኮነ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዘለዓለም። ወለእመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነስሐ ፡ ወተነስሐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይእቲ” ፡፣ ድቀት ፣ አሐተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዘኀለፈቶ ፡ እም ጊዜ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ በጊዜሁ” ፡፣ ዘሥሩዕ ፡ ወበጽሖ ፡ ሞት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢኀደገ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ኢይከውን ፡ ለሠራዊተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሥልጣን ፡ ወኢይክሉ ፡ ይቅረቡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መንፈሱ ።
22 And when they baptize him, let them confirm to him that the hosts of the devil will envy him for his heavenly kingdom that he desires to ascend to, and they make the actions of the flesh pleasant to him so that he submits to them (subtracting) from the time of the glorification of God and his sanctification, and they make him fall as they (themselves) fell. Because they fell from heaven when they abandoned the glorifications and the sanctification. And when they make the believer lazy and submit him to the transitory actions of the flesh (subtracting) from the time of the glorification and the sanctification, and death reaches him, then he will have fallen from the eternal kingdom and will be with them in eternal condemnation. But if he repents and does penance because of this fall for which he has missed the canonical time of glorification and sanctification for once, and death comes upon him without him having neglected the time of glorification and sanctification, the hosts of the devil will have no power over him and will not be able to approach his spirit.

### Destiny of the faithful believer: intermediate eschatology
አላ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘረሰዮ' ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ይዕ ቀቦ ፡ እምዕለተ ፡ ዘተጠምቀ ፡ በልደት ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ መንፈሶ" ፡ በስ ብሐት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ዘየሐውር ፡ ባቲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወትከውን ፡ ምስለ ፡ መኖፍስት ፡ እለ ፡ አዕረጎሙ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ እምሲኦል ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጠምቁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይመውቱ ፡ በንስሐ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በነፍሶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ትፍስሕት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይከውኑ ፣ በከመ ፣ ጭልቁፋሙ ፡ ለሠራዊት ፡ እለ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ይመይጥ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ መናፍስ ቲሆሙ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወያነሥኦሙ ፡ ፣፲፣እምነ ፡ ምውታን' ፡ ወያዐ ርጎሙ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሥርዐት ፡፣ ልዕልት ፣ መንግሥት ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ዘእምኔሃ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወይነ ግሥ ፣ ምስለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ በትስብ እቱ ፡ ዘነሥአ' ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ መንግሥት" ፣ ዘኢየኀልቅ ፡ ወትፍ
23 But the holy angel of God whom Christ has placed to protect him, from the day in which he was baptized with a new birth, takes his spirit with the glory and honor with which he goes to paradise, and he will be together with the spirits that Christ made ascend from Sheol. And all those who have been baptized, if they die in penance, will go with their soul into the paradise of delight until they are according to the number of the hosts that fell together with the devil. God restores their spirits to their bodies and makes them rise from the dead (Ez 37:1-14) and makes them ascend to the exalted degree, to the heavenly kingdom above all the angels from which the devil and his hosts fell, and they will reign together with Christ in the place that is within it, in his incarnation that he took from them, in the kingdom that does not end, in the delight that does not pass, and the life without death.

### The reason for Satan's grudge against humans
ሥሕት ፡ ዘኢየኃልፍ ፡ ወሕይወት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሞት ። ወበእንተ ፣ ውእቱ" ፣ መንግሥት ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ተሰቅለ ፡ እግዚእነ" ፡ ኢየ ሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጦሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወበእንቲአሁ ፡ ይቀ ንኡ ፡ ላዕሌነ'' ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወይጸብኡነ ፡ ፣ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ" ፡ ወየሀክዩነ'' ፡ በምግባረ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ እምጊዜ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ይብጽሐነ ፡ ሞት ፡ በውእቱ ፡፣ ጊዜ ፣፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ንስሐ ፡ ወንወድቅ ፡ እም ውእቱ ፡፣ መንግሥት ፡ በከመ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወንከውን ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤለነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተዐቅቡ ፡ ወይኤዝዘነ” ፣ ከመ ፡፣ ኢንስማፅ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ የሀክዩነ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ እም ጊዜያት ፡ ስብሐታተ ፡ ወጸሎታተ ፡ ወኢንጐንዲ ፡ እምንስሐ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ኢነአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ሞትነ ፡ ይከውን ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እምቅ
24 By reason of this great kingdom our Lord Jesus Christ was crucified: so that he might lead humans back to him, and for this reason the hosts of Satan are envious of us and fight us night and day. They make us lazy through the works of the flesh (subtracting) from the time of the glorification and the sanctification, so that if death reaches us in that time, before penance, we fall from that kingdom as they fell and we will be with them in eternal condemnation. For this reason, God said to us: “Be careful,” and commands us not to listen to them when they make us lazy in the time between the times of praises and prayers. Let us not delay penance because we do not know how our death will be then, before we do penance.

25 And our Lord in the Gospel said: “This only know and confirm! If the master of the house knew the time in which the thief comes, he would have watched and would not have let his house be dug into. Thus be awake at all times, because at the time you have not known or thought, the bridegroom will come to you” (Mt 24:43-44). He confirmed to us that death is like the thief; man does not know when it comes to him. And if he becomes lazy from the glorification of God and his sanctification once, and death comes upon him in

26 Things similar to this I wrote to you in few words in which I have enumerated the explanation to you, so that you may read it to those of little understanding and knowledge, in quiet without agitation or interruption, and you will repeat it to them up to this place many times until it enters into their knowledge and they understand it in their minds, because they are like children who suck milk because of their small capacity to eat strong food that the perfect eat (1 Cor 3:2). As for me, now I begin to repeat the explanation to you and I will divide it for you according to its order with the help of the Holy Spirit. Be attentive to what I remind you: in it the intelligent, the teachers, the knowers of the holy scriptures will be healed, and this is like this fact that I reminded you of; it is the mystery of God that is hidden from every creature, from angels and from humans. Details on the “hidden mystery”
ሔር ፡ ዘሀሎ" ፣ ኅቡእ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ እመላእክት ፡ ወሰብእ ። በከመ ፡፣ ይቤ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ በመልእክቱ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ኤፌ ሶን ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘእቴሐት ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወሀበኒ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፣፡ እምሀርክሙ ፡፣ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘአልቦ ፡ አሠረ" ፣ ወአብርሀ ፡ ለኩሉ ። ምንት ፡ ሥርዐቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡፣ ምሥጢር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ እምፍ ጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኩሎ ፈጠረ ። ከመ ፡ ይእዜ ፣ ይትዓወቅ ፡፣ ለቀደምት ፡ ወለመኳንንት ፡ እለ ፡ በሰማያት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ በጥበቡ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንተ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ሕበሪሃ'' ። ወዘሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ ሥርዐትሰ ፡ ዘከሥትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠርያዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኖሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕማም ፡፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወስቅለቱ ። ወይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅቡእ ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ መላእ ክት ፡ ሰማያውያን ፡ ኢየአምርዎ ፡ እስከ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎሙ ፣ እምውሉደ ፣
27 As the apostle Paul says in his letter to the Ephesians, he said: “To me who am the humblest of all the saints he gave his grace so that I might teach the nations the wealth of Christ which has no trace, and he illuminated everyone” (Eph 3:8-9). What is “the plan of this mystery that was hidden from the creation of the world with God who created everything, so that now it may be made known to the principalities and powers that are in the heavens, through the church, in the multiform wisdom of God?” (Eph 3:9). As for what he designated as “the plan of this mystery,” it is the plan that I have revealed to you now, (the plan) that God arranged to save you humans with justice, without suffering, namely the incarnation of Christ and his crucifixion. And he said that this is hidden with God and the heavenly archangels do not know it until it was manifested to them by the sons of the Church to whom he gave the wisdom of God through the Holy Spirit.

He confirmed that the wisdom of this mystery was hidden from all the fathers, and to the prophets it was not revealed in the same way with which Christ revealed (it) to his holy apostles and to the pure disciples, sons of the church whom he named authentic prophets for their knowledge of this mystery that was revealed to them by the Holy Spirit, and for Him they were more honored than the prophets and the fathers who were before them.

29 As our Lord and our God and our Savior Jesus Christ says: “Because many prophets and kings longed to see what you see but did not see, and to hear what you hear and did not hear. As for you, blessed are your eyes that saw and your ears that heard” (Mt 13:16-17). And also Paul the apostle, again on this mystery, in his letter to the Colossians says: “I rejoice for my sufferings for you and I complete the deficiency of the suffering of Christ in my flesh, in favor of his body which is the Church of God. They are the community of believers for which I have been appointed, minister of the institution of God that he gave me in your favor so that I might fulfill the word of God, the mystery that is hidden before the world and without man having been created. But now he has manifested it to his saints, those to whom God wanted to unveil the wealth of his glory through this counsel of his” (Col 1:24-27).

30 Great is indeed this mystery, and it was hidden before the creation of the world and the generations of the prophets, of the saints, and of the angels. And the reason for which he hid it, as we have reminded, is because He hid it from Satan and his hosts, just as that one (Satan) hid himself from Adam and Eve in the serpent. And Paul, the apostle, illustrates this to us again, in his letter to the Corinthians, when he says: “Wisdom we pronounce to the wise, and it is not the wisdom of this world that we pronounce to them, nor the wisdom of the princes of this world who pass away. We pronounce rather the wisdom of God hidden and concealed before the creation of the world that God arranged in our honor. The rulers of this world do not know it, because if they had known the Lord of

፣ለእግዚአ ፡ ስብሐት፤ ፡ እም ፡ ኢሰቀልዎ፤ ። አጠየቀ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ዝየ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ ሠናይ፤ ፡ ዘዘከርኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰመዮ ፣ ለሥርዐት ፡ ዘነገርኩከ ፡ እምትስብእቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ' ፡ ወስቅለቱ ፡ ጥበቡ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። "ወዘንተ ፡፣ ሥርዐተ ፡ ሠርርዐ' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምቅድመ ፡፣ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቤዝዎሙ ፡፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ በወ ልዱ ፡ ወያዕርጎሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ሠርዐ ፡ ዘንተ" ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም' ፡ ለክ
glory, they would not have crucified him” (1 Cor 2:6-8). The apostle here has confirmed all the good that I reminded you of, namely that he called the institution that I described to you the wisdom of God, the incarnation of Christ and his crucifixion. And this institution God arranged before the world was created to redeem all the sons of Adam, through his Son, and to make them ascend to his glory. For this reason the apostle says: “Because He arranged this in our honor, before the world was created.” The devil and his troops excluded from access to the “mystery”
ብረ ፡ ዚአነ ፣ ወአጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ "መኳንንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኢየአምሩ" ፡ ዘንተ ፡፣ ምሥጢረ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀብአ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ በፍትሕ'' ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥኦሙ ፡ ለሰ ብእ ፣ እምኔሆሙ፤! ፡ በፍትሕ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ሶበሰኬ ፡ አእመሩ ፣ ጥበበ ፣ ዘሠርዐ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እም ፡ ኢሰቀልዎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ፡ ሰብሐት ፡ እስመ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ሶበሰ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተሠገወ ፡ ወተ ሰብአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅባእ ፡ መለኮቶ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ይትኀበል" ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ ይቅትሎ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ቅትለቱ"' ፡ እም ፡ ኢሰቀሎ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ አላ ፡ ኀለየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡
31 He confirmed that the devil and his army, the rulers of this world, do not know this mystery because God has hidden this mystery from them with justice, to snatch humans from them, with justice. Therefore he said: “If they had known the wisdom that God had arranged, they would not have crucified the Lord of glory.” Because if the devil had known that He had become incarnate and humanized to hide his divinity from him, so much so that he presumed to kill him, he would never have crucified him, so that He might snatch humans from him in ransom for his killing, but he thought that he was a weak man.

32 And if someone doubts and says that the rulers of this world are not the devil and his hosts, the holy Gospel removes his doubt because it says that many times in the Gospel he calls the devil “prince of this world” (Jn 12:31; 14:30). We will remember this in its time, because the apostle Paul in his letter often reveals to us that the demons are the rulers of this world. In fact, he in his letter to the Ephesians says: “Therefore stand firm in God and in the firmness of his power; put on the armor of God so that you can resist the machinations of Satan: in fact, your battle is not with flesh and blood but with the princes of darkness, the malignant demons that are under the heaven” (Eph 6:10-12). This word has revealed openly concerning them, that they are rulers and chiefs of this dark world. In fact, they have reigned there when they reigned over Adam and his lineage for their obedience to him. This mystery, hidden from the superiors and the inferiors, God has manifested to those who believe in Christ
ለእለ ፡ የአምኑ፤ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፣መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ የሐትት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረ" ፡ ወየአምር ፡ መዐምቅቲሁ፤ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡
in the Holy Spirit, “because the Holy Spirit probes every thing and knows the depths of God” (1 Cor 2:10) as the apostle says.

### Hermeneutics on the silence of Genesis regarding the creation of the angels

33 But what we have mentioned regarding the creation of the angels, the fall of the devil and his hosts, God did not remind Moses of in the Book of Creation because of the weakness of the minds of the sons of Israel and their great love for the worship of creatures. He knew that if he had reminded them of the creation of the angels and their degrees and they had heard his word: “After this let us make man in our image, in our likeness and our face” (Gen 1:26). And he said again: “Behold, Adam has become like one of us” (Gen 3:22). And he said again: “Come, let us descend, let us divide the languages” (Gen 11:7), they would have thought that He had said these words to the angels and they would have made them creators together with them and they would have betrayed the divinity of the Son and of the Holy Spirit when their mystery was manifested. And they would have said that there was no one else to whom he addressed these words except to the angels whom we have reminded that He created before that.

34 For this reason God omitted the mentioning of the angels and did not mention that in the heavens there were with him any creatures, so that they would not share the worship with him and because they would not attribute to him words that are not worthy of his Son nor of his Holy Spirit. And thus he said by the mouth of the prophet Hosea: “I created the hosts of heaven and I do not proclaim to you who they are, O Israel, because you do not incline toward them in worship” (Hos 13:4). For this reason God omitted the memory of the creation of the angels and did not write it in the Book of Creation so that the sons of Israel would not incline toward their worship. Because they would not attribute to them words that belong to his Son and to the Holy Spirit. And the memory of their creation was hidden just like the fall of the devil and his hosts until the Lord Jesus Christ revealed it in his holy Gospel. And the holy fathers revealed it in the Holy Spirit and confirmed it with the Torah and the Prophets, with many certain witnesses. And it happened thus that “in the beginning God made the heaven and the earth” (Gen 1:1), as Moses wrote in the Book of Creation. What he called heaven is the first (work) that he made, namely, the exalted heaven in which there are the angels. And immediately he created the orders in which there are the angels.

### Creation of the four natural elements
ወሶበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ወማየ' ፡ ወነፋሰ ፣ ወእሳተ ፡ አርባዕቱ ፡ ጠባይዐት! ፡ እንተ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ዘሕያው ። ፈጠራ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ስጥምተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢታስ ተርኢ ፡ ወኢኮነት ፡ ድሉተ ፡ ለተወክፎ ፡ ዘርእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነት፤ ፣ ቀላየ ፣ ማይ ፡ ኩለንታሃ ፡ ስውርት ፡ በማይ ፡ እመልዕልት ፣፡ ወእመትሕት ፡ ወእም ኩሉ ፡ ገበዋቲሃ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፣ በመዝሙር ፡ ዘ፻፻ቱ ፡ ሣረራ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ወአጽንዓ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ኢታንቀልቅል ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወቀላይኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ልብስ ፡ ዐጽፋ ፡ ወነፋስኒ ፡ የዐውድ ፡ በማይ ፡ በኩሉ ፣ ገበዋቲሃ ፡ በከመ ፡፣ የዐውድ ፡ ማይ ፡ በምድር። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሙሴ ፣ ነቢይ ፡ ወጽልመትሰ' ፡ ኮነት ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ በአርአያሁ ፡፣ በእንተ ፣ ብዝኃ ፡፣ ጽልመቱ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ አሳተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የዐውድ ፣ ባበነፋስ ፡ በከመ ፡፣ የዐውድ ። ነፋስ ፣ በማይ ። ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ለሠ ለስቱ ፡ ጠባይዐት ፡ ካልአን ፡ ወእመልዕልቶሙ ፣ ወእመትሕቶመጮ፡፥ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ለይኩን፤' ፡ ብርሃን" ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡
35 When then he made the heaven, immediately he created the earth, the water, the wind, the fire, the four natures from which he made everything that is living. He created the earth immersed in the water, not visible and not ready to receive the seed because it was an abyss of water, totally hidden by the water, from above, from below, and from all its parts. As David the Prophet said in Psalm 103: “He founded the earth and made it firm so that it would not shake for ages of ages, and also the abyss is like a garment that covers it; and also the wind circulates in the water in all its sides as the water flows in the earth” (Ps 103:5-6). For this, the prophet Moses said: “And the darkness was over the summits of the abyss” (Gen 1:2) because the wind is of dark appearance because of the intensity of its darkness. And again he created the fire while it circulates in the wind, as the wind circulates in the water. He created the other three natures and above them and below them he created the fire when he said: “Let there be light!” (Gen 1:3). He created immediately with his Word which is his Son, all the kinds of light and the fire from the species of light.

### Creation of the angels according to their degrees


[p. 240] {#po.56.p240}
ወልዱ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ አዝማዳተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወእሳት ፡ እምዘመደ ፡ ብርሃነን" ። ወበጊዜሃ ፣ 'ፈጠሮሙ ፣ ለመላእክት'፤' ፡ በመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ። ፈጠሮሙ ፡ እምብርሃን ፡ መንፈሳውያነ'' ፡፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ በመንፈስ" ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወበወልዱ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ ወአርባዕተ ፡ ጠባይዐተ ፣ ወበመንፈስ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ መላእክት ፡ መንፈሳውያን ። በከመ ፡፣ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ"' ፡ በመዝሙር ፡ ዘ ወ፪" ፡ በቃለ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወበመንፈሰ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ኃይሎሙ'' ። አጠየቀ ፣ ከመ ፡ ለሠራዊተ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወሰማ
36 And at that juncture he created the angels with the Holy Spirit. He created them from the light, spiritual, without flesh because He created them with the Holy Spirit. And through his Son he created the heavens and the four natures, and through the Holy Spirit he created all the spiritual angels. As David the prophet said in Psalm 32: “By the word of God the heavens were created and by the spirit of his mouth their powers” (Ps 32:6). He confirmed that God created the hosts with the Holy Spirit and also the heavens with his Word which is his Son, living together with Him.

### The hosts of the angels
ያትኒ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘህልው ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ፈጠሮሙ ፣ ለመሳእክት ፡ ሠራዊተ" ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ወለለ ፣ ሠራዊት ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ልዕልት ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ቀዳሚ" ፣ ፍጥረት ። የወለለ ፡ ሠራዊት” ፡ "በከመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ሥርዐቱ" ፡ ወለለ ፡ ሠራዊት” ፡ ሊቅ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወጥልቆሙ ፡ ለሠራዊታት ፡ ምእት ፡ ሠራዊት ።” በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ” ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ብወንገሉ ፡ ቅዱስ” ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ምእት ፡ አባግዕ ፡ ፲እንዘ ፡ ሀለውኩ” ፡ እዘክር ፡ ውእተ ፡ በግዜሁ ። እስመ ፡ ሰብእሰ ፡ ኢየአምሩ ፣ እምሠራዊት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ 8"' ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ዘሰመይዎሙ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ፣ ወመኳንንት ፣ ወሥልጣናት ፡ ወመናብርት ፡ ወአጋእዝት ፡ ወኃይላት ፡ ወኪ ሩቤል ፡ ወሱራፌል ። ወእግዚኦሙሰ ፡ ለነቢያት ፣ ፈጣሬ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ይቤ ፡
37 He created the angels, hosts of hosts, and for every host (he placed) a degree in the exalted heaven which is the first creature. For every host according to the dignity of its degree, and for every host there was above them a chief, and the number of the hosts was one hundred hosts. As our Lord Jesus Christ said in his Holy Gospel concerning the hundred sheep that I will remember in its time. Because men, among the hosts, do not know except nine hosts that they call Prophets. They are: the Angels, the Archangels, and the

### Principalities, Authorities, Thrones, Dominions, Powers, Cherubim, Seraphim
ከመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ምእት'፤ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ወሰብእሰ ፡ ኢያአምሩ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ፪ወ ፡ ፲: ፡ ዘወድቀ ፣ ባሕቲቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ለመሳእክት ፡ በጥ ንተ' ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዕለት፤ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፣ ሰብሕዎ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ ወቀደስዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰብሕዎ ፡ ወይቀድስዎ፤ ። ወአልቦሙ ፡ ሕይወተ ፣ ወኢትፍሥሕተ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፣ ወየሐይው ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መንፈሳዊያን ፡ ወይትፌሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ ዘለዓ ለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። በከመ ፡ የሐይው ፡ ሥጋዊያን ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዝዓለም ፣ ወይትፌስሑ ፡ በመብልዕ ፡ ኀላፊ፤ ፡ ወሕይወት ፡፣ ኀላፊ' ፡ ወጸጋ ፣ ኀላፊ" ።
But the Lord of the Prophets, the Creator of the angels, said that they were one hundred hosts, even if men knew only nine of them, and the tenth which fell by itself. And when He created the angels with an initial creation of light, on the first day, which is Sunday, they all immediately glorified and sanctified Him because He had created them so that they might glorify and sanctify Him. And they have neither life nor joy except in His glorification and His sanctification, and they live by this, because they are spiritual and in it they enjoy eternal life and eternal grace together with God who lives forever. Just as carnal beings live in this world and enjoy transitory food, transitory life, and transitory grace.

### On Satan
ወኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ወሥርዐትሰ" ፡ እንተ ፡ ትትሌ ዓል ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ ሥርዐታተ"' ፡ ሳጥናኤል ። ወውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሥዩመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ምእት'፤' ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሥርዓታት ፡ ምእት" ፡ በከመ ፡ እቤለከ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ!'' ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ አቡየ ፡ ብዙኅ ፣ ማኅድር ፡ ቦቱ ፡ የያጤይቅ ፣ ትርጓሜሁ" ፡ ለምእት ፡ አባግፅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰብሕዎ" ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ ወቀድስዎ ፡ ለፈጣሪሆሙ ፡ ተፈግዐ ፡ ሳጥናኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልዕልተ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወተዐበየት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወኢሰብሐ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ" ፣ ወኢቀደሶ ፡ ከማሆሙ ፡ አላ ፡ አምሰለ'' ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቦቱ" ፡ ውስተ ፣ ልቡ ፡ ወከልኦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሠራዊት ፡ እለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥር ዐቱ ፣ ‹እምስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ” ። ወሰምዑ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፣ ወሶቤሃ ፡፣ አውደቆ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወለኪያሆሙኒ” ፡ ኩሎሙ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተአዝዞቶሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዘኢይሠምር ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ፈጣሪሆሙ ፡ ወአውደቆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፣ እስመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ድቀቶሙ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ የአምር ፡ ዘይከ ውን ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ። ወኮነ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ በትዝኅርቱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ። ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ሶበ" ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እፎ” ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ኮከበ ፡ ጽባሕ” ፡ ዘሠረቀ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ ከዋክብት ፡ እመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አነብር ፡ መንበርየ ፣ መልዕልተ ፣ ደመና" ፡ ወእከውን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡፣ ልዑል ። ሰመዮ ፡ ኮከበ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡፣ መላአኩ" ፣ ብርሃናዌ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ በጽባሕ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡
38 And Satan was the head of the exalted rank, the highest rank of all the ranks. He was placed over the exalted rank above the hundred ranks because, as I told you, all the ranks were one hundred. For this reason, our Lord said in the Gospel: “In my Father's house there are many dwelling places” (Jn 14:2), thereby confirming the explanation of the one hundred sheep. And when all the hosts glorified and sanctified their Creator, Satan, the head of the exalted rank, rejoiced as if he were above all, and his soul became proud and he did not glorify his Creator, nor did he sanctify Him as they did, but in his heart he likened himself to Him, and he forbade all the hosts that were with him in his rank from glorification and sanctification. They obeyed him, and God made him fall immediately, and all the others as well, because of their obedience to him, which did not please God their Creator. He made them fall to the earth because He had created it on account of their fall, for He knows what will happen before it happens. And he became a witness for us that the devil was the head of the angels and fell because of his arrogance toward his Creator. The prophet Isaiah says thus: “How did the morning star fall, which rose at the time of the morning, which was honored more than all the stars?” (Is 14:12-14). Otherwise, how did he say: “I will set my throne above the cloud and I will become the likeness of the Most High.” He called him “morning star” because he was a luminous angel and was created when God created the light in the morning of the first day. For this reason, he says that he rose at the time


[p. 244] {#po.56.p244}
ውእቱ ፡ ሠረቀ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀዳሚ'፤ ፡ ዘፈጠሮ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ኩሎሙ፤ ፡ መላእክት ። ወከሠተ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ሚመቱ ፣ ወምክንያተ ፡ ድቀቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ንቤ ።
of the morning because he is the first that He created and he is the head of all the angels. The prophet revealed the greatness of his position and the reason for his fall as we have said.

### Fall of Satan and his subsequent plans
ወሶበ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወዐረቀ ፡ እምብርነሃኑ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጸሊመ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፍጥረቱ ። እስመ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ተከልአ ፡ እምስብ ሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ በቀዳሚ' ፡ ዕለተ ፣ እሑድ ፡ ርእሰ ፣ ዕለታት ፡ ዘባቲ፤ ፡ ተገብረት ፡ ሰማይ፤ ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ወሥርዐታቲሃ ፡ ወመላእክ ቲሃ ፡ ወአርባዕቱ ፡ ጠባይዐት ፡ እንተ ፡ መትሕታ፥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ይቤ ፣፡ በኅሊናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ አአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ኢየኀድግ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ወደቁ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ፅርቅተ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ትትሌዓል ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፣ ሥርዐታት ። ወሀለዎሰ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመልእ" ፡ በዘይሴብሕዎ ፡ ወይቄድስዎ ፡ አው ፡ ይምሕረነ ፡ ወይመይጠነ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አው ፡ ይፈጥር ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ባዕዳነ ። ንሕነሰ ፡፣ "ንሴፎ ፡ ተመይጦተነ" ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ዕር ቅተ ፡ ወንመትር ፡ ተስፋነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይፈጥር ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ህሀየንቴነ ፡ ባዕዳነ ። ወዓዲ ፣ ኮነ ፡፣ ያጤይቅ ፡ ለእመ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይፈጥር ፡ ህየንቴሁ፤ ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይምትር ፡ ተስፋሁ ፡ ኀለየ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ሶበ ፣
39 And when he fell, he and his host, he was stripped of his light and became black at the time of his creation. For at the time when he was created, he was excluded from glorification and sanctification and immediately fell on the first day of Sunday, the head of all days, in which the exalted heaven and its ranks, its angels, and the four natures that are beneath it were created. And when he fell, he said in his thought: “I know that God will not leave empty the order from which I have fallen, and it is higher than all the orders, but it must be filled with those who praise and sanctify Him, or He will forgive us and make us return to it, or He will create others in it, but we hope for our return to it, it being empty, and we will lose our hope if He creates others in it in our place.” He also pondered (the eventuality) that God would create (someone) in his place and in the place of his host so that he would lose his hope. He thought of this when he fell on Sunday. The account of the creation continues
ወድቀ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጥነ ፡ ዕለተ'፤ ፡ ሰኑይ ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንተ" ፡ ሰማየ ፡ እንተ ፡ ታስተርኢ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እስመ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ቀላየ ፡ እምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ልፅልት ። ፈጠረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንተ" ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ወምድር ። ወኮነ ፡ ማይ ፡ እም ፡ መልዕልታ ፡ ወእምታሕታ" ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ሰማይ" ፡ ፣ፈለካት ። ወበፅለት ፣ ሣልስት'' ፡ አስተጋብአ" ፡ ለማይ ፡ ዘመትሕታ"” ፣ ውስተ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ጉባኤ ፣ ወተከሥተት ፡ ምድር ፡ "ወአስተርአየ ፡ የብስ” ። ወአብቁለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በቀልተ"' ፡ ወዕፀወ ፡ ዘይፈርዩ ፡ ወዘአልቦሙ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ወኩሎ ፣ ዘመደ” ፡ አሕማላት"' ። ወበዕለተ ፣፡ ረቡዕ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ወወርኀ ፡ ወከዋክብተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዘፈለክ”" ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ እምብርሃን ፡ ዘፈጠረ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ብርሃነ ፡ ዝርወ ፡ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ጉባኤ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ ሐሙስ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ለዓ ሣት ፡ ወለአዕዋፍ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማይ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ ፣በጽባሕ ፡ ፈጠ ሮሙ ፡ ለተሐዋስያን ፡ ወለእንስሳት" ፡ ወ"ለአራዊት ፡ እምነ ፡ ምድር ። ወሶበ ፣ ነጸሮሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለእሎንቱ ፡ ያንሶስው ፡፣ ኀለየ ፡ ከመ ፣ ውስቴ ቶሙ ፣ መንፈስ ፣ ለባዊት"' ፡ በከመ ፡፣ መላእክት ፡ ደንግፀ ፣ ወኀኅለየ ፡ ከመ ፡
40 When it was Monday, God created this heaven that is seen now, because the water was a single abyss, from the earth to the exalted heaven. God created this heaven in the depth of the abyss in the midst of the exalted heaven and the earth. There was water above it and below it, and it was the heaven of the stars. On the third day, He gathered into one place the water that was below it; the earth appeared, the dry land manifested itself. God made all the sprouts and trees that bear fruit and those that have no fruit, and all kinds of vegetables, sprout upon the earth. On Wednesday, He created the sun, the moon, and the stars in the heaven of the orbit; He created them from the light that He created on Sunday, because it was a diffused light that had no reservoir. On Thursday, He created the fish and the birds from the water, and on Friday, in the morning, He created from the earth the reptiles, the animals, and the beasts. And when Satan saw that those were moving, he thought that inside them there was a rational spirit like

እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጠረ ፡ መናፍስተ ፡፣ ይረስዮሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሥርዐቱ ፡ ወኀ ብኦሙ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ አብድንት ፡ እንስሳዊት፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ይኔጽሮሙ ፡ ወይፈትኖሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ' ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፣፡ መንፈስ ፡፣ ለባዊት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ገብረ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በከመ ፣ ኅሊናሁ ፡ ዘአብድ ፡፣ ከመ ፣ ይትመየጥ ፡ እበዱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ኀለየ ፣ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጠረ" ፡ መኖፍስተ ፡ ከመ' ፣ አምሳሉ ፡ ወአምሳለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡፣ ወኀብኦሙ፤ ፡፣ እምኔሁ ።
the angels, he was frightened and thought that God had created spirits to put them into his order and that He had hidden them from him and (placed them) in those animal bodies; he watched them and tested them to know if there was a rational spirit inside them. Immediately, God created something in accordance with his foolish mind so that his foolishness might turn against him. He thought that God had created the spirits according to his likeness and the likeness of his host and had hidden them from him.

### Creation of humans and their privileges
ወፈጠሮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአዳም ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ፡' ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡፣ ለእሎንቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ይኔጽሮሙ ፡ ወይፈትኖሙ ፣ ወገብሮ' ፡ ለአዳም ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ለባዊተ ፡ በአምሳለ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ወኀብኦ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ከመ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወደም ፡ ወዐፅም ። ፈጠሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ድያብ ሎስ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገነተ ፡ በምሥራቀ ፡ ምድር ፡ መትሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ጥንቱ ፡ በምድር ፡ ወርእሱ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወውእቱ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር" ፡ በምሥራቅ ፡ ፲ ወድ ፡ በእመት ፡፣ ወኅደጎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እስከ ፣ ይፈጥር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በከመ ፣ ኀጭልቆሙ ፡ ለሠራዊት ፡ እለ ፣ ወድቁ"' ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰይጣን'' ፣ ወየዐርጉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥርዐቱ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ እምገቦሁ ፡ ዐፅመ ፡፣ ወመልኦ ፣ መካኖ ፣ ሥጋ ፣ ገብረ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ዐፅም ፡ ሔዋንሃ" ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ወሥጋ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዳም ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብ
41 God created Adam on Friday; in it He created those whom the devil was observing and subjecting to the test. (God) created Adam as a rational spirit in the likeness of the angels and hid him in a body similar to the body of animals, flesh, blood, and bones. He created him to put him in the exalted order from which the devil fell and made for him the paradise to the east of the earth, under the heaven, its origin in the earth and its head in the heaven, because it is the gate of heaven. And it is above the mountain in the east, by fifteen cubits, and He left him inside it until He would create his peers, according to the number of the host that fell together with Satan, and they would ascend to his rank. Immediately, He took a bone from his side and filled its place with flesh. From that bone, He created Eve, spirit and flesh like Adam, because God is capable of taking from Eve a bone to create another, and from the other another (still).
ሔር ፡ ከሃሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ እምሔዋን ፡ ዐፅመ ፡ ይፍጥር" ፡ ካልእ" ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ካልኦ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፣ ይከውኑ ፣ በከመ"' ፡ ጭልቁቄሙ ፡ ለሠራ ዊተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተፈ ጥሩ ፡ ኢኮነ ፣ ሎሙ ፡ ኀፍረት ፣ ዘይትአመር ፡፣ እምኔሃ ፡ ዕድ ፡ ወአንስት" ፡ አላ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ከመ ፣ መላእክት ፡ እስመ ፣ ብርሃነ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ኮነ'' ፡ ይከድን ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፣ ፈጠራ ፡ ለሔዋን ፡ ኢገብራ ፡ ሕጽጽተ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዐፅም ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እምአዳም ፡ አላ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ ፍጽምተ ፡ አዕፅምተ ፡ ገበዋ ቲሃ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕጸት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቀነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሃሊ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ እምኔሁ" ። ውእተ ፡ ዐፅመ ፡ ዘውሱክ ፡ ይግበር ፡ እምኔሁ" ፡ ካልአ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ በገበዋቲሁ”' ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ፍጽምተ ፣ ወት ከውን ፡ ይእቲ ፣ በከመ ፡ አዳም ፣ ወይግበር" ፣ እምካልእ ፣ ካልኡ” ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሃሊ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወጥዩቅ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእሲት፤' ፡ ባቲ ፡ "ዐፅመ ፡ ገቦሃ"፤ ፡ ይትወሰክ” ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡፣ በከመ ፡ ነበቡ ፡ ጠቢባን ፡ ፈካርያን ፡ ወተሰናአው ፡ ምስለ ፡ መጽ ሐፈ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በፍካሬሆሙ ።
42 He acts in this way until they reach the number of the devil's host. Now, when Adam and Eve were created, there was no shame for them through which man and woman were recognized, but they were like the angels because the light of their face covered their body. And when He created Eve, He did not make her lacking that bone He had taken from Adam, but He made her perfect in the bones of the sides, without defect, to assure us that He is capable of taking from her that bone which is added, to make from it another (human) perfect in his sides, as He created that one (Eve) perfect, and he would become like Adam, and so that He might make from one, another (still), because He is capable in every work. This thing is certain, that even now the woman has one more rib than the man, as the wise exegetes said and agreed with the book of God in their exegesis.


[p. 248] {#po.56.p248}

### Satan in action against humans
እስመ ፣ ሰይጣንሰ ፡፣ ሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡፣ ጠይቆቶሙ፤ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እን ስሳ ፡ ጽኑዓን ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወነጸሮሙ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለሔዋን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ቅውማን ፡ ጽኑዓን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽነት፤ ፡ ወቦሙ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወነቢብ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ እንስሳ ። አእመረ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ እሙንቱ ፡ መና ፍስት ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኅቡአን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ እንስሳ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኅሊናሁ ፣ ኮነ' ፡ በደረ ፡ ዝክቱ፤' ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በርእሱ ፡ አንሰ ፡ አአ ምር" ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተሀከይኩ ፣ እስከ ፡ ይከውኑ ፡ በከመ ፣ ጭልቆሙ ፡ ለሠ ራዊትየ ፡ የዐርጉ ፡ ወይወርሱ ፣ ሥርዐትየ ፡ ወእተርፍ ፡ አነ' ፡ ውዱቀ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወአነ ፡ አአምር ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጻድቅ ፣ ወኩነኔሁ' ፡ ፍትሕ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፣ ዓመፃ ወእሎንቱ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይትዔ በዩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዚአየ ፡ ያወድቆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አውደቀኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተዐበይኩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተመሰልኩ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ እሎንቱ" ፡ በከመ ፡ ምግባርየ ፡ ያወድቆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዚአየ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ አገበርክዎሙ ፡ ለው እቱ ፡ ምግባር" ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፣ ምክንያት ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ አላ ፡ ይደልወኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትመከነይ ፡ በላዕሌሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ውእተ ፡ "በፈቃዶሙ ፡
43 For Satan, when he finished the verification of all the robust animals and reached paradise, unlike all the animals, he saw Adam and Eve in an upright position, without inclination, endowed with mind and speech. He knew immediately that they were spirits and angels hidden in the body of an animal, because he was quick to understand this. And immediately he said to himself: “As for me, I know that if I become sluggish until they reach the number of my host, they will ascend and inherit my rank and I will remain fallen forever. And I also know that God is just and His condemnation is just and that with Him there is no injustice. And if those become proud like me, He will make them fall as He made me fall when I became proud against Him and likened myself to Him. And if those behave according to my work, He will make them fall like me, but if I force them to (perform) this work, the cause will be imputable only to me. I must therefore have a pretext over them so that they do that, with their own will, without violence, nor coercion, so that the motive (of their ruin) may be imputable only to themselves.”
ዘእንበለ ፡ ግፍዕ ፡ ወኢግብርናት" ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ምክንያት ፡ በባሕቲቶሙ፤፤ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ኀብአ ፡ መንፈሶ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ወተናገ ሮሙ ፣ እምኔሃ" ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ለምንት ፡ ከልአክሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትብልዑ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዕፀው" ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ገነት ። ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅለየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዘዞሙ ፣ በትእዛዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍትን ፡ ተአዝዞቶሙ ። በከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ምስ ሌነ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወኢርኢኩ ፡፣ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ" ፡ የአዝዞሙ”፤ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትዐቀቡ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ዮጊ ፡ አዘ ዞሙ ፣ ከመ ፣ ኢይብልዑ ፡ እመቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ" ፡ ኅሊና ፡ ዘኀለየ ፡ በፍትሑ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ ዘዘከርናሁ ፣ እምቅድመ ፡
44 And immediately he hid his spirit from them inside the serpent and spoke from it, saying: “Why has God forbidden you not to eat from all the trees that are in paradise?” (Gen 3:1). He made this speech because he thought in his heart: “God must have given them an order to test their obedience, as He did with us regarding the glorification and sanctification. And I have not seen among them anything that He had commanded so that they might abstain from it, except the trees that He commanded them not to eat any of.” It is because of this thought that he took note of the justice of God and that we have already mentioned before this, that


[p. 250] {#po.56.p250}
ዝንቱ ፡ ሰመዮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ጠቢብ!' ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፣ ከይሲ ፡ ኮነት ፣ ትጠብብ' ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ አራዊት ።
the Book of Creation called God “wise,” because He said “that the serpent was the wisest among all the beasts” (Gen 3:1).

### Demonic seduction and fall
ወትቤሎ ፡ ሔዋን ፣ ኢከልአነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ንብላዕ ፡ እምኩሎሙ፡፤ ፣ ዕፀው ፡ አላ ፡ ከልአነ ፡ እምበሊዕ' ፡ እምአሐቲ"' ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ወይቤለነ ፡፣ ኢትብልዑ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወኢትቅረብዋ ፡ እስመ ፡ በዕለት ፡ ዘትበ ልዑ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ትመውቱ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ የአልቦ ፡ ትመ ውቱ፡ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ትበልዑ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ አላ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አእመረ ፡ "ከመ ፡ አንትሙ፤ ፣ ሶበ ፡ ትበልዑ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ትከውኑ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ በከመ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አፍቀረት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወበልዐት" ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ፣ወረሰየቶ ፡ ለአዳም" ፡ ይብላዕ'' ፡ ፈቂዳ'' ፡ ይኩን ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወበልዐ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዕረቆሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምጸጋ ፡ ብርሃ ናዊት ፣ በከመ ፣ አዕረቆ'' ፡ ለዲያብሎስ"'' ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወአውደቆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አውደቆ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንኖሙ ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ ከመ" ፡ ኩነ
45 And Eve said to him: “God has not forbidden us to eat from all the trees; He has rather forbidden us to eat from one single tree and has said to us: ‘Do not eat from it and do not approach it because on the day in which you eat from it, you will die the death’” (Gen 3:2-3). And Satan said to her: “You will not die if you eat from it, but God knew that if you eat from it you will become gods according to His likeness” (Gen 3:4). She immediately loved to be God and ate from it and made Adam eat from it, desiring that he become God. And he ate from it. God immediately stripped them of the shining grace, as He had stripped the devil of it, and made them fall as He had made him fall to the earth, so that (God) might condemn them with the justice corresponding to the condemnation of the devil.

### Adam and Eve receive a fair condemnation
ኔሁ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አበሱ ፡ በአምሳሉ ፡ ወተመሰሉ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በርእ ሶሙ ፡ ወሰምዑ ፡ እምከይሲ ፡ ወአምንዋ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ አማ ልክተ ። ወሐሰውዎ ፡ ለፈጣሪሆሙ ፡ ወኢኮኑ ፡ አብዳነ'' ፡ ወኢሕጹጳነ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ 'አላ ፡ ምሉአን ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ጥበብ" ። እስመ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ፍጥ ረት ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አዳም ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እን ስሳ ፣ ወአራዊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይሰምዮሙ ፡ ወሰመዮጮ፤ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፣፡ ወአስማቲሆሙ" ፡ ዘሰመዮሙ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ጽኑዓን ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓ ለም ። ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ያጠይቀነ" ፡ ከመ ፡ አዳም ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ጥበብ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጠራ ፡ ለሔዋን ፡ በከመ ፡ አዳም" ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወኢኮኑ ፡ አብዳነ ፡ አላ ፡ ጠቢባነ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይደልዎሙ”
46 They had in fact sinned like him, and they themselves likened themselves to him and listened to the serpent and believed him and desired to become gods. They denied their Creator even though they were neither foolish nor lacking in wisdom, but filled with all wisdom. The Book of Creation says in fact that God led to Adam all the animals and the beasts to see with what name he would call them; and he named them all (Gen 2:19-20). And their names with which he designated them are confirmed forever. With this, God wanted to assure us that Adam was full of all wisdom. Therefore, God became a witness that He created Eve like Adam in everything and that they were not foolish but wise. For this reason, they deserve the condemnation that the devil deserved. The reason for the entry of the serpent into Paradise
ኩነኔ ፡ ዘደለዎ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ” ። ወከይሲሰ ፡ በምንት ፡ ምክንያት ፡ ቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምጽኦ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አዳም ፡፣ ኩሎሙ” ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወከይሲ ፡ ትሜኒ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ ኀደጋ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይነጽርዋ ። ወበዝንቱ” ፡ ረከበ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያስሕቶሙ ፡ የባቲ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ እንዘ" ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፣
47 As for the serpent, for what reason did it enter paradise? This happened because God led to Adam all the animals and the serpent was the most beautiful of all, He left it with them in paradise so that they might look at it, and for this reason, Satan found a pretext to make them err through it because he had


[p. 252] {#po.56.p252}
ባቲ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ሔዋን ። ወምክንያትሰ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ዘከራ ፣ ለከይሲ ፡ በኦሪት ፣ ወኢዘከሮ ፡ ለሰይጣን፤' ፡፣ የእስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ኢዘከሮሙ ፣ ለመላእክተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈጠሮሙ ፡ ወኢድቀቶ ፡ ለሰይ ጣን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፪ቱ ፡ ፈቃድ ፡ ዘቀደምነ' ፡ ዘክሮቶሙ ።
seen that they rejoiced greatly in it and Eve even more. And the reason for which God in the Torah remembered the serpent and did not remember Satan is because He did not remember the angels when He created them, nor the fall of Satan, because of the two wills that we have mentioned previously.

### Satan chains every newborn to an evil spirit until death
ወሶበ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰይ ጣን ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ አውደቆሙ ፡ በፍትሕ፤' ፡ ወእሙ ንቱ ፡ የሀልው፤ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውዱቃነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወሲኦል ። ሶበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውዱቅ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ለለአሐሩ፤' ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ፡ ፡ መን ፈስ ፣ ርኩስ ፡ እምሠራዊቶሙ ፡ ያገብሮሙ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ምግባራት ፡ ባሰይ ጣኖዊት ፡ እንስሳዊት ። ወሶበ ፡ ወለዱ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ በልደተ ፡ ሥጋዊት ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ለለአሐዱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ፣ እምሠራዊቱ ። እምጊዜ ፣ ተወልደ!" ፡ ታገብሮ'' ፡ የለገቢረ ፡ ምግባራት"! ፡ ሥጋዊት ፣ እንስሳዊት ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አመ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሞቶ ፡ ያስ ተርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በራእዩ!'፤ ፡ ዘሕሱም ፡ ወጸሊም ፡ ወያፈርሆ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይየ ብስ ፡ ደሙ ፣ ወይመውት ። ወእምሕሰመ ፡ ራእዩ ፡ ወፈሪሆቶ ፡ ያረግዕ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሥ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወይእኅዝ ፡ መንፈሶ ፡ ወያወርዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ "ለባዊት ፡ ነባ ቢት" ፡ ኢትነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በዐራቂ"'' ፡ ደም ፡ በከመ ፡ ኢይነብር ፡ ብርሃን ፡ በፈትል ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በዐሪቀ ፡ ቅብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይየብስ ፣
48 When Adam and Eve fell to the earth, Satan knew that God had made them fall with justice and that they would be with him, fallen to the earth and into Sheol. And while he was fallen, he arranged for each of them an impure spirit from their host so that it might force them to the fulfillment of satanic, animalistic works. And when they generated children in the carnal generation, he made for each of them an impure spirit from his hosts. From the time in which he was born, he subjected him to perform carnal, animalistic works until the day in which God established his death, (the day on which he) appeared to him with his evil and dark appearance and frightened him to the point of drying up his blood, and he died. Because of the wickedness of his appearance and the fear that he inspired, he made his blood curdle in his belly. As Ezekiel the prophet said: “And he will take his spirit and will make him descend into Sheol.” Because the rational and intelligent spirit does not dwell in the flesh except in the blood that mediates, as the light does not dwell in the thread without the oil that mediates. When the blood dries up, the spirit separates from the flesh, as the light separates from the thread when the oil is exhausted.
ደም ፡ ትትፈለጥ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ እምሥጋ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይትፈለጥ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እም ፈትል ፡ ሶበ ፡ የኀልቅ ፡ ቅብእ ። ወምክንያትሰ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ምንት ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ "ደቂቀ ፣ አዳም" ፣ መኖፍስተ'' ፣ ርኩሳተ” ፡ ጣብከመ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አዳም" ፣ ወሔዋን ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘያጠሪ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ይከውኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ "ደቂቀ ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ ገብር"! ፡፣ ‹አግብርቶ ፡ ሎቱ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚእ" ፡ ላዕለ ፣ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ፡ በተአዝዞቶሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ” ፣ ዘርኦሙ ፣ ፣ወሶበ ፡ ቀተሎሙ ፡ ቀተለ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘርኦሙ”" ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡፣ ለአይሁድ” ፡ ወሶበ ፣ ፈቀዱ ፡ ቀቲሎቶ"' ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ እምአቡክሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ። አንትሙ ፣ ፍትወቶ ፡ ለአቡክሙ ፡ ትፈቅዱ ፡ ትግበሩ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ቀታሌ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምትካቱ" ።
49 The reason for which the devil arranged impure spirits over the children of Adam, as he arranged them over Adam and Eve, is because whoever acquires a slave, all the children of these become his servants. When he became the lord of Adam and Eve for their obedience to him, so he became lord of all their descendants, and when he killed them, he killed all their descendants. Thus says God regarding the Jews when they wanted to kill Him: “You are indeed from Satan your father. You want to do the will of your father, and he is a killer of the human soul from the beginning” (Jn 8:44).


[p. 254] {#po.56.p254}

### Obedience to Satan reduces humans to his slavery
ወኢይቀውም ፡ በጽድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሐሰወ ፡ በአዳም ፡ ወሔ ዋን ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፣ አንትሙ ፡፣ ትከውኑ ፣ አማልክተ ፡ ወኢትመውቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ።፤ እስመ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፣ ይቤ ፡፣ ዘይመውኦ፤ ፡ ለአሐዱ ፡ ይከውኖ ፣ ገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ አግብርት ፡ ለዘትትኤዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወበመዊኦቱ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ፡ በተአዝዞቶሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘርኦሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ" ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ በመልእክቱ፤ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሞት ፡ ኮነ ፡ በተዐ ድዎቱ ፣ ለአዳም ፣ ወነግሠ ፡ እምአዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ እለ ፣
50 He does not rule on justice because he lied to Adam and Eve, saying to them: “You will become gods and you will not die.” He became their lord when they obeyed him. In fact, Peter the apostle says: “The defeated becomes the slave of the one who has conquered him” (2 Pt 2:19b). And Paul the apostle says: “You are slaves of the one whom you obey” (Rom 6:16). By his victory over Adam and Eve who obeyed him, they became his slaves, so also their descendants forever, as Paul says in his Letter to the Romans: “For death occurred through the transgression of Adam and reigned from Adam until Moses and over those who had not sinned” (Rom 5:14).
ኢአበሱ ። ይእዜ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአተ ፡ አዳም ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይመ ውቱ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዘርኡ ፡ ወይወርዱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ። እስከ ፡ ሕፃ ናት ፡ እለ ፡ ኢአበሱ ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ወጻድቃን ፡ እምአበው ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ኢዮብ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኢይከውን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ለእመ ፣ ኮነ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ በዲበ ምድር ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ እስመ ፡ በተዐድዎቱ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይሬሲ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕፃን' ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ዘይትወለድ ፣ እምከርሠ ፣ እሙ ፣ መንፈሰ ፡ ርኩሰ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይመውት ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ያወርዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ኀበ ፡ አዳም" ፡፣ አብ" ፡ ቀዳማዊ ። ወነበሩ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ 2፻ ወ ፳፻ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ለዘይትወለድ ፡ ይሬሲ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ርኩሰ ፣ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞቱ'' ፡ ወይቀትሎ ፡ ወያወ ርዶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አበሱ ፡ በከመ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፡
51 Now, because of the sin of Adam, all those who died among all his descendants descended into Sheol, even the children who had not sinned, the prophets and the righteous among the patriarchs. As Job, the righteous, says: “For man is not without sin, even if his life on earth were but an hour” (Job 4:17?). In fact, because of the transgression of Adam, Satan used to put an impure spirit on the child at the moment in which he was born from his mother's womb. And when he dies, in that hour he makes him descend into Sheol to Adam, the primordial father. Humans lived in this way for 5,500 years. To whoever was born, he put on an impure spirit (that possessed him) until the day of his death; he killed him and made him descend into Sheol, because they had sinned like the devil and had deserved the condemnation with the devil until eternity.

### Redemption of humans with justice
ወደለዎሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ምስለ'፤ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ እስከ ፣ ለዓለም ። ወለእመ ፡ አእተተ ፡ እምላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወየኀድጎ ፡ ለዲያብ ሎስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ እም" ፡ ገብረ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ዘእ ንበለ ፡ ፍትሕ ፡ ወሐሰ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እምዝንቱ" ፡ ገጽ ፡ እምፍትሑ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይከልኦ ፡ እምአድኅኖቶሙ ። ወካልእ ፡ ገጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዲያ ብሎስ ፣ ኢነሥኦሙ ፡ በግፍዕ ፡ ፤አው ፡ በግብርናት"' ፡ አላ ፡ በምክንያቱ ። ወሶበሰ'' ፡ ነሥኦሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምኔሁ" ፡ በግፍዕ ፡ አው ፡ በግብ ርናት ፡ እምኮነ ፡፣ ዓመፃ'' ፡ ወሐሰ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰመየ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ ጻድቀ ፡ ወኩነኔሁ ፡ ፍትሕ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ነቢያት ። ወእፎ ፡ ይከውን ፣፡ ጥበብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያድኅኖሙ ፡ እምእሎንቱ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ገጽ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዐመፃ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወበኀበ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔርሰ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ይትከሃል ፣ ወውእቱኬ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ እለ ፡ ገብሩ ፣ ሥምረቶ ፡ እምዘርአ ፡ አዳም ፣
52 And if He had removed from them the condemnation and had left the devil together with his host in the condemnation, He would have acted with him without justice. But far be it from Him (that)! This aspect of the justice of God prevented Him from saving them. The other aspect is that the devil did not take them with violence, nor with coercion, but with a pretext. If God had taken them from him with violence or with coercion, it would have been an injustice to him, but far be it from Him (this), because He called Himself just and His judgment is just in all the prophets. And how will the wisdom be that has come to save them from those two aspects, without injustice? Among men there is no wisdom

on this, but with God everything is possible, namely that God (effected the salvation) through the righteous and the prophets who among the descendants of Adam have done His will and whom the devil has overcome and has placed his host upon them, they are: Abel, Seth, Enoch, Noah, Melchizedek, Abraham, Isaac, Jacob, Job, Moses, Aaron, Joshua, Samuel, David, Nathan, Gad, Elijah, Elisha, Isaiah, Jeremiah, Ezekiel, Daniel, the three youths, Ezra, Hosea, Micah, Amos, Joel, Nahum, Habakkuk, Jonah, Haggai, Obadiah, Zechariah, Zephaniah, Malachi, Ruth, Esther, Judith, Judah and his brothers and his mother and many other righteous and holy ones.

53 For them, God accepted to deliver His Son, His beloved, to the beating, to the scourging, to the cudgeling, to the death sentence to redeem in Him the condemnation that was due to them in justice like the devil, forever. They in fact were not equal to the death of His Son for them, and the condemnation of all for eternity was not equal to the condemnation of His Son in their favor for an hour. In fact, for a craftsman, his son is greater than his product, that is, if the potter shapes one hundred thousand clay pots that he has made, it will not be equal to one limb among the limbs of his Son that breaks. Therefore, the work of God, His justice together with His mercy, was manifested. And He offered His Son instead of the condemnation to be justly imputed to Adam and his descendants, because they have sinned until the end like the devil and his hosts. Again on the ransom through the hiding

hid His divinity from the devil in His humanity, as that one hid himself inside the serpent. He did all the human actions that were weak, to the point that the devil thought that He was a man like every man. He shouted because of the multiplicity of His miracles and wanted to kill Him and make Him descend into Sheol and get rid of Him like the other (people). He raised the Jews against Him and caused Him the sufferings through which He paid for Adam and for his descendants the condemnation that hung justly against them until eternity.

### Final clash between the devil and the Son

55 And when the devil arrived to frighten Him so much that His blood would dry up and He would die to take His soul and make it descend into Sheol, our Lord died by His own will without His blood drying up. And the devil demanded from Him the price of His killing, for His ransom without violence. Here in fact occurred the wisdom, before the creation of the world, as we have cited previously from the apostle Paul. Thus says again He Himself in the Gospel: “Glorify me, O my Father, with your glory that is with you before the creation of the world” (Jn 17:5). This means: the killing of the enemy, the victory over him, the manifestation of His justice, of His mercy, of His power and of His glory. Because all this is His glory and He prepared all this work before the creation of the world. Allegorical reading of the creation

the abyss and the Spirit of God hovered over the waters. And God said: ‘Let there be light’. And God separated the light from the darkness. And God called the light day, and the darkness He called night” (Gen 1:1-3, 4b-5a). Behold, God with this word has manifested that the earth is not removed from under the darkness and from the waters that are in it, namely the prophets, the righteous patriarchs from whom the darkness of the demons as masters was not removed, until His Word appeared, which is the Son, as He says. In fact, the light was not seen until God said: “Let there be light”. “God said this” means that His Word was manifested. Thus were the prophets, the righteous patriarchs, who in those days were alive like the waters and made the thirst extinguish and lived under the darkness because of the envy of the devil (Wis 2:24) and because of the enslavement to him, and they were black because of their transgression.

57 The Spirit of God went and came over them, namely, the Holy Spirit spoke inside them prophecies about the coming of Christ to save them. As for what He said: “went and came over them” (the reason) is because it is not possible for Him to settle inside them because of the transgression of Adam their father. In fact, if He had settled inside them, He would have taken them from the host of the devil with violence. This would have been an injustice. But when the Word of God came, His only-begotten Son, He became incarnate and did this work. He drove away the darkness with justice and revealed the light on the earth with the holy Gospel, the preaching of life, and with baptism, the new birth (Ti 3:5) through which He drove away from men the impure spirits, the darkness that enslaved them, it and the impure spirits. And when He drove them away from men through Christian baptism, they will have the capacity to keep the preaching of life and the Kingdom of Heaven. In fact, the meaning of Gospel in the language of the Greeks is: preaching and announcement. And He announced that therefore it was manifested that God had prepared this arrangement before the creation. Again on the fall of Adam and on his redemption

Paradise and hid himself in the midst of the trees and God called him saying: “Adam! Adam! Where are you?” (Gen 3:9). That God perhaps had a body that was with Him? In fact, the noise of the foot cannot exist without a body. Or, that perhaps God did not know where Adam was? But He, pronouncing this word, manifested to Adam that ‘from that moment there remained for you no salvation from that fall until I had become incarnate, become man, and had fulfilled the defects and the weaknesses that men fulfill’. Those actions that the Son of God fulfilled (were) so that He might hide His divinity from the devil. Behold, He revealed it to Adam at the moment of his transgression. It was in fact the Son who spoke to Adam and it is He who saved all his descendants, as He says in the Holy Gospel: “No one has ever seen God” (Jn 1:18). This means “that the only-begotten Son of the Father who is in the bosom of His Father, He has narrated Him to us” (Jn 1:18). This means that as He spoke to all, so He spoke with Adam and revealed to him what He was about to fulfill for his salvation, that He would hide His divinity from the devil in His incarnation and in His humanization. Incarnation without lust

59 And when He wanted to become incarnate and become man, He knew that He could not become incarnate from the lust of Adam because all those who were born from the lust of Adam are by their will, and without their will, slaves of the devil. If He had become incarnate from the lust of Adam, He would have been deserving of death and of remaining in Sheol with Adam His father, and when he killed Him, the cause would not have been imputable to Satan, and it would not have been required of him [the penalty on his behalf because He is a slave. And He knew that if He had become incarnate from the Virgin without lust and Satan had seen her pregnant and had known that she was pregnant without lust, he would have recognized Him immediately and would have destroyed His plan. Because if he had recognized Him, he would not have crucified Him, nor killed Him. And if he had not crucified Him, [and if he had not killed Him, it would not have been possible that (the Son) would take men from him with violence.

### Again on the hiding of the Son

60 And He employed divine wisdom so much that Satan did not recognize Him when he saw the Virgin pregnant. And this (happened) because He made the pure Virgin Mary, betrothed to Joseph. And she was with him inside his house, before He became incarnate from her. And when He became incarnate from her, the devil thought that she was pregnant by Joseph. And the knowledge of Him was hidden from him. For this fact, his heart was blinded and the hearts of the children of the Jews were blinded and they thought as he thought. They said in fact that He was the son of Joseph (Lk 4:22). But God became capable of becoming incarnate and of becoming perfect man in an instant. And He did not do this so that the devil would not know Him, but He was patient in her womb for nine months. He endured the birth, the breasts, the growth little by little, and He did every human work in the mystery that He had arranged in His wisdom for our salvation, because He is God, Son of God.

ቲተ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ በምሥጢር ፡ ዘሠርያዖ ፡ በጥበቡ ፣ በእንተ ፡ አድኅኖትነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ኢያገምሮር፤ ፡ መካን ፡ ወኢይትኅኀጣእ"፤ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ መካን ፡ በከመ ፡ ከሥትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፣ ሠለስቱ ፡ ህላዌ ፡ ወተዋ ሕዶቶሙ ። ተሠገወ ፡ እመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወእማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ "በሥጋ ፡ እንስሳዊ ። እስመ፤ ፣ ሥጋ ፣ እንስሳ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ ወዐፅም ፡ ወደም ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ነባቢት ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ኀላፊት ። ወሥጋ ፡ ሰብእሰ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወዐፅም ፡ ወደም ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ ነባቢት ፡ እንተ ፡ ተዋሐደት" ፡ በደም ፡ በከመ ፡ ይትወሐድ ፡፣ ብርሃን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅብእ ። ከማሁ ፣ ነሥአ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ሥጋ"! ፡፣ እማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ዘሎቱ" ፣፡ "መንፈስ ፣ ለባዊት ፡ ወነባቢት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዋሕድ'' ፡ ምስለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መን ፈስ ፡፣ ዘተዋሐደት ፡ በደም" ፡ ወደምኒ ፡ ተዋሐደ ፡ በሥጋ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፣ አሐዱ ፡ በመንፈስ ፣ ወበሥጋ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ ወኢውሳላጤ ፣ እስመ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ለባዊት ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ስፍሕት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነት ፡ አሐደ ፡ በአምላክ ፡ ቃል ፡ ተሰፍሐት ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ዘመልዕልት ፡ ወመ ትሕተ ፡ ዘመትሕት ። ወኮነ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወመንፈሱ ፡ ዘትስብ
61 No place circumscribes Him and He is not absent from any place, as I manifested to you in the book, the explanation of the three essences and of their unity. He became incarnate from the Holy Spirit and from Mary the Virgin in human flesh and not in animal flesh. Because animal flesh is only flesh, bones, and blood, without a rational, speaking spirit, because their blood is their life, a transitory life. Human flesh instead is flesh, bones, and blood and a rational, speaking spirit, which has united with the blood as light unites with oil. In the same way, the Son of God took from Mary the Virgin the flesh that had a rational, speaking spirit and became one with this spirit that had united with the blood, and the blood united with the flesh. And the Son of God became one in spirit and in flesh, without separation, nor mutation, because the rational spirit is an extended spirit. When He became one with the Word of God, He extended together with Him in the height of the height and in the depth of the depth. He became flesh in the earth and His spirit of humanization is one with the Word of God, extended in the height of the height and in the depth of the depth. Exhortation and further details on the Incarnation

It exposes to you the explanation of our word that the Son of God descended from heaven and became incarnate. is not limited to one place. Indeed, He occupies every place. Regarding what He said: “As for His descent,” this means that He lowered Himself and became one with the flesh and became man, because this descent is great, in that He became such that they met Him, looked at Him, touched Him, and limited Him in the body that had become one in Him. As it says in the holy Gospel: “The Word became flesh” (Jn 1:14). And when He was born, the angels preached Him to the shepherds, saying: “We announce to you a great joy that will be for all the people. For behold, today the Savior is born for you, who is Christ, the blessed Lord in the city of David” (Lk 2:10-11). The devil recognized Him immediately. And our Lord immediately hid His divinity from him, when they wrapped Him in rags and left Him in the manger.

63 And when the magi came saying: “Where is the king of the Jews who is born?” (Mt 2:2), he recognized Him again immediately, and our Lord hid His divinity from him when He fled to the land of Egypt (Mt 2:14), and thus He hid His divinity from him. He also hid Himself from all men; He did not reveal to them that He was God and prophet until He was thirty years old, like Adam at the moment he was created. Indeed, Adam was created a perfect man, like a person of thirty years. For the meaning of Adam in the Hebrew language is man. And when He was thirty years old, He was baptized by John in the river Jordan. And the Holy Spirit descended upon Him in the likeness of the appearance of a dove. And a voice came from heaven saying: “You are my Son whom I love and in whom I am well pleased” (Mt 3:16-17). The devil recognized Him again, a perfect knowledge, and immediately went out into the desert.

but these were hungry, to the point that their appearance changed, but this one was not hungry. God immediately knew his thought, (that is) that he had recognized Him because He had not been hungry.” He immediately said: “I am hungry” (Mt 4:2) as it says in the holy Gospel because He fasted, and in the end, He was hungry. And the devil dared (to confront Him) when he heard Him say: “I am hungry.” He approached Him to test Him if He was the Son of God, but God did not reveal His divinity to him in a single word, but answered him from the Law of the Torah to show him that He was a man who cared for the Law, like all those who were under the Law. He gave him power over Him, even to carry Him from one place to another, and the devil returned and doubted and said: “This is truly a man.” But regarding Him, God expressed Himself as He had previously pronounced regarding Israel. As he (Israel) is my son, my firstborn (Ex 4:22), even though he was not truly a son, but He honored him with this name.

65 The reason why the devil in person tested Christ is because when a son was born among the children of Adam, he would send one of his army so that he would be with him and subjugate him until the day of his death. When Christ was born, he sent many from his army to Him, great and small, but they could not stay with Him because of the multitude of the angels who surrounded the cave in which He was born. These are those who preached to the shepherds, as it says in the holy Gospel (Lk 2:9). Because of them, it was not possible for the devil's army to stay with Christ. The devil in person immediately came to Him, and God wanted to hide Himself from him as He had begun to do (since His conception), and He signaled to the angels and they all left. And He signaled to His mother with a sign of the thought. She wrapped Him and left Him in the manger, and with this, He hid Himself from the devil.

66 And when he saw His humiliation and His lowering, from that day on he tested Him, personally, alone. And when He was baptized and His Father revealed His divinity and the devil came to Him to test Him,
መለኮቶ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመክሮ ። ኀብአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በዘተናገርነ ፣ እምጾም ፡ ወረኀብ ። ወኀኅለየ ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ በክብር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አስተዳለወ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ወጥ በበ ፣፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ። በከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ እመጻሕፍት ፣ ቅዱሳት ፡ እምቀዳሚ ፡ ጸውያ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ወልዱ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሰ ምዮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወልዱ፤ ፡ በድኅር ፡ ኢያእመሮ ፡ ዲያብሎስ' ፡ አላ ፣ የኀሊ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወከማሁ ፡ ወሀቦሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ ሥልጣነ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ክርስ
He hid Himself from him, through the fasting and hunger of which we have already spoken. He thought of Him that He was the Son of God only in honor, in the same way as Israel. For God prepared this disposition and wisdom before the world was created. As He had prophesied in the Holy Books: in the beginning, He called Israel His son until in the end He would call Christ His Son. The devil did not recognize Him, but thought of Him that He was like Israel. In the same way, He gave the Prophets authority and they performed wonders, until Christ would perform wonders. The devil thought that He was like them.


[p. 270] {#po.56.p270}
ቶስ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ የኀሊ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ከማሆሙ ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሰመዮሙ ፣ ለነቢያት ፣ አማልክተ ፡ ወመሲሐነ' ፡ እስከ ፣ ሶበ ፣ ይሰመይ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በዝንቱ" ፡ ስም ፡ ኢያእመሮ ፡ እስመ' ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ሴት ፡ ተሰመዩ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ረሰይ ኩከ ፡ አምሳከ ፡ ለፈርዖን ። ወይቤሎሙ ፣ ለመሳፍንት ፣ በመዝሙር ፡ ዘጵ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እቤ ፡ አማልክት ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ ልዑል' ፡ ትሰ መዩ ። ወረሰዮሙ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ ወኤልሳዕ ፡ ያንሥኡ፤ ፡ ምውታነ ፡ ወአንጽ ሕዎሙ፡ቶነ ፣ ለእለ ፡ ለምጽ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወበዝንቱ ፣ ጥበብ ፡ ተኀብአ ፡ ምሥጢሩ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ እምዲያብሎስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይ።ገብር ፣ መንክራተ"! እስመ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ቦበ ፣ ኮነ ፡ ይሬእዮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ይገብር ፣ ትእምርተ ፣ የኀሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ያሜክሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ጥያቄ ፡ ዝንቱ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ የኃብእ'፤' ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ መለኮቶ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ በድካም ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ በረኀብ ፡ አው ፡ በጽምእ ። አው ፡ በድካም ፡ አው ፡ በጸሎት ፡ አው ፡ በጾም ። አው ፡ በንዋም ፡ አው ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ በድካም ፣ ይናፍቅ" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወየኀሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነቢያት ፡ እለ ፡ ፣ቀደምዎ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ የኀሊ ፡ ከመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምእሉንቱ ፡፣ እለ ።" ዘከርናሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይሰምዖ ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አው ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ኅለየ ፡ ቦቱ" ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መሲሕ ፡
67 In the same way, again, He called the Prophets “gods” and “anointed” (Ps 105:15), until Christ would be called by this name. He did not recognize Him because the children of Seth were called “sons of God” (Ps 82:6). To Moses He said: “I have made you a god to Pharaoh” (Ex 7:1). He said to the judges in Psalm 82: “As for me, I say — you are gods and you will be called sons of the Most High” (Ps 82:6). And He caused Elijah and Elisha to raise the dead (1 Kgs 17:17-23; 2 Kgs 4:18-37), they cleansed the lepers (2 Kgs 5:9-10), they performed many wonders, and with this wisdom, the mystery of Christ was hidden from the devil when He performed wonders. Because the devil, when he saw Christ perform a sign, thought that He was God and immediately tested Him to know the verification of this. And immediately our Lord hid His divinity from him through the weakness that He expressed with hunger or thirst, or with fatigue or with prayer or with fasting or with sleep, or with the word that He spoke in weakness. Then (the devil) doubted Him and thought that He was like one of the Prophets who had preceded Him. He also thought that He was like one of those we have remembered. And when he heard Him say, or it was said of Him that He was God, he thought of Him in this way, and when they said that He was the anointed, he thought that He was like Saul, or David, or others who were called by this name.
ኀለየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሳኦል ፡ አው ፡ ዳዊት ፡ አው ፡ ካልአን ፡ ዘተሰምዩ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ስም ። ወጥያቄ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢገብረ ፡ ኃይለ" ፡ ያጠ ይቅ ፡ ባቲ ፡ መለኮቶ ፣ ወኢይቤ" ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ኃለ ፡ በኀይል ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ ዘይገብር'' ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ድካም ፡ እምግባረ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዑሮ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ አእምሮቱ ። ወእሎንቱ" ፡ ምግባራት ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፣ ኮነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ የዐውር ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ዘይትቃረንዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡
68 The conclusion of this is that God did not use force to ascertain His divinity with it and never spoke a word with force, except for the human work of weakness that He then did to blind Satan through this knowledge of His. And those were the works through which Satan blinded the hearts of all those who opposed Christ and doubted Him and
