ወይናፍቁ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወይሬስይዎ ፡፣ ድኩመ ፡ የዐውሮሙ ፡ በዘተዐወረ ፡ ቦቱ ። እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ቦበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይሬእዮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ኃይለ ፡ በከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይሬእዮ ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ይገብር! ፡ ምግባረ፡ ፡ ድካም ፣ ይቤ ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ተአመን፤ ፣ አንተ፤ ፡ ከመ' ፡ 'ይጸውም ፣ አምላክ፤ ፡ አው' ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ አው ፡ ይበኪ ፡ አው ፡ ይርኅብ ፡ አው ፡ ይጸ ምእ" ፡ አው ፣ የሐምም ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ድኩመ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢገብረ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወበከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይና ፍቅ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አው ፡ ዘይሬስዮ ፡ ድኩመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አሐቲ ፣ እምእሎንቱ"' ፡ ምግባራት ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተምሀረ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምዲያ ብሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢያእመረ'! ፡ ምሥጢሮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወለብዙ
considered Him weak; He blinded them by means of that with which he himself was blinded. Because when he saw Christ performing wonders by virtue of His being God, and then saw Him performing works of weakness, he said to himself: “Do you believe that God fasts, or prays, or weeps, or feels hunger, or feels thirst, or suffers? If this one were not a weak man, He would not have done this.” Anyone who doubts the power of Christ expresses himself with words similar to these, or he who considers Him weak because of one of those works. This person has learned this from the devil who did not know the mystery of Christ, and with this, Satan has led many among the faithful into error. Know and be certain that they have learned from Satan their master and do not accept their word. The Son reveals His divinity to His disciples

69 And our Lord hid His divinity from Satan until the night of the day of His crucifixion when He gave His disciples the mysteries of His life-giving death, His flesh and His precious blood; He began to explain to them the mystery of His divinity with many and long words. As it is written in the Gospel of John about the Paraclete which in its translation is “comforter.” And when He says: “No one can come to my Father without me” (Jn 6:44). “If you know me, you have known my Father. And whoever has seen me, has seen my Father; I am in the Father and the Father is in me” (Jn 14:7, 9) and He promised them that He would send them His Holy Spirit the comforter. And what He said to them: “I will send Him to you from my Father” (Jn 15:26a), and “my Father will send Him in my name” (Jn 14:26), and “He will glorify me because He will take from what is mine and will tell you that everything that is my Father's is mine” (Jn 16:14). And His speaking with His Father (was) with a clear divine discourse, saying to Him: “Glorify me, O my Father, with your glory that is with you before the creation of the world” (Jn 17:5). And He said to Him: “Everything that is mine is yours, and everything that is yours is mine” (Jn 17:10). And His word that says: “So that all may be one, as I and you are one, you in me and I in them” (Jn 17:21). “You loved me before the world was created” (Jn 17:24).

### Satan hears the Son and is assured of His divinity

70 When Satan heard this great word, he recognized Him with a truthful knowledge that had no doubt. And immediately our Lord [performed a weakness that was not greater than Him, so much so that He hid from him the recognizing of Him because the power of the weakness that He manifested defeated the great word that He had said to him. For He spoke a divine discourse and did and said a thing of weak humanity and hid from the devil the mystery of the divine word. As it says in the Gospel: “And He began to be sorrowful and distressed and said: ‘My soul is sad even to death.’” (Mt 26:37-38). He rebuked His disciples for their sleep and asked them why they watched with Him and said: “Because the Spirit desires but the flesh is weary” (Mt 26:41).

71 And He prayed with ardor and asked that the cup of death might pass from Him, of which His disciple and beloved Peter said to Him: “Far be it from You, O Lord!”. And He became angry against him with great anger and called him “Satan” and said to him: “Get behind me, Satan!” (Mt 16:22-23) and He became angry and cursed him in this way. Because he had said to Him: “Far be it from You that You should be crucified or die.” But our Lord did not come to the earth except for that, as He said: “Because I have come for this hour. If the grain of wheat does not fall to the ground and die, it will remain alone, but if it dies it will bear much fruit” (Jn 12:27, 24). He assured us that if He had not died, He would not have saved His people from the hand of their enemies. For this reason, He became angry against Peter when he said to Him: “Far be it from You that You die.” And on that night, He asked with ardor that the cup of death might pass from Him to hide His divinity from the devil, and said: “Not according to my will but according to your will” (Mt 26:39), showing Satan how a weak man does not love death.

እምሰማይ ፡ ዘያጸንፅዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ በፍርነት'፤ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወኢኮኑ ፣ ያአምሩ ፡፣ ምክንያተ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ ምንት ፡ ውእቱ ። ወዐሶበ ፣ ጠየቀ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ድካሞ ፡ ወኖፈቀ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ አጽንዐ፤ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወአምጽአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፣ በጸሎት ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ውእተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘዘከርናሁ ፡ ወኩነ ንዎ ፡ በሞት ። ወእምድኅረ' ፡ ወደዩ ፡ ምራቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወተሳለ ቅዎ ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፡ ወመጠውዎ ፡ ለሏላጦስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወይቤሉ ፣ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወመጠዎ ፡፣ መኩንን ፡ ለሠራዊተ ፡ ቄሳር ፡፣ እግዚኡ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮም ፡
angels and they appeared to Him from heaven to strengthen Him because they had seen Him in great fear and did not know the reason for the mystery (Lk 22:43). And when the devil ascertained His weakness and doubted His divinity, he strengthened his work within his mind. He brought the high priests to Him and they took Him while He was standing in prayer pronouncing the word that we have remembered and they condemned Him to death. And after they put spit on His face, they mocked Him, struck Him, and delivered Him to Pilate, governor of Rome. And regarding Him, they said that He had said: “I am the king of the Jews” (Jn 19:21) and the governor delivered Him to the army of Caesar, his lord, king of Rome, and they scourged Him. The Son, with His passion, pays the debt of Adam

73 He paid for Adam and (for) his lineage the humiliation and the scourging that they deserved, and they mocked Him after they stripped Him of His clothes. He paid for Adam the mockery that he deserved from God because he tried to become God. And he became naked and ashamed and they mocked him as God said: “Behold, Adam has become like one of us” (Gen 3:22). And He mocked him because he deserves to be mocked forever. Christ paid for him the mockery that he deserved when they mocked our Lord. And He paid the nakedness that Adam deserved forever. And when they stripped Him of His garments, they clothed Him with the scarlet cloak instead of the skins with which He clothed Adam and Eve when they transgressed His command (Gen 3:21). They placed on His head the crown of thorns instead of the thorns and thistles that He ordered the earth to sprout for them (Gen 3:18) when they sinned. He carried the thorns on His head to show that He carried their sins, because the thorns are the sins, in that they sprouted for their sin. He was crucified naked on the wood of the cross and they pierced His hands and His feet in the midst of heaven and earth. And He was naked on the wood as Adam was naked because of the tree. They pierced His hands on the wood because of Eve who stretched her hands toward the tree and took of its fruits. They pierced His feet on the wood because of Eve who moved her feet near the tree to take of its fruits. They mocked Him again, and He was naked, crucified instead of the mockery that was due to Adam as we have already said. All this He paid in Himself, (for) the condemnation that Adam and his lineage deserve forever. Because they sinned like the devil

### Dramatic final encounter between Satan and Jesus Christ

74 And when the devil saw Him in this great weakness, he believed that He was a man and came to intimidate Him with his grim appearance, until His blood dried up and He died and to make His spirit descend into Sheol. But when he approached Him, he looked at the sun in all the earth and it became darkness (Lk 23:44-45) and he heard Him say to the thief on the right: “Today you will be with me in Paradise” (Lk 23:43). And he observed that He had seen him and did not fear and desired to flee and not approach Him because he had recognized Him, and immediately God hid His divinity from him and as if He were intimidated by him, He cried out: “My God! My God! Why have you abandoned me?” (Mt 27:46). And then He said: “I am thirsty” (Jn 19:28) because this is the habit of those who die, as one (who is at the point of death) becomes thirsty for the affliction of fear. When He pronounced these words, the devil dared against Him and made the appearance of his face grim, as his armies used to do against the descendants of Adam. And when he approached Him, he began to intimidate Him, He immediately delivered the soul of His humanity with His will without His blood drying up. Jesus captures Satan. Liberation of the deceased

75 When the devil desired to seize His soul, He seized him with the power of His divinity to kill him as a return for his killing because as I have manifested to you, the soul of Christ that separated from His body became one with His divinity, and with the power of the divinity that was one with it, He seized the devil when he wanted to seize Him. The earth shook immediately for joy, like one who dances for the destruction of the enemy. The rocks split, the tombs opened, many bodies of holy deceased rose (Mt 27:51-52). This which it says in the holy Gospel assures us that in that hour Satan was chained. From the affliction of the fear that came upon him and upon all his army, they released from their chains numerous souls of the saints and they returned to their bodies in the tombs and rose instantly.


[p. 280] {#po.56.p280}
The soul and body of Jesus during His death and resurrection.

### Reference to the Eucharist

76 And if you have heard that the soul of the humanity of Christ separated from His body, this is one in the essence of the Son. Do not think that His body became empty of His divinity because His body is from the Holy Spirit and from the Virgin Mary and He is anointed in His essence by the Holy Spirit God because the Holy Spirit is with Him: He has never separated from Him. With the firmness of the Holy Spirit, He indicated in the body after His death, in the body of Christ and in His blood that is with us, because it is the mystery of His death and is His likeness. He died for us, because He is wrapped with the cloths in the paten as He was wrapped with the shrouds in the tomb. His blood that is poured into the chalice is like what was poured on Golgotha when they pierced Him. And the Holy Spirit is with His body and His blood, is one with them in the paten and in the chalice. The divinity and humanity of the Son are inseparable
ዋዕ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፣፡ ያጠይቀነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢተፈልጠ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ወእምነፍሰ ፣ ትስብእቱ ፡ ኢ።በዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወኢበመቃብር ፡ እስመ ፡ ህላዌ ፣ መለኮቱ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ትስብእቱ ። ወህላዌሁ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢይሴስል ፡፣ ይቅብኦ" ፡ ለሥጋ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይቀብኡ ፡ ቅብአ ። ወበዝክቱ ፡ ተሰምየ ፡ መሲሕ' ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፣ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ከመ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ወህላዌ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ኢተፈልጡ ፡ እምነፍሰ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ወኢእምሥጋሁ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ሞቱ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ከልሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ አነብራ ፣ ለነፍስየ ። ዝብሂል" ፣ ከመ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወመንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። አጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ መለኮተ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐደ" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሰ'' ፡ ትስብእቱ ፣፡ ኢተፈልጠ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወህላዌ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢተፈ ልጠ ፡ እምሥጋ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ኢየብሰ ፡ ደሙ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ኢሞተ ፡ በግፍዕ ፡ አላ ፣ በፈቃዱ ። ከመ" ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ረገ ዝዎ ፣ እምድኅረ ፡፣ ሞቱ ፣ ወውኀዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወደም ፣ እምገቦሁ" ፡ ያጠ ይቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ኢየብሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢሞተ ፡ በግፍዕ ።
77 It assures us that His divinity did not separate from His body and from the soul of His humanity, neither on the wood of the cross, nor in the tomb because the essence of the divinity of the Son became one with the soul of His humanity. And the essence of the Holy Spirit does not cease to anoint the body, as one is anointed with ointment. And for that, He was called Messiah, and God in the Gospel revealed to us that His essence and the essence of the Holy Spirit did not separate from the soul of His humanity and from His body at the time of His death because He cried out saying: “O my Father, into your hand I place my soul” (Lk 23:46). This means that the hands of the Father are His Son and His Holy Spirit. He assured that the divinity of the Son became one with the soul of His humanity, it did not separate from it, nor did the essence of the Holy Spirit separate from the body. And this is that His blood did not dry up in the flesh because He did not die by compulsion but by His will. As they pierced Him, after His death water and blood flowed from His side (Jn 19:34): (this) confirms that His blood did not dry up, that He did not die with violence.

### The pierced side

78 In the same way when they pierced Him, all the blood that was in His body flowed because when they wrapped Him, Joseph and Nicodemus (Jn 19:38-40) were amazed at how death could have had power over Him. He immediately opened His eyes before them and they glorified Him with the glorification known to all Christians, that is: “Holy God! Holy Mighty! Holy Living One who does not die!”. And what follows confirms that His blood did not dry up when they pierced Him; He did not die by compulsion, but He did all this work to redeem Adam and his descendants from the condemnation they deserved. He paid for them with the binding, the beatings, the mockery, the scourging, the crucifixion, the death. Because when this one knew that He did not fear death and did not taste the bitterness of death like all the children of Adam, at the moment of His death He drank vinegar and gall instead of the bitterness of death that He could not taste, because He did not fear. And the holy Gospel says thus: “Because He, when He tasted the vinegar, said — ‘Everything that is written is accomplished’ and then He reclined His head and gave His soul” (cf. Jn 19:30). And for what reason did they pierce His side even though He was dead (Jn 19:33-34)? This means that as Eve came out of the side of Adam and he was sleeping, God opened the side of Adam and took from him a bone and from it created her, (and) from her happened the transgression of Adam and his death. In the same way, the side of Christ was opened and He was dead and water and blood flowed from Him (Jn 19:34) and from Him there was life for Adam and for all his lineage that drinks from Him. The Son defeats “the prince of this world”
ምውት ፡ ወውሕዘ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወደም ፡ ወእምኔሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሕይ ወት ፡ ለአዳም ፡፣ ወለኩሉ ፡ ዘርኡ" ፡ እለ ፡፣ ይሰትዩ" ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ'' ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ። ፣ወአልቦ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ ሥልጣነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ፡ አላ” ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡ ይመውት ፡ በፈ ቃዱ ። ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ስቅለቱ ። እስመ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሥልጣነ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ አላ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምር"! ፡ ዓለም ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ" ፡ አፈቅሮ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ይቤለኒ ፡ አቡየ” ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ እገብር ። ወሊቀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለምሰ ፡ ዝብሂል ፡ ከመ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡
79 And our Lord Jesus Christ revealed to us that the devil came to Him at the wood of the cross. And he did not have power over Him to kill Him, but He died by His own will. He said thus to His disciples the night (that preceded) the day of His crucifixion: “Because the prince of this world will come to me; he has no power over me, but so that the world may know that I love the Father and as my Father tells me, so I will do” (Jn 14:30b-31). And “the prince of this world” means that the devil has become king over all the children

of Adam; from the time of his transgression all have become his slaves, Adam and Eve and all those who have come out of the lust of Adam. And what He said: “He has no power over me,” means: “I was not born from the lust of Adam and he does not have over me (the power of) slavery and I did not obey him regarding sin, rather I die by my will to fulfill your word.” This means His will for which He wanted to redeem all the descendants of Adam in me.

80 Thus He says again: “And they will cast out the prince of this world and will expel him outside, and I when I am lifted up from the earth will draw all to me” (Jn 12:31-32). This means: “When I am crucified, I will pull away from him all the world over which he was placed, and in exchange for my death I will make them return, when he comes to kill me I will cast him out of his glory and I will make him fall as retribution on my behalf.” As David the prophet says in the second Psalm: “And the kings of the earth rose up and the princes gathered together with them against God and against His Messiah” (Ps 2:2). And in it he says: “He who sits in heaven laughs at them and God mocks them when He speaks to them in His anger and with His anger He disturbs them” (Ps 2:4-5). The prophet assured the rising of the devil and his armies who were kings and princes over all the earth and their gathering against Christ to kill Him. The rebel against Christ is the same ancient opponent of God

81 As for what he said: “Against God and against His anointed,” this means that the devil who had risen against the Son is he who before (rising against) Him, had risen against the Father. And what he said: “He who sits in heaven laughs at them and mocks them,” means that He mocks them when they gather with force to kill Him, as a man mocks the moth that wants to kill the lion. It (the moth) indeed has no power over him; it is for this that then He will speak to them in His anger and with His anger He will disturb them. And what he said: “Then,” means when they accomplish their action upon Him and their power is exhausted. He will speak to them in His anger and with His anger He will disturb them, because when the devil acted with Christ (he acted) as he used to act with the children of Adam and

killed them, but Christ died by His will and seized the devil with the power of His divinity that was one in the spirit of His humanity and spoke to him in His anger and with His anger disturbed him and bound him with justice, in retribution for his killing. 82 He made him descend into Sheol and there bound him, because his bonds are his killing, because the killing of spirits consists in their chains. And our Lord killed him in his retribution as David the prophet says in Psalm 88: “You, O Lord, have dishonored the proud as one killed and with the arm of your power you have scattered your enemies” (Ps 88:11). He assured that his killing is the humiliation deriving from his arrogance. And thus says Isaiah the prophet regarding Christ, that “He will strike the earth with the word of His mouth” (Is 11:4). This means that He struck the devil with the word of His mouth when He said: “My God! My God! Why have you abandoned me?” (Mt 27:46). Indeed with this He struck him when He hid His divinity from him. He thought that He was a man and dared to attack Him to kill Him. And immediately our Lord gave the soul of His humanity and with it killed the wicked devil. And the prophet also said that “He will kill the wicked with the spirit of His lips” (Is 11:4). Our Lord killed him as retribution for his killing. Descent of Christ into Sheol and liberation of the prisoners

83 And He captured Adam and all his descendants that the devil had captured from Paradise to Sheol. In the same way, Christ captured them from the captivity of the devil at the price of His retribution and introduced them into Paradise. And He assured the word of David the prophet who in Psalm 67 said: “God has ascended into the heights, capturing the prisoners” (Ps 68:18) and gave grace and honor to the children of man. And what he said: “He has ascended into the heights,” this means His ascent on the cross, from which He descended into Sheol and captured those whom the devil had captured, therefore he said: “He has captured the prisoners.” He gave men the honors, that is, the grace of the Paradise of delight into which He introduced them and the honor of the Kingdom of Heaven that He had promised them and the grace of the Holy Spirit that He gave to His disciples and that

all those who have believed in Him would have ascended and that they would have reached the number of the armies that fell with the devil. 84 And in the same way, Isaiah the prophet became a witness and regarding the descent of Christ into Sheol he said that “the child will introduce his hand into the hole of the serpent and into the lair of the children of vipers and they will not harm him” (Is 11:8). The “child” of whom he spoke, this is Christ who is great but lowered Himself by His will and became small for us, and also, like a child without deceit nor sin. “He will introduce his hand into the hole of the serpent.” “His hand” is the soul of His humanity. As for the “hole of the serpent,” it is Sheol which is the hole of the devil, the serpent who destroyed the ancients (Rev 12:9). He said that Christ became one with the soul of His humanity and descended into Sheol which is the hole of the devil, the malignant serpent. And what he said “the lair of his children,” these are his armies. The children of the serpents cannot harm Him, but He defeated them with the power of His divinity and brought out those who were in their captivity, Adam and all his descendants. On Baptism and Eucharist

85 He established holy baptism so that it might save those among them who have believed and makes them free from their slavery. Indeed, all those who were born from the lust of Adam, who was a slave of the devil from the time of his birth and subject to the command of the impure spirits, whether they want it or not, are slaves of the devil from the time of their birth and are under the command of the impure spirits, until He frees them with holy baptism from the impure spirit and from the slavery of the devil that he imposed upon them, just as the children of Israel were free from the slavery of Pharaoh and his army and were saved in the Red Sea. God has established for us baptism instead of the Red Sea so that we might pass through it and become free from the bitter slavery. And so that after this, we might enter the holy Church as the children of Israel entered the tent, in the desert of Mount Sinai and ate the Body of Christ, “the Bread of life that has descended from the heavens” (Jn 6:33), as those ate the manna that was called “bread of heaven” and so that we might drink the water and the blood that flowed for us from the side of Christ. “The stone rejected by the builders has become the cornerstone, stone of disbelief and stone of scandal” (1 Pt 2:7-8).

86 As those drank the water that flowed from the rock (Nm 20:11; 1 Cor 10:3) and we fight the armies of the devil, who are more powerful than us and we believe that we defeat them with the power of our Lord Jesus Christ who for us has performed wonders. We fight them when they make us lazy from the glorification of God and from thanking Him, because of the affairs of this world, and make them pleasant to us, to the point that we love them more than the glorification and the thanksgiving and we fall as they fell. It is for us a duty that we be vigilant when they make them (pleasant) to us; we do not obey them, but we say to them as God said to the devil: “Because not by bread alone does man live but by every word that comes from the mouth of God” (Dt 8:3; Mt 4:4). We immediately abandon the behavior of this world that they have made pleasant to us and we go to the glory and the thanksgiving which is a spiritual behavior. And we repeat what we have lost from the time of the sanctification and the glorification, because thus He said: “Seek first the Kingdom of God and His justice and all this will be added to you” (Mt 6:33) and His word is worthy of faith and just. If we believe in this way and fight the armies of the devil we will have inherited His heavenly kingdom, as the children of Israel fought the people of the land of Canaan and from them inherited the land of Canaan. But if we doubt and our faith diminishes that we cannot receive that kingdom and we become lazy from the glorification of God and from His continuous sanctification, we would perish as those among the children of Israel perished who doubted and said: “We cannot reign in the land of Canaan” (Nm 13:25-33). Analogy between the hundred sheep, the hundred angelic tribes and Adam

87 And our perishing was so that we might live in eternal condemnation together with the devil and his hosts, and that we might distance ourselves from the eternal kingdom which the God of angels abandoned, and He descended to serve us, He became human, and in His flesh He bore

ኀጣውኢነ ፡ ወኩነኔነ ፡ ዘይደልወነ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያገብአነ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወንንበር ፣ ውስቴቱ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በወንገል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብእሲአ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ፻ ፣ አባግዐ ፡ ወተሐጉለ ፡ አሐዱ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወኀደ ጎሙ ፡ ለ3ወ8ሀ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ይኅሥሥ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘተሐጉለ ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መትከፍቱ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትፌሣሕ ። ወ፻ ፡ አባግዕሰ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ፻ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘዘከርናሆሙ ፡ ለከ ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወአ ሐዱ ፡ ዘተሐጉለ' ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዳም ፡ ዘፈጠሮ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ፻ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሥራዕ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሥር ዓተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ፅርቅተ፤ ፡ እምዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ተሐጉለ ፡ አዳም ፡ በስሕተተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ እምዘርኡ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ አሥመ ርዎ' ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይደልዎሙ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወኀደጎሙ ፡ ለ3ወ8 ፡ ሠራዊተ ፣ መላእክተ ። ወሖረ ፡ ይኅሥሦ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ እለ ፡ አሥመርዎ ። ወጾረ ፡ ኩኀኔ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተዐድዎቶሙ ፡፣ በከመ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ጾሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡
our sins and our deserved condemnation, until He brought us to Himself, so that we might live in Him. For thus He says in the holy Gospel: “There was a man who had a hundred sheep and one of them was lost; he left the ninety-nine and went to seek that sheep which had been lost until he found it, he loaded it upon his shoulder rejoicing” (Lk 15:4-5). The hundred sheep are the hundred hosts of angels that we reminded you of before. The one of them that went astray is Adam, whom God created so that he might become the completion of the hundred, so that in him He might arrange the order that had been emptied by the devil and his hosts. Adam went astray through the seduction of the devil (Wis 2:24), but from his lineage came forth righteous ones who pleased God, who were worthy of His Kingdom. He left the ninety-nine hosts of angels and went to seek Adam and his lineage by virtue of the righteous who had pleased Him. He bore the condemnation that they would have deserved until eternity for their transgression, like the devil. He bore it upon His flesh, as He said: “For he bore it upon his shoulder rejoicing and glad for their salvation.” Analogy between the ten drachmas, the hundred angelic tribes, and Adam
ሥጋሁ ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ጾሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መትከፍቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ ወይትሐሠይ ፡ በአድኅኖቶሙ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኢያእ መረ" ፣ ከመ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ፻ ፡፣ ሠራዊት ፡፣ "አላ ፡ የኀልዩ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ፣ ሠራዊት ፡ ወአዳምኒ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ፲ ። መሰለ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ምሳሌ ፣ ይደግም ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በአምጣነ" ፡ ልቡናሆሙ ። ወይቤ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ኮነ ፣ ላቲ ፡ ፲ ፡ ጠፋልሕት" ፡ ወጠፍአ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ወአኅተ ወት ፣ ማኅቶተ ፡ ወኩስተ።ረት ፡ ቤታ ፡ ወኃሠሠቶ ፡ አስተሐሚማ ፡ እስከ ፣፡ ረከበቶ ። 8" ፣ ዘይቤ ፣ ዘኢጠፍኡ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ፪ ፡ ሥርዓታተ ፡ መላእክት ፣፡ ዘአእመሩ'' ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፡ ወጠፋልሕሰ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ዘጠፍአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ ። ወሰመዮ'! ፡ ጠፋልሐ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጠፋልሕሰ ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ይሥዕሉ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወአዳምኒ ፡ ውእቱ'' ፡ አርአ ያሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወብእሲትሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ። ወማኅቶትሰ ፡ ዘአብርሀቶ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘቀደሰ ፡ ዓለመ ፣ ወኩስተሮ” ፡ እምእኪት ፡ ወኃጣውእ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ በሕማማቲሁ ፡ ማሕየዊት ፡ ዘጾረ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፣ እስከ ፡ ይመይጦሙ ፣
88 In the same way, when God knew that man did not know that the angels were a hundred hosts but thought that they were nine hosts and Adam the tenth, He gave them another parable, through which He repeated this, according to the capacity of their minds. And He said: “There was a woman who had ten drachmas and one of them was lost; she lit the lamp, swept her house, and sought it, applying herself with care until she found it” (Lk 15:8). The nine (drachmas) that He said were not lost are the nine orders of angels whose names men have known, while the single lost drachma is Adam and his lineage. And He called it a drachma because on the drachma they draw the image of the king, and Adam is the image of God. The one He called “woman” is the mercy of God. The lamp that the woman lit is Christ, who sanctified the world and swept it of the evil and sins that belong to the children of Adam through His life-giving passion, which He took upon Himself in their favor, to make them return to the heavenly kingdom for which they were created.

(2 Cor 5:21). And what He said: “He made Himself sin” is because the motive for the death of all men is the sin of Adam their father and also the sin of those. The devil killed them by reason of those two actions. Because on account of the sin of Adam, (the devil) killed him who had not sinned, while the one who had sinned he killed for the sin of Adam and also for his own sin. As for Christ, He had not personally committed (any) sin and was not born of the lust of Adam. He did not deserve death either for Himself or for Adam, but He died for us because He took us away from the devil instead of his vengeance. In fact, he killed Him who did not deserve death, (Christ) killed him in his place and took us away from him because we were his slaves and he was our king, and his impure spirits dominated each of us from the day of his birth until the time of his baptism. Duties after baptism 90 But from him who is baptized, the impure spirit that is of the hosts of the devil departs, and the Holy Spirit dwells in him and frees his soul from the power of the devil if he always glorifies and sanctifies God for whom he was created. But if he becomes lazy even once and repents and converts and returns to glorification and sanctification, that sin will be forgiven him immediately, if he repents of it before death. In fact, penance extirpates sin for him who believes in the Son of God, while he will separate himself from him who denies the Holy Spirit, and an impure spirit will enter into him, and with him seven impure spirits that are worse than him, and they will enter into him and dwell inside him, and his end will be worse than his beginning (Mt 12:43-45). As He says in the Gospel, if he repents of it and converts warmly and believes in the Son of God,

he will receive the crown of testimony, whether they kill him or do not kill him. For if he perseveres in his penance with warmth, the Holy Spirit will return to him and with His power will drive the impure spirits from him. Even if they were thousands and thousands of thousands. And he who says that this is not possible and his penance will not be accepted, he has insulted the Holy Spirit and it will not be forgiven him (Mt 12:31), neither in this world nor in the world to come, because he has made the Holy Spirit weak, as if He were not capable of driving out impure spirits, and has doubted the mercy of God.

91 In fact, the mercy of God does not depart from him who repents, but rather from him who has apostatized voluntarily, except (in cases of) constraint, or (if) he feared death, or the sword, because this distances mercy from him. That is, if he apostatizes to escape from those who mistreat him, or for the love of offices or honor, and thinks within himself that he will repent and do penance after this, it is from him that the mercy of God departs at the moment of his apostasy. The impure spirits will strengthen themselves against him, to such a point that he will become an apostate with all his heart. Many people apostatize and sin and hope for penance. They say within themselves: “We will do penance after this and He will forgive us.” But to these will come the wrath of God and death before penance, and it will put them into eternal perdition, because they have sinned voluntarily and have trusted in penance and have forgotten the word of God: “for death is like the thief that the master of the house does not know when he will come to him” (Mt 24:43). Conclusion

Joshua, son of Nun, a precious word that confirms and causes the Christian faith to be honored, which is upright, and confirms everything that I have told you from the law of the Torah and of Joshua, son of Nun, and confirms the Word of the Lord Jesus Christ who said: “For Moses wrote of me” (Jn 5:46). And to our Lord Jesus Christ belongs glory, thanksgiving, honor, and prostration for ages of ages, Amen.

ድርሳን ፡ ሣልሳይ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ትርጓሜ ፣ ብዙኅ ፡ ነገር ፡ እምኦሪት ፣ ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ያጤይቅ፤ ፡ "ክብረ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ርትዕት፤ ። ኦእኀየ" ፣ ፍቁር! ፣ ‹እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያብርህ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልበከ' ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡ ከመ' ፡ ትለቡ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ መለ ኮቱ ። ፣ቀዳሚ ፡ ተካየድኩከ" ፡ ከመ ፡ እክሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ እመጽሐፈ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ዘያጤይቅ ፡ ሳከ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ዘከሠትክዎ"' ፡ ለከ ፡ በመጽሐፈ፤' ፡ ከሚተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወስቅ ለቱ ። እስመ ፡ አነ'' ፡ ጸሐፍኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔ ዋን ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ ወኀደጎሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ አኮ'' ፡ ይትርፉ" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርጉ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥርዐት!" ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ሰማያዊት ፣፡ እንተ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ። ወዲያብሎስኒ ፡ አው ደቆሙ ፡፣ በስሕተቱ" ፡ እስከ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራ ዊቱ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ዘርኦሙ ፡ በሕይወቶሙ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞቶሙ ፡ 'እስከ ፣ ፈነወ'' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወልዶ ፡ በኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ዘዘከርኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ትስብእቱ ። ወኀብአ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ እምዲያብሎስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኀለየ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ሮጸ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትሎ ። ወክ ርስቶስሰ" ፡ ቀተሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍዳ ፡ ዚአሁ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ አዳም ፡ እመዋቅሕቲሁ" ፡ ወሖረ ፣ ቦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አዳም ፣ ወሔዋን ፡፣ ቀዳሚ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ እምሰብእ” ፣ ዘይመውቱ ፡ በንስሐ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጥልቆሙ ፡ ለሠራዊት ፡ እለ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ የዐርጉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥት"! ፡ ሰማ ያዊ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ተፈጥሩ ።
1 Third Homily: on the commentaries of many words from the Torah and from Joshua son of Nun that confirm the merit of the orthodox Christian faith. O beloved brother, may God illuminate the eyes of your heart with the light of His Holy Spirit Paraclete, so that you may understand the mystery of His divinity. I have already promised to reveal to you from the book of the Torah and from Joshua son of Nun what confirms for you the hermeneutics that I illustrated to you in the book on the revelation of the incarnation of the Son of God and His crucifixion. In fact, in it I wrote to you that Adam and Eve were created and (God) left them in paradise, not so that they would remain there forever, but so that they would ascend to the exalted heavenly order from which the devil and his host fell. The devil, with his error, made them fall down to the earth, and with his host he reigned over them and over their offspring, during their life and after their death, until God with His ingenious wisdom, of which I reminded you, sent His Son in the body of His incarnation. He hid Himself from the devil, so much so that the devil thought that He was a weak man and ran to Him to kill Him. But Christ killed him to avenge him (i.e., man). He redeemed all the offspring of Adam from his chains and with them went to paradise, the place where Adam and Eve lived of old, until the number of people who died in penance equaled that of the hosts who fell with the devil, and after that they would ascend to the heavenly kingdom for which they were created.

### Declaration of the typological character of the Old Testament
ነጽር ፡ ኦፍቁር ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ በኦሪት ፡ ወኢያሱቡ፤ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወዘርኡ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አምሳሉ?" ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ግብ ራት ፡ ዘዘከርክዎሙ ፡ ለከ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ ፡ አምሳሉ ፡ ገሃድ ፡ ወክሥት ። ወዘሰ ፡ መሰለ ፡ ምሳሌሃ' ፡ በከመ ፡ አምሳሉ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ኅብእተ ፡ ታጽህቅ ፣ ለዘይከሥታ ፡ ወያበይን ፡፣ ኅቡአቲሃ ። ለቡ ፣ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ዘእከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ እምዜናሁ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ወዘርኡ ፡ እስመ ፣ ያጌብረኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እክሥቶ ፡ በኅዳጥ ፡ ነገር ። እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኢያነብቡ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወኢመጽሐፈ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ የወኢይሌብዉ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ አጤይቀከ' ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ወአንተሰ ፡ አንብቦሙ ፡ ለዘኢያአምር ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፣ ወኢይሌብው ፡ ትርጓ ሜሆሙ ። ወአንሰ ፡ እከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ በቅሩብ ። ወእዜክር ፡
2 Observe, O beloved, what it says in the Torah and Joshua son of Nun about Abraham and his offspring. All of this is a type of these facts concerning Adam and his offspring, which I have (already) reminded you of; it is its manifest and revealed type. He who represented its likeness (implying) that its type is within it, and that it was hidden, requires efforts (expository) on the part of him who reveals it and manifests its mysteries. Pay attention, O my beloved, to what I set before you from the story of Abraham and his offspring, since I must present it in a few words. Many people, in fact, do not read the book of the Torah, nor the book of Joshua son of Nun, and do not understand their history. I, however, explain to you the interpretation of their history, and you, read it to those who do not know their history and do not understand their interpretation. I disclose to you, therefore, in brief their history, and after that I will mention its interpretation.

### The story of Abraham


[p. 300] {#po.56.p300}
ትርጓሜሃ ፡ እምድኅረ" ፣ ዝክቱ ። ጽሑፍ ፣ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ እምኦሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሶርያዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃድር ፡ በካራን ፡ ሀገር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ዘዕራቅ ፡ አስተርአየ'፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአፍለሶ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሳራ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወአኅደሮሙ!' ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ኀይመት ፡ በገዳም ፡ በምድረ ፡ ከንአን ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወተካየዶ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ያውርሶ ፡ ለይ እቲ ፡ ምድር ፡ ኩለንታሃ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለዘርኡ ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወኮኑ ፡ እለ ፡ የኃድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምድር ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ረዐይት ፡ ያመልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ" ፡ ዲበ ፡ ገጻ ፡ ለምድር"' ፡ በእማንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ያመልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡በምድረ ፡ ከናአን ፡ ወበካልአን ፡ ባዕ ዳን ። ኮነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ የኃድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐይመት ፡ እስከ ፡ ወሀቦ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይስሐቅ!'' ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ይስሐቅ?" ፡ የኃድር” ፡ በው ስተ" ፡ ሐይመት ፡ በከመ" ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸንሐ” ፡ ኪዳኖ ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውርሶ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከኖአን ፡ በከመ ፡፣ ተካየዶሙ ።
3 It is written in the Book of Creation of the Torah that Abraham was a Syrian. While he was dwelling in the land of Haran on the island of Iraq, God appeared to him and transferred him from it, him and his wife Sarah, and made them dwell in the tent in the desert, in the land of Canaan which is the land of Jerusalem. He promised him that He would make all that land an inheritance for him and for his offspring after him (Gen 17:8). The inhabitants of that land were giant peoples, worshippers of idols, because all those who were on the face of the earth in those times were worshippers of idols, in the land of Canaan and elsewhere. Abraham used to dwell in the tent until God gave him Isaac his son, and Isaac dwelt in the tent like his father, waiting for the promise of God that He would make him inherit the land of Canaan according to what He had promised them.

### Isaac, Jacob, and Joseph
ወይስሐቅኒ ፡ ወለዶ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ዘሰመዮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስራ ኤል ። ወሀለዉ፤ ፡፣ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ የኃድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐይመት ፡ ይጸንሑ" ፡ ኪዳነ ። ወያዕቆብሰ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወለደ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፣ ፅደወ ። ወርእየ ፡ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ሕልመ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዮሴፍ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይኤምር" ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ንጉሠ ። ወቀንኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አኃ ዊሁ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ወመሐክዎ ፡ ወጥዎ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ባፅዳን ፡ ነጋድያን ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ወሰድዎ" ፡ “ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፣ ወነበሩ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በእሙንቱ' ፡ መዋፅል ፡ ያመልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ" ። ወነጋድያ ንሰ ፡ ጫጥዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፣ እምአግብርተ" ፡ ፈርዖን ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኮነ ሎቱ ፡ ምክንያት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ዘነዊኅ'' ፡ ተፍጻሜቱ ፡ አላ ፡ አመክነየ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ እ!'ግዚአብሔር ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ለፈር ዖን ፣ ንጉሠ ፣ ግብጽ" ። ወፈነወ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከናአን" ፣ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ እስራኤል” ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡፣ አኃዊሁ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ያዕቆብ ። ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአኅደሮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፣
4 Isaac begat Jacob whom God called Israel (Gen 32:29): Isaac and Jacob his son dwelt in the tent waiting for the promise. Jacob Israel begat twelve men, and one of them saw a dream; it was Joseph, who indicated that he would become king. His brothers had envy of him, for which they decided to kill him. They had compassion on him and sold him to foreign merchants, and these led him to the land of Egypt. In those days in the land of Egypt they worshipped idols. The merchants sold him to an official among the servants of Pharaoh, king of Egypt. Various situations of long development happened to him, but God procured for him such occasions that he was able to rise to be head of the army of Pharaoh, king of Egypt. He sent (envoys) to the land of Canaan and caused Jacob Israel his father to arrive, and all his brothers, sons of Jacob. He caused them to arrive at the land of Egypt and made them dwell in the land of Goshen (Gen 45:10); they grew and multiplied, until Jacob died and (died) all his sons, and Joseph died, and the Pharaoh who knew him. Death of Joseph and slavery of the Jews in Egypt

5 Another Pharaoh was elected who did not know him and saw that the children of Israel, the Hebrews, had grown much and feared when they grew and imposed themselves on the people of Egypt (Ex 1:6-9). He began to force them to perform his works, so that they would build and produce bricks. He set over them

ያጌብርዎሙ ፡ ወትረ ። ወዓዲ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስጥምዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ተባዕት ፡ ዘይትወለዱ፤ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይብዝጉ ። ወእግዚአብሔርሰ' ፡ ያልህቆሙ ፣፡ ወያበዝፕሙ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ጣብዝንቱ ፡ ኩነኔ" ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ነዊኃ ፡ መዋፅዕለ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ተሣሃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ።
(people) from his army who subjugated them continuously. Furthermore, he commanded that they drown in the river all the males that were born to them (Ex 2:22) so that they would not multiply. God, however, made them grow and multiply while they were in this great torment. They lived thus for long days until God had pity on them.

### The mission of Moses
ወአስተርአዮ ፡ ለአሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሙሴ ። ወፈነዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡፣ ንጉሠ ፣ ግብጽ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አምሳከ ፡ ዕብራዊያን ፡ ጸው ዓነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንፅ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሞርባነ ። ወንሕነ ፡ ንፈቅድ ፡ ንፃእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ መጠነ ፡ ሠለቱ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሥሠዕ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ወንትመየጥ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይወፅኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ፣የይሖሩ ፡ ውስተ' ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከናአን ፡ ወይቅትሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ወይንግሥ ፡ ባቲ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተካየደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአበዊሆሙ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ ይስ ሐቅ ፡ ወያፅቆብ ፣ እስራኤል ። ወእግዚአብሔርሰ ፡ ተናገሮ ፡ ለፈርዖን" ፡ ዘንተ'" ፡ ነገረ ፡ ጥበብ ። ፣ወናሁ ፡፣ ሀለውኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እተርገም፤! ፡ ለከ ፡ ድኅረ ። ወፈርያዖንሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ በፀአቶሙ'' ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙ
6 He appeared to one of them named Moses (Ex 3:2) and sent him to Pharaoh, king of Egypt, and said to him: “The God of the Hebrews has summoned us to immolate a sacrifice to Him. We desire to go out to the desert for three days to immolate a sacrifice to Him and return” (Ex 5:1). With this, He permitted that when they would go out into the desert and go to the land of Canaan, they would kill the people, and reign over it as He had promised their fathers Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob Israel. God addressed this word of wisdom to Pharaoh. I will give you the interpretation later. When Pharaoh heard of their departure, he realized that they had made a pretext toward him and added against them torment upon their torment. The punishments against Egypt
ንቱ ፡ ተመክነዩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ወወሰኮሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ በዲበ'' ፡ ኩነኒሆሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ዝብጥ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ በበትርከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ዘበጣ ፡ ኮኑ” ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አፍላሳጋተ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ'' ፡ አብሕ ርት ፡ ወአንቅዕት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ደመ። ይሰትዩ ፡ ‹እምኔሆሙ ፡ ግብጻውያን" ፡ ፣‹ደመ ፣ ወዕብራዊያንሰ ፡ ይሰትዩ'' ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፣ ማየ'"። ወይቤሎ ፡ ፈርዖን ፥፡ ለሙሴ አእትት ፡ እምላዕሌነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ወአነ ፡ አኀድጎሙ” ፡ ይፃኡ። ወሶበ ፡ አእተተ ፡ ዝብጠተ ፡ አጽንዐ”' ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ልቦ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ ወኢኀደጎሙ ፣ ይፃኡ። ወዓዲ ፡ ዘበጣ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ለምድር ፡ በበ ትሩ ፡ ወመልአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ቁርነነዓተ ፡ በባሕር ፡ ወበየ ብስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምድረ” ፣ ጌሜም፤' ፡ ዘሀለዉ ፡ ዕብራውያን ፡ ውስቴታ” ። ወዓዲ ፡ ዘበጦሙ” ፣ በበትሩ ፡ ፣ወአውረደ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ” ፡ አኩተ"' ፡ ዘከልብ ፡
7 God said to Moses: “Strike the sea with your staff.” When he struck it, all the rivers of the land of Egypt, the seas, the springs became blood (Ex 7:17-21). From them the Egyptians drank blood while the Hebrews from the same drank water. Pharaoh said to Moses: “Remove this calamity from us and I will let them go out.” When he removed the plague, God hardened the heart of Pharaoh and he did not let them go out. Moses struck again with his staff and filled all the land of Egypt with frogs, in the sea and on the dry land, except the land of Goshen in which the Hebrews lived (Ex 7:26; 8:2). He struck them again with his staff and sent down upon them dog-flies and small locusts (Ex 8:12).

He also killed all their animals and their goods. He then brought syphilis upon men and women (Ex 9:10). Furthermore, He made hail and stones rain upon them, and broke their plants and their seeds (Ex 9:23-25). He then sent against them the locusts and they devoured all their fruits (Ex 10:13-15). And again, upon them He made the sun, the moon, and the stars darkness. The darkness (was) like walls by day and by night for three days (Ex 10:21-23). Nine (were the) plagues that Moses inflicted upon the people of Pharaoh and his army with his staff, by order of God.

### Behavior of Pharaoh
እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይኤዝዞ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዝብጦሙ'"፣፡ ይቤሎ፤! ፡ ዝብጦሙ!'' ፡ በዝብጠተ ፡ እገሌ ፡ ወአንሰ'' ፡ ፣'አገዝፍ ፡ ልበ" ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ 'ወኢይሰድዶሙ ፡ ይሖሐሩ" ፡ እስከ ፡ አርኢ ፡ ኃይልየ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወያእምሩ" ፡ ስምየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ'' ፡ ምድር ። ወኮኑ" ፡ ሶበ” ፡ ይበጽ ሖሙ ፣ መከራ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእትት ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወይ ፈንውዎሙ፤' ፣ ለደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ። ወሶበ ፡ ያእትት"! ፡ ያገዝፍ ፡ ልበ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢይበጽሖሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራ ኤል ፡ ወኢምድረ ፡ ጌሜም” ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ዝብጠታት” ።
8 When God gave him orders to strike them, He said to him: “Strike them with such and such a plague and I will make the heart of Pharaoh fat and he will not send them so that they may go, until I have shown my power in him and they will know my name in all the earth” (Ex 10:1-2). When the affliction reached them, they asked Moses to remove (the calamity) from them and they released the children of Israel. When (the calamity) departed, he fattened the heart of Pharaoh, as God had said. None of those calamities reached the children of Israel, nor the land of Goshen.

9 After nine calamities, God commanded Moses to order the children of Israel that they should slaughter in their houses a lamb for every family; that they should slaughter it on the evening of the 14th of the month of Nisan which enters into the night of the 15th, in such a way that with its blood they would anoint the door of their houses. God said: “For in that night I will send an angel who corrupts and kills all the firstborns of Pharaoh, all his people, that is, the males that are born to them. That blood will become a sign for the angel who destroys so that

### Release of the Hebrews and their exodus

10 This is the tenth calamity. In that hour all the troops of Pharaoh and all the Egyptians went out and released the children of Israel. They urged them so that they would go away quickly. The Hebrews borrowed from them many riches which they took with them when they went out. When they went out on the 15th day of the month that God in Hebrew called fash, which means migration, He ordered them to make it a memorial every year on account of what God had done for them. At their exit from Egypt, the number of those who carried the sword was 600,000 (Ex 12:37), and together with them there were many other people with all their properties, with their wives, with their children, and their rabbis. God led them on the way with a pillar of cloud (Ex 13:21) and did not lead them to the land of Canaan that He had promised them, but brought them to the east until the sea of Eritrea which is the desert of Clysma. Pharaoh pursues the Hebrews after having released them and drowns together with his army
ቅ-ልዝም ። ወገዝፈ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ ወነስሐ” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፀአቶሙ ፡ ወተለ ዎሙ ፥፣ እንዘ ፡ ምስሌሁ” ፡ ፳፻፻ ፡ ሰረገላ ፡ የእምኅሩያን ፣ ሠራዊቱ" ፡ በአ ፍራስ ፣ ወበንዋየ ፣ ሐቅል ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ'”' ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ በሐይቀ ፡ ባሕር ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ዘበጦ”" ፡ ለባ ሕር ፡ በበትሩ ፡ ወፈለጦ ፡ ይምነ ፡ ወጽግመ ፡ ወአቀሞ ፡ ከመ ፡ አረፍት ፡ ወአንሶሰዉ”' ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ውስጡ ። ወኀለፉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወገዝፈ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተለውዎሙ” ፡
11 The heart of Pharaoh hardened and he repented of their exit, therefore he pursued them with 60,000 (Ex 14:7) chariots among his chosen troops, with horses and with weapons. When they reached them, while they were near the shore of the sea, Moses struck the sea with his staff, according to what God had ordered him. He divided it to the right and to the left and erected it as a wall, and the children of Israel walked in the middle of its interior. gift of the Torah 12 Moses went before the children of Israel to guide them, until the desert of Sinai, and there they dwelt in tents, just as their fathers dwelt in tents. In the desert, God nourished them with the manna that rained upon them every day like coriander fruit (Ex 16:13-15). They gathered it and made it bread and ate it. God ordered Moses and he struck the rock with his staff and from it flowed so many waters and they became like many rivers and they drank from it (Ex 17:5-6; Num 20:7-11). He ordered them to constitute a church from the tent in the desert, and inside it they erected an altar and offered a sacrifice to Him. He gave them a law and commandments which is the Torah, because in the Hebrew language, Torah means “law.” Sending of the spies to Canaan

13 He also ordered the twelve men so that one from every tribe would go to the land of Canaan, (as) spies who would bring to him from its fruits so that they would know its goodness (Num 13:1-20). In fact, it is the land that makes milk and honey flow, and they loved it and were resolute to fight the people until they would reign in it. God knew that they did not believe in His word that He had said regarding that (land). The twelve spies went to the land of Canaan, wandered in it, and dwelt there for forty days wandering in its interior. They made a return to them, and together with them there was among its fruits a cluster of grapes that they carried on a bed, many pomegranates and figs, of large fruits (Num 13:23). When they made a return to them, some of the spies said to them: “As for the land, it is very good, as God has said, and this is its fruit; however, its cities are strong and its

14 Upon hearing this, the people diminished their faith in God as if He were not capable of making them inherit that land according to what He had promised them. They began to cry and to shout saying: “We cannot return to Egypt and the land of Canaan we cannot inherit. We and our children will die in this desert.” The other two spies, among whom one was Joshua son of Nun, disciple of Moses, who was with Moses throughout all his days, and the second Caleb son of Jephunneh, said to them: “O men! God who has done great wonders upon Pharaoh and his army, the Same who always manifests these wonders to us, will He not perhaps be able to make us reign over this land and fight for us those who inhabit it? Why do you frighten yourselves and diminish your faith?” (cf. Num 14:6-9). But the people did not listen to them because the other ten spies were diminishing their faith, to the point of inducing them to doubt. The people desired to stone Moses and his brother Aaron because they had made them go out from Egypt (Num 14:10). Divine punishment against the little faith of the people

15 At the instant, a pillar of cloud descended upon the church-tent inside which, by order of God, they had constituted the tabernacle of the testimony, and God manifested Himself inside it. He spoke to Moses and to Aaron and wanted to destroy all the people. Moses interceded with Him until He withdrew His wrath and did not destroy them at the instant, but swore that He would deal with them according to what they had believed and thought (that is), that they would all die in that desert (Num 14:22-23). (God) did not let any of them see that land of Canaan, nor that they would inherit it, but that they would live in that desert for forty years, (exactly) like the forty days that the spies spent

wandering the land of Canaan, so that they would stay in the desert one year in exchange for one day until they would all die and their children would grow up, the nursing children, who do not know (how to distinguish) good from evil and whose faith did not diminish. These (latter) would go and would inherit the land, while none of the sixty thousand who went out from Egypt would enter into it and would inherit it, except Joshua son of Nun and Caleb of Jephunneh (Num 14:30) who had believed in God that He is just. They lived in that desert for forty years, the spies and all those who dwelt in the tents, and Moses was with them.

### Sunset of Moses and the succession of Joshua. Conquest of Canaan
ወሶበ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ ወልህቁ ፡ ውሉዶሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ግ ፡ ዓመት ፡፣ ሖረ፤'' ፣፡ ሙሴ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፣ የኀሥሥ" ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከና አን" ። ወሶበ" ፡ ቀርቡ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ማእከሌ ሆሙ ፣ ወማእከሌሃ''። ወአእመረ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ኢየኀልፍ"' ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብጣእንተ ፡ አሐቲ"" ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘስሕተ ፡ ወተቁጥዖ ፣ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ በእንቲአሃ ። ወከልኦ ፡ በዊአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከናአን ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ለኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ረድኡ" ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣ ወሞተ ። ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ነሥኦሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ወአቆመ”' ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ ወተእኅዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ እም ሑረት ። የብሰ”! ፡ ማይ ፡ እምፈለግ ፡ እስከ ፡ የዐድው” ፡ ኩሉ” ፡ ሕዝብ” ። ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፣ በቅድሜሆሙ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ያጸንዖሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጸቢእ ፣ እለ ፣ የኀድሩ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ከናአን” ፣ ወየቴጌይሎሙ"” ፡ እስከ ፡ አር ኀዉ ፡ ኩሎ" ፡ አህጉረ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነግሙ ፣ ቦሙ ። ወተካፈሉ ፡ ምድረ ፡፣ ፲ወ፪ ፡ ነገድ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፣ "ለለ፳8 ፡ ነገድ” ፡ ክፍሎሙ ፡ በመሥፈርት ።
16 When they all died, and their children grew up and reached forty years of age, Moses went with them, pursuing the land of Canaan. When they approached it, they found the Jordan River between them and it (Canaan). Moses knew that he would not pass over with them because of a word he spoke wrongly, for which God was angry with him and prevented him from entering the land of Canaan (Num 20:12). In his place, he appointed Joshua son of Nun, his disciple, over the people, and he died (Num 27:12-22). Joshua son of Nun took the people and went down to the Jordan River and erected the altar of the covenant within it, and the sea was stopped from flowing (Josh 4:9). The water of the river dried up until all the people crossed (it). Joshua son of Nun was before them, encouraging them to fight against the inhabitants of the land of Canaan and strengthening them until they had opened all the cities with the sword and reigned over them. The twelve tribes divided the land, and he gave each tribe its portion according to measure.

They lived within it and rejoiced in its fruits according to what God had promised their fathers Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob.

### Exhortation to the reader. Beginning of the allegorical reading of the texts of Genesis
ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ በኦሪት ፡ ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ። አንብቦ ፡ ለዘኢያጽንያ' ፡ ወድግም ፣ ሎቱ ፣ እስከ ፡፣ ‹ይሌቡ ፡ ሠናየ' ። ወዓዲ ፡ አለብዎ ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ዘእከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሴብሖ" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወንቄድሶ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘወሀበነ ፡ አስተዳለወ ፡ ለነ ፣ ወበደረ'' ፡ ወአመረ'! ፡ ባቲ'' ፡ በውስተ ፡ መጻሕፍቲሁ ፡ ለቡ ፡ ትር ጓሜሃ" ። አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይመስሉ" ፣ ለአ ዳም!፤ ፡ ወለሔዋን'' ፡ እስመ ፡፣ እሙንቱ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራ ኤል ። ወአዳምኒ ፡ ወሔዋን፡ እስመ፤' ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወሐይመታት ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ገነት ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐይመ ታት" ፣ ኮኑ ፡፣ አፍአ ፡ እምዓለም ። ወከማሁ ፡ ገነት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አፍአ ፡ እምዓለም ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ትኩል ፡ በምሥራቀ ፡ ምድር ። ወሐይመታት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ትኩላነ” ፣ ውስተ ፡ አንቀጸ” ፡፣ አህጉረ ፣ ከኖአን?፤ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ገነት ፣
17 This is what is written in the Torah and in Joshua son of Nun. Read it to those who do not learn it and repeat it to them until they are able to understand well. Make them understand also its interpretation, which I explain to you now, so that we may glorify God and sanctify Him for the grace He has given us, which He has prepared and anticipated for us, and through which He has given a sign in His books: understand its interpretation. Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob Israel resemble Adam and Eve because they are the fathers of the children of Israel. Adam and Eve are in fact the parents of humans. The tents in the desert are the antitype of paradise because the tents were outside the world; similarly, paradise is outside the world and is planted to the east of the earth (Gen 2:8). The tents were planted at the gate of the cities of Canaan; similarly, paradise is near the gate of the heavens, because the land of Canaan is the antitype of the Kingdom of Heaven from which the devil fell.
ኀበ ፡ አንቀጸ” ፡ ሰማያት ፡ እስመ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከኖአን” ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መንግ ሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ዘእምኔሁ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ። ወተፈጥሩ ፡ አዳም ፡፣ ወሔዋን” ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ወእምገነት ፡ ይከ ውን ፡ ዕርገት ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መንግሥት ። እስመ ፡ ገነት ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ወእምኔሁ” ፣ ዕርገት ፡ ውስተ” ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ በዊእ ፣ ውስተ፤' ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ከናአን” ፡ እምገዳም ፡ ዘነበሩ ፡ ሐይመታት” ፡ ትኩላት” ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወምድረ ፡ ግብጽሰ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ፣ወፈርዖን ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፡ መኩንነ'"! ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡፣ የምስለ ፣ ሠራዊቱ” ። ወፈለግሰ ፣ ዘኮኑ ፡ ያሰጥሙ ፣ ውስቴቱ” ፡ ሕፃናተ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ያሰጥሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ውስ ቴቱ ። ወሕንጸት ፡፣ ወግንፋል ፣ ይመስሉ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ሥጋዊያተ” ፡ ዘዓ ለም” ፣ ዘይሠምር ፣ ቦሙ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እስመ ፡ እማንቱ” ፣ ምግባራቲሁ ። ወሙሴ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወዝብጠታት ፡ዘዘበጦ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ አምሳለ ፡
18 For them (that is, the Kingdom of Heaven) Adam and Eve were created, and from paradise the ascent to that Kingdom will take place. Indeed, paradise (is located) between heaven and earth, from which the ascent to heaven (will take place), just as the entry into the cities of Canaan will take place from the desert in which the tents were planted. Egypt is the antitype of this world, and Pharaoh and his army are the antitype of the devil, the prince of this world (Jn 12:31), together with his army. As for the river into which the children were sunk, it is the antitype of Sheol into which the armies of the devil sank humans. (The work of) construction and the brick resemble worldly carnal actions in which Satan delights because those are his works. Moses is the type of Christ, and the blows he dealt to Pharaoh are the type of what Christ has

done against Satan. The lamb that they slaughtered is the type of the blood of Christ shed and of his crucifixion. The staff of Moses is the type of the wood of the cross. 19 The Sea of Eritrea here is the type of Sheol. The tents and the desert, again, resemble paradise. Joshua son of Nun is the type of the Holy Spirit, and the Jordan River is the type of the baptism of Christianity. The forty years after their exodus from Egypt are the type of the forty days following the resurrection of Christ. The twelve tribes to whom Joshua son of Nun distributed the land of Canaan are the type of the twelve apostles to whom the Holy Spirit distributed the land by lot. God made Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob dwell in the desert of the land of Canaan and promised them that He would make them inherit it and their progeny. Their small number did not allow them to inherit that spacious land, but (God) created a pretext in their favor that made them go down to Egypt, (where) they reproduced, grew, and multiplied. Similarly, God made Adam and Eve dwell in paradise to make them inherit the exalted order from which the devil and his army fell.

20 But Adam and Eve, when they were few in number, were unable to realize that order in which thousands upon thousands and myriads of myriads lived. The transgression was devised against them so that they went down into the world and grew and multiplied. Just as Pharaoh, king of Egypt, and his army forced the children of Israel to be their slaves, so the devil, the prince of this world, and his army imposed upon the children of Adam their transitory works and the worship of idols through incantations and sorcery and other satanic actions. And as Pharaoh saw to it that his army watched over the children of Israel and forced them (to perform) his actions, similarly the devil placed upon each of the children of Adam an unclean spirit from his army to force him to perform carnal pleasure.
መንፈሰ ፡ ርኩሰ ፣ እምሠራዊቱ ፡ ያጌብሮ” ፣ ለገቢረ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ ሥጋዊት ። ወበከመ ፣ ኮኑ ፣ ሠራዊተ ፣፡ ፈርዖን ፣ ያሰጥሙ ፡ ተባዕታተ” ፣ ደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ያሰ ጥሙ ፣ ለዘይመውት?' ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ አዳም” ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስከ ፣

the children of Adam dies, even the righteous who had not committed sin (Rom 5:14). Indeed, in those sinless children whom they used to sink, God indicated the righteous who had not committed sin. As God sent His servant Moses, who was one of the children of Israel, so that he might free them from Pharaoh and his army and liberate them from their slavery and make them ascend to the land of Canaan and inherit it, according to what He had promised their fathers, similarly God sent Christ, His only-begotten Son, and He became incarnate from the children of Adam and saved them from the devil and his army. He liberated them from his slavery and made them ascend to the heavenly kingdom so that they might inherit it according to what He had promised their fathers. As Moses did not reveal to Pharaoh the secret of their ascent to the land of Canaan but hid it from him when he told him that they would go to the desert to offer a sacrifice, similarly Christ did not reveal to the devil the secret of the ascent of the children of Adam to the heavenly kingdom and did not reveal to him His divinity but hid it from him through His incarnation and humanization and His human actions. Plagues against Egypt, types of the punishments of Christ against Satan.

22 This is what I have explained to you in the book of the interpretation of His humanization and His crucifixion. As Moses struck Pharaoh and his army with those great plagues and God hardened his heart until he had shown His power (Ex 14:4; Rom 9:17), similarly Christ used to strike the devil with those great plagues and hardened his heart to such an extent that he did not recognize Him. When He (Christ) performed a sign or spoke a word that He was God, immediately (afterward) He performed weak human actions, or spoke a weak word in order to hide His divinity from the devil, so that he did not recognize Him. He (Christ) hardened his heart (preventing him) from recognizing Him, exactly as it was said about Pharaoh. The children of Israel were saved from Pharaoh and his army on the morning of the night in which they slaughtered the lamb and became free from the bitter slavery.

world (Jn 1:30). They became free from the bitter slavery through His life-giving cross. As God called that day the day of the Passover (Ex 12:11), the meaning of which is migration, that is, the migration of the children of Israel from Egypt, from the kingdom of Pharaoh. Similarly, on that day which is the fifteenth of the month of Nisan, God made the children of Adam migrate from Sheol, from the kingdom of the powerful devil. As for the interpretation of the slaughter of the lamb and the passover, it is long, and I will explain it to you in another book. The day of the passover has been called the passover of Christ: its interpretation is the passing of the Lord, because this is truly the day of the passing of the Lord, that is, the day of His death for us. Look, my beloved, how great is this word whose figure is written in the Torah of the Jews. Again on the struggle between the devil and Christ in light of the crossing of the Red Sea.

24 As God hardened the heart of Pharaoh so that he pursued Moses and the children of Israel, he and his army, to kill them, similarly the heart of the devil hardened and he became blinded during the crucifixion of Christ so that he thought that He was a weak man and ran to Him, he and his army, to intimidate and kill Him. In the same way as the behavior of the children of Adam, the heart of Pharaoh hardened after he saw the sea that had been divided and was standing like a wall, so that he pursued Moses into the middle of the depth of the sea to reach and kill him. Similarly, after the devil saw the sun that had become darkness for the crucified Christ (Mt 27:45), Christ hardened his heart and blinded him, preventing him from recognizing Him when He said, “My God, my God, why have you forsaken me?” (Mt 27:46), so that he thought that He was a weak man, and he ran to Him to intimidate and kill Him. As Moses struck the sea with his staff and sank Pharaoh and all his army, similarly Christ struck the devil and his army on the wood of His cross and sank them into Sheol when they ran to Him to kill Him. As God brought the children of Israel out of the kingdom of Pharaoh with many people added

to them, similarly He made the righteous ascend from Sheol, from the kingdom of the devil, and with them all those who died, those who worshipped idols and the sinners. He redeemed everyone with His blood and saved them because of His condemnation, because they sinned without knowing it (Rom 5:13-14).

### Types and antitypes of the conquest of Canaan
ወበከመ ፡ ሶበ" ፡ አውፅኦሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወኢወሰዶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ምድረ ፡ ከናአን ፡ አላ ፡ ወሰዶሙ ፡ ወአኅደሮሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሐይመታት ፡ በገዳም ። በከመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ" ፡ እስራኤል' ፡፣ ከማሁ" ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አውፅኦሙ ፡ ለሙታን ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ እምሲኦል ፡ ኢያዕረጎሙ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግ ሥት ፡ ሰማያዊት'' ፡ አላ ፡ ወሰዶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ አኅደሮሙ ፡ ውስ ቴታ፤! ፣ ኀበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አበዊሆሙ! ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ። በከመ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እለ ፡ አዕረጎሙ ፡ እምባሕረ ፡ ኤር ትራ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሐይመታት ፡ በገዳም ፡ ግ ፡ ዓመተ ። ከማሁ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ክር ስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ ዘአዕረጎሙ ፡ እምሲኦል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ እምድኅረ ፣ ትንሣኤሁ" ፡ እሙታን ፡ በ 8" ፡፣ መዓልት"፤ ። ወበከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ረድአ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ "ሞተ ፣ ሙሴ" ፣ ጓ ፣ ዓመት ፡ አንገሦሙ ፣ ለውሉሌድ'' ፡ እለ ፡ መፅኡ" ፡ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ከናአን ፡ ወከፈለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ ፡ ነገድ ። ከማሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈሰ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ መንፈሰ” ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፣፡ እምድ ኅረ ፡ ዕርገተ ፡ ክርስቶስ” ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሰማይ ፣ እምድኅረ"! ፡፣ ጓ ፡ መዓልት ፡
25 Just as when God brought them out of Egypt He did not lead them to the land of Canaan but led them and made them dwell in tents in the desert in the same way as their fathers, Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob Israel, similarly, when Christ brought the dead of the children of Adam out of Sheol, He did not make them ascend to the heavenly kingdom but led them to paradise, made them dwell in it where their parents, Adam and Eve, were. Just as Moses lived together with the children of Israel whom he made ascend from the Sea of Eritrea to the tents in the desert for forty years, similarly Christ stayed with the children of Adam whom He had made ascend from Sheol to paradise after His resurrection from the dead on the fortieth day. Just as Joshua son of Nun, disciple of Moses, did, who forty years after the death of Moses made the children who came out of Egypt reign over Canaan and distributed (it) to the twelve tribes; similarly the Holy Spirit, the Spirit of Christ, the Spirit Paraclete, forty days after the ascension of Christ to heaven, descended upon the apostles, strengthened them, consoled them, and distributed the land to the twelve apostles, gave them victory over the demons who dwell within people.
ወረደ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣ ወአኀየሎሙ ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ፡ ወከፈለ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ለ፲ወ፪ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ መዊአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እለ ፡ የኀ ድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰብእ ። ሞኦሙ”" ፣ በሃይማኖት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ርትዕት” ። ወበከመ ፡ ሙሴ” ፡ ኢያኅለፎሙ ፡፣ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ አላ ፡ ኢያሱ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ዘሀሎ” ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኩሉ” ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ክር ስቶስ ፣ ኢወሀቦሙ?' ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፣ ጥምቀተ ። ፡ ዘባቲ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ውስተ” ፣፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ አላ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሀቦሙ ፡ ዝክተ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡፣ መንፈሰ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይሄሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ወይናዝዞሙ ፣ ወያ፳ንዖሙ!'' ፡ ወይሁቦሙ ፣
26 He defeated them through the holy (and) upright faith. Just as Moses did not make the children of Israel cross the Jordan River, but rather Joshua son of Nun, his disciple who had always been with him, similarly, Christ did not give the children of Adam the baptism through which they reach the Kingdom of Heaven, but rather the Holy Spirit. He gave them that, that is, the spirit of Christ which is with Him, and it will always be with all the children of the apostles who are the true children of Israel, and it consoles them and strengthens them and gives them victory over the army of the devil until they have inherited their heavenly


[p. 328] {#po.56.p328}

kingdom. This interpretation, O my beloved, has become the type of Adam and his offspring, of those who died and whom Christ made ascend from Sheol to paradise. This is also the type of all the living who have believed in Christ, since in them the number of the armies that are in paradise is equal: understand that as you have understood this.

### Typology of Adam and Eve
"ወበከመ ፡፣ ወአኅደሮሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለሔዋን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረሱ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት" ፡ ወአኅደር" ፡ ለአብ ርሃም'' ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሐይመታት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከናአን ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ በከመ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ወአምሳሊሁ'! ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ወራሴ!፤ ፡ ለመንግሥቱ"! ፡ ሰማያዊ” ። ወአኅደሮ"፤ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ከርሠ ፣ እሙ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አዳም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፣ ወየሐዩ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ድካም ፡ ወይሴ።ሰይ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ሕዳጠ'' ፡ እንበለ ፡ ጸማ'' ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ወዒአ ፡፣ ኩስሕ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ባሕ ቲቱ ፣ ርቅ ፡ እምዓለም ። ወውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፣ እሙ ፣ በማእከለ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ በከመ ፡ ገነት ። ወከርሥ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በአምሳለ ፣ ሐይመት ፣ ወገዳም ፣ እንተ ፡ ርሕቅት ፡ እምዓለም ፡ ዘኮነ ፡፣ ውስቴታ ፡ አብርሃም ። ወበከመ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወረዱ” ፡ ውሌሉደ ፡፣ አብርሃም ፣ እምሐይመታት ፡ እስከ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ይወርድ ፡
27 Just as God made Adam and Eve dwell in paradise so that they might inherit the Kingdom of Heaven and made Abraham dwell in the tents so that he might inherit the land of Canaan, similarly, God created man openly, according to His image and likeness (Gen 1:26-27) so that he might become the heir of His heavenly kingdom. He made him dwell in the womb of his mother so that he might stay inside it, exactly as Adam was inside paradise and lived without toil and fed on little food, without sin, without excretion; only he was far from the world. He was in the womb of his mother, between heaven and earth, like paradise. The womb is also the type of the tent and the desert, far from the world within which Abraham lived. As Adam fell to the land of the world, the children of Abraham descended from the tents to Egypt; similarly, man descends from the womb of his mother to this world, a receptacle of toil and humiliation, and the devil reigns over him, as he reigned over Adam his father.
የሰብእ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እሙ?" ፡ ለዝዓለም ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ድካም ፡ ወኀሳር ፣ ወይ ነግሥ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አዳም ። ወበከመ ፡፣ ነግሠ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወኮነ" ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ርኩስ ፣ እምሠራዊተ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ያገብሮ” ፡ በምግባራ ቲሁ ፣ በከመ ፣ ገብረ ፡፣ በአዳም ፡ ከማሁ” ፡ ገብረ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ በደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ። ወበከመ ፡ አግዓዞሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር” ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራአል ፡ እምፈርዖን ፡ ሶበ ፡ አኅለፎሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ ፡ ከማሁ ፡

Just as God freed the children of Israel from Pharaoh when He made them cross the Sea of Eritrea, similarly, God made man pass through the

አኅለፎ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወአ ግዓዞ ፡ እምዲያብሎስ ፣ ወአስጠሞ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ማየ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ በከመ ፡ አስጠሞ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወያበውኦ፤ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ደብተራ ፣ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕርገቱ ፡ እም ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ በከመ ፡ አብኦሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፣ ውስተ ፣ ገዳም ፡፣ ወአኅደሮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሐይመት" ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕርገቶሙ ፡ እም ባሕር ። እስመ፤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ጣብከመ ፡ አምሳለ" ፡ ሐይመት ፡ ወከ ማሁ ፣ ገብረ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በቀዳሚሩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሐይመተ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ለነ ፡ ትእምርተ" ፡ ወይእቲ" ፡ ርሕቅት ፡ እምዓለም ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ገዳም ፣ ይበውእ'' ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕርገቱ ፡ እም ጥምቀት ፡ በከመ ፡ ቦኡ'፤' ፣ ደቂቀ'' ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐይመታት ፡ በገ
baptism of Christianity. He freed him from the devil and sank into the water of baptism the unclean spirit that was upon him, as He sank Pharaoh in the sea. And He introduces him to the tent of the temple, after his ascent from the baptism of Christianity, just as He introduced the children of Israel into the desert and made them dwell in the tents after they emerged from the sea. Indeed, the church is like the antitype of the tent, so that God first made the church-tent to manifest a sign to us, and it was far from the world (as) an antitype of the desert. Man enters it after his emergence from baptism, as the children of Israel entered the tents in the desert after their emergence from the sea, as the children of Adam entered paradise after their ascent from Sheol by means of Christ. The Church as antitype of multiple elements of the Old Testament.

29 As for the church, it is according to the model of the tent. When the baptized enters it, he eats the manna, that is, the bread of life that descended from heaven, the flesh of Christ to live by it forever (Jn 6:48-51), as the children of Israel in the tents in the desert used to eat the manna. The baptized in the church drinks the water and the blood that flowed from the side of Christ when they pierced Him on the wood of the cross (Jn 19:34), as the children of Israel in the desert drank the water that flowed for them from the rock through the staff of Moses (Ex 17:5-6; Num 20:7-11). Christ is indeed that rock of division and the stumbling stone as the prophet Isaiah called Him (Is 28:16; Rom 9:33) and said: “For he who believes in Him will not be put to shame” (Is 28:16; Rom 10:11). As the prophet David called Him in Psalm 117: “The stone which the builders rejected has become the cornerstone” (Ps 117:22; Acts 4:11). As the prophet Daniel says: “The stone that was cut out from the mountain, without human hand” (Dan 2:34), this means that Christ was born of the virgin, without human seed, and became a great mountain and filled the whole world and shattered all the kingdoms (Dan 2:35, 44-45).

30 The church truly resembles the tent in the desert. It also resembles paradise as we have already said, because inside paradise there were four rivers that flowed and quenched the thirst of the whole earth

(Gen 2:10-14); similarly, in the church there are the four gospels, four rivers of life that flow in it and quench the thirst of humans of the whole earth, those who are thirsty for the Word of God, those who love it. As the prophet David says in Psalm 41: “As the deer loves the springs of waters” (Ps 41:2). Again, in Psalm 118 he says: “How sweet is your word to my throat, it is tasted at my mouth more than honey and sugar” (Ps 118:103). In paradise there are many plants, and in the church there are many books that are the Word of God that satisfy the souls and vivify them.

### Types of the eucharist

31 As Moses says in the Torah: “Indeed, man does not live by bread alone but by every word that comes out of the mouth of God” (Dt 8:3). He affirms that the Word of God is the bread of life. In the same way, the prophet Isaiah also called the Word of God bread and water (Is 55:10-11) and pronounced a prophecy about the Jews of these days. He said: “It will happen that in those days there will be a great famine, not of bread and water, says the Lord, but of the Word” (Am 8:11; cf. Is 55). He ascertained that His Word is bread and water. And now the Jews are hungry for it because they read it but do not understand its meaning because God has closed their eyes, their hearts, and their ears, just as this prophet says regarding them (Is 6:9-10). And in paradise there is the tree of life of which God said: “He who eats of it will live forever” (cf. Gen 2:9; Rev 2:7). And in the church there is the true tree of life, the flesh and blood of Christ who says: “He who has eaten my flesh and has drunk my blood will live forever” (Jn 6:54). The Church “the paradise of God on earth”.

32 The church is truly the paradise of God on earth, mother, progenitor, because Adam was in paradise and fell to this world, then to Sheol. And when Christ made him ascend from it, He made him return to paradise again; similarly, also the man who stays in the womb of his mother and is born to a carnal birth and falls to this world inhabits it in condemnation. When Christ decides to free him from the condemnation and leads him back to the womb of his mother a second time (Jn 3:4), which is baptism, just as He led Adam a second time to paradise so that he might be reborn through baptism, a new spiritual birth (Tit 3:5), he is born of God so that he may stay with Him

33 They nurse him with rational milk (1 Pet 2:2), the flesh and blood of Christ from the pure breasts, the paten and the chalice, just as his mother nursed him with milk on the day of his carnal birth. His mother indeed nurses him with milk, that is, her flesh and her blood, because he is a child and does not have the capacity to eat food, but his mother nurses him until he becomes like her and feeds on her food. Similarly, the person who in baptism is born of God, they nurse him with the body and blood of Christ during his life in this world. Indeed, he is carnal and never has the capacity to feed on the spiritual food, Christ, but he nurses on the milk of Christ, that is, His flesh and His blood, until he becomes like Christ after his death and his resurrection and lives in the life of Christ with His food that does not run out and with His grace that does not pass. Because the Gospel says to you thus: “You will eat and drink with me at my table in my kingdom” (Lk 22:30), this means that they will live like Him, in the same way as a son who lives like his fathers when he grows up and becomes like them. Baptism, eucharist: sources of life under the Lordship of the Holy Spirit.

34 And in the holy baptism of Christianity the person becomes free from the power of the devil and becomes born without the pleasure (of the flesh), like the humanization of Christ, because the spirit of Christ sanctifies from conception from the pleasure (of the flesh), and Satan will not have power over him after the spirit of Christ is within him. The spirit of Christ, which is the Holy Spirit, is indeed the antitype of Joshua, son of Nun, he who strengthened all the faithful, until they would reign in the Kingdom of Heaven from which the devil and his army fell. He saves them from them and has victory over them just as Joshua, son of Nun, did with the children of Israel. As for the faithful in this world, they are the antitype of the children of Israel when in the desert of Mount Sinai

35 As the spies sent to the land of Canaan brought to the children of Israel from the fruits of the land (Num 13:1-20), similarly the priests who understood the knowledge of the Heavenly Kingdom bring to the faithful the fruit of the Kingdom of Heaven, the flesh and blood of Christ, because it is a tasty fruit that the spies brought. It is a little of the grace of the land so that the children of Israel might see, taste, and ascertain its goodness and love that land and rejoice in the abundance of its fruit. Similarly, the priests bring to the people of the faithful the flesh and blood of Christ so that they might contemplate it and taste it, and love the Kingdom of Heaven within which they live eternal life. They indeed receive a little here, in the measure of their capacity, because they are like children who suck milk. There, instead, they will receive it and will rejoice in it with perfect joy, seeing His face, glorifying Him, sanctifying Him, and they will be together with Him in His kingdom, and their life will be together with Him because they will become in His image and will live of His life, as a son lives in the life of his fathers when he becomes like them. Symbology of Joshua son of Nun and the disbelieving spies.

36 As for the eucharist, it is a pledge of the fruit of the Kingdom of Heaven through which we live here, and the priests are the spies. Joshua son of Nun is one of the two spies who believed and were justified through that Holy Spirit, because the Holy Spirit is truly the exalted Kingdom of Heaven; He is with us down here and He makes us understand the goodness of that Kingdom with us. It is He who dwells within us in baptism and in the priesthood and makes our hearts ascend together with Him to that Kingdom of Heaven, just as the spies ascended with Joshua son of Nun to the land of Canaan, so that we might contemplate the goodness of that Kingdom and assure ourselves of it

in the Holy Spirit who made us ascend to it together with Him. And we come to the faithful and proclaim to them its goodness and make them eat of its fruits. He who among all the priests will have ascertained the goodness of this Kingdom, and will have taught it to the people and will have assured them of it, and will have strengthened them because they love and perform the good work through which they inherit this Kingdom, and strengthens their faith to fight the demons who envy them because of this kingdom, he will become like Caleb son of Jephunneh who strengthened the children of Israel together with Joshua son of Nun and inherited with them the land of Canaan.

37 Such is the priest who strengthens the people and teaches them good works, so that he inherits the Kingdom of Heaven together with the Holy Spirit, who is the antitype of Joshua son of Nun. Conversely, those priests who have experienced the goodness of this Kingdom of Heaven and do not teach it to the people, and do not confirm them, and do not strengthen their faith, they shall be deprived of the grace of God, just as the ten spies perished who saw the goodness of the land of Canaan but dampened the people's enthusiasm for conquering it. They said to them: “For the land is very good, but those who inhabit it are powerful; in their eyes we were like grasshoppers, and its walls are raised up to the heavens,” and they perished because of this word and diminished the faith of the people and caused the 60,000 who came out of Egypt to perish (Nm 13:1-33). Exhortation to the priests.

Undoubtedly, the demons are powerful, while the faithful are like grasshoppers, and the place that the faithful seek is more exalted than all the
