heavens. However, if we faithful believe that Christ defeated their leader on the wood of the cross and saved from their hands all those who were in Sheol (Ps 68:18; Is 24:21-22; 1 Pt 3:19-20, 4:6; Heb 2:14-15), He is capable of fighting them on our behalf and saves us from their hands. If we believe this, we glorify Him and sanctify Him always and beseech Him to save us from them and give us victory over them and make us reach the exalted heavenly order over which they were kings, just as He made those who among the children of Canaan had believed reach the land of Canaan and gave them victory over those who inhabited it.
ከኖአን ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፣ መዊአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እለ ፡ የኀድሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ። በእንተዝ ፣ ይደልዎሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ካህናት" ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጽንዑ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ለጸቢኦቶሙ ፡ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ መንግሥት ፣ ዘይነብሩ!" ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ሠናይት ። ወዝንቱ'' ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለክርስ ቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረቦን'' ። ወሰይጣናትሰ ፡ ኀያላን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፣ ሰብእ ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አንበጣ ፡ አላ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ለእመ'' ፡ አመነ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ንሰብሖ' ፡ ወንቀድሶ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ የሀበነ ፡ መዊአ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኖፈቅነ ፡ ወንብል ፡ ኢንክል ፣ ንጽብኦሙ ፡ ወኢን ክል ፡ ንረስ ፡ መንግሥቶሙ ፡ "ንሕነኒ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ንጠፍእ" ፡ ወነሐውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ በከመ ፡ ጠፍኡ ፡ እለ ፡ ናፈቁ ፣ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወኢይፈውሰነ'' ፡ ዘበላዕነ ፡ ወሰተይነ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፣ ወደሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ በከመ ፡፣ ኢተፈወሱ ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ በዘበልዑ" ፡ እምነ ፣ መኖና ፣ ወበዘሰትዩ” ፡ ማየ ፡ ዝውኅዘ ፡ እምኩኩሕ"”' ወይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ!፤' ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይናፍቁ ፡ ወኢይቤሉ” ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ኃጥአን ፡፣ ወኢኮነ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ ለይ እቲ ፣፡ መንግሥት ፡ አላ ፡ 'ይኔስሑ ፡ ወይትኔስሑ?' ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጣውኢ ሆሙ ፡ ወይሰብሕዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቀድስዎ ፡ ወትረ ። ወያንብቡ ፡ መጻ
39 Therefore, it is necessary that all priests corroborate the faith of the people so that they may fight them and tell them that the kingdom in which they will live is good. And this flesh and blood of Christ is its pledge. As for the demons, they are very powerful and all people before them were like grasshoppers, but if we believe in Christ, we glorify Him and sanctify Him always: He will give us victory over them. If, however, we doubt and declare: “We cannot fight them and we cannot inherit their kingdom,” we too shall all perish and go to Sheol, just as those who among the children of Israel doubted perished. What we have eaten and drunk of the flesh and blood of Christ will not heal us, just as those who ate of the manna and drank of the water that flowed from the rock were not healed. It is necessary that none of the faithful doubt and that they do not say: “We are sinners and are not worthy of that kingdom,” but that they repent and confess their sins and glorify Christ and sanctify Him always, and that they read His books at all the times established for them and believe that for this they will inherit the kingdom; that they will inherit it in truth.
ሕፍቲሁ ፡ ብኩሉ ፡ ጊዜያት” ፣ ዘሥሩዕ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወይእመኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙ ንቱ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ይወርሱ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ ዘበአማን” ፣ ይወርስዋ ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወይሁቦሙ” ፣፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መዊአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጸላእቶሙ ፣ ዘው እቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ። ወያእምር” ፡ ከ“ሉ ፡ ካህን ፡፣ ዘይ ሜህሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰብሕዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቀ ድስዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ የሀቦሙ” ፣ ሱታፌ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይንብሩ ፣ ምስሌሁ” ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወካህንሰ ፡ ዘኢይሜህሮሙ ፣፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ያእምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡
40 The Holy Spirit, who is the type of Joshua son of Nun, will give them victory over their enemies. Every priest who instructs the people and encourages them to glorify Christ and to sanctify Him, let him know that He will give them participation in the Holy Spirit and with Him they will sit in the Kingdom of Heaven. As for the priest who does not teach the people the glory of Christ and His sanctification,
ይጠፍእ ፡ እምቅድሜሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፣ ጠፍኡ ፡ ዐሠርቱ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዓይን ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ዐ"ወይወርስ ፡ ኩነኔሁ፤ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ምስለ ፣ ዲያብ ሎስ ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወይርኀቅ ፡ እም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መንማሥት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወናሁ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ እከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁር"' ፡ ጊዜያተ ፡ ዘሥሩዕ' ፡ ለነ ፡ ለስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፣ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ከተ ፡ ጸቢኦተ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ለክርስ ቲያን ። ለቡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ቫጪፈጸምኩ ፣ ለከ' ። ወአእምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወክቡር ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያጤይቅ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እምብሊት ፡ ወዘይደልዎሙ ፡ ለመሃይ ምናን" ፡ እምጸቢአ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ወዘአስተዳለዎ ፡ ሎሙ ፣ እመንግሥት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወአኩቴት ፡ ወፅ በይ ፡ ወክብር ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም' ፡ አሜን ።
let him know that he will perish before them just as the ten spies perished before the people (Nm 14:36-37). He will inherit his eternal damnation together with the devil and his army and will be far from that eternal kingdom. And behold, O beloved, I am about to illustrate to you the times ordained for us for glorification and sanctification in the book of the explanation of the combat of the demons against the Christian. Understand this book that I have completed for you. Know that in this book there is a great and precious word, because it certifies the Christian faith through the Old (Covenant) and (instructs) on what the faithful must do (to defend themselves) from the attacks of the demons and on the kingdom that He has prepared for them together with Christ unto eternity. To Him be glory, thanksgiving, greatness, and honor for ever and ever, amen.
